# On the Enumeration Complexity of Unions of Conjunctive Queries

联合合取查询的枚举复杂性研究

NOFAR CARMELI, Technion - Israel Institute of Technology MARKUS KROLL, TU Wien

诺法·卡梅利（Nofar Carmeli），以色列理工学院；马库斯·克罗尔（Markus Kroll），维也纳工业大学

We study the enumeration complexity of Unions of Conjunctive Queries (UCQs). We aim to identify the UCQs that are tractable in the sense that the answer tuples can be enumerated with a linear preprocessing phase and a constant delay between every successive tuples. It has been established that, in the absence of self-joins and under conventional complexity assumptions, the CQs that admit such an evaluation are precisely the free-connex ones. A union of tractable CQs is always tractable. We generalize the notion of free-connexity from CQs to UCQs, thus showing that some unions containing intractable CQs are, in fact, tractable. Interestingly, some unions consisting of only intractable CQs are tractable too. We show how to use the techniques presented in this article also in settings where the database contains cardinality dependencies (including functional dependencies and key constraints) or when the UCQs contain disequalities. The question of finding a full characterization of the tractability of UCQs remains open. Nevertheless, we prove that, for several classes of queries, free-connexity fully captures the tractable UCQs.

我们研究联合合取查询（UCQs）的枚举复杂性，旨在识别具有线性预处理阶段和恒定元组间隔延迟特性的可处理UCQs。现有研究表明，在无自连接且符合常规复杂度假设条件下，可满足此类评估的合取查询（CQs）恰好是自由连接类型。由可处理CQs组成的联合总是可处理的。我们将自由连接性概念从CQs推广到UCQs，证明某些包含难处理CQs的联合实际上是可处理的。值得注意的是，某些仅由难处理CQs组成的联合也具有可处理性。本文还展示了如何在包含基数依赖（含函数依赖和键约束）的数据库或含不等式的UCQs中应用所述技术。关于UCQs可处理性的完整特征刻画问题尚未解决，但我们证明对于多类查询而言，自由连接性完全适用于可处理UCQs的判定。

CCS Concepts: - Theory of computation $\rightarrow$ Database theory; Complexity classes; Database query languages (principles); Database query processing and optimization (theory);

CCS概念：- 计算理论$\rightarrow$数据库理论；复杂度类别；数据库查询语言（原理）；数据库查询处理与优化（理论）；

Additional Key Words and Phrases: Unions of conjunctive queries, enumeration, complexity, constant delay

附加关键词：联合合取查询、枚举、复杂性、恒定延迟

## 1 INTRODUCTION

## 1 引言

Evaluating a query $Q$ over a database instance $D$ is a fundamental and well-studied problem in database management systems. Most of the complexity results dealing with query evaluation so far aim to solve a decision or counting variant of it. Starting with Durand and Grandjean in 2007 [14], there has been a renewed interest in examining the enumeration problem of all answers to a query, focusing on fine-grained complexity [11, 16, 24, 25]. When evaluating a non-Boolean query over a database, the number of results may be larger than the size of the database itself. Enumeration complexity offers specific measures for the hardness of such problems. In terms of data complexity, the best time guarantee we can hope for is to output all answers with a constant delay between consecutive answers. In the case of query evaluation, this enumeration phase comes after a linear preprocessing phase required to read the database and decide the existence of a first answer. The enumeration class achieving these time bounds is denoted by Delay ${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$ . Hereafter,we refer to queries in ${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$ as tractable,and queries outside of this class as intractable.

在数据库实例$D$上评估查询$Q$是数据库管理系统领域基础且深入研究的课题。现有复杂度研究多集中于决策或计数变体。自2007年Durand和Grandjean开创性工作[14]以来，学界重新关注查询所有答案的枚举问题，聚焦细粒度复杂度[11,16,24,25]。非布尔查询的答案数量可能超过数据库本身规模，枚举复杂度为此类问题提供了精准的难度度量标准。就数据复杂度而言，最优时间复杂度表现为：在完成读取数据库和首答案判定的线性预处理阶段后，能以恒定延迟输出所有结果。实现此时限的枚举类记作Delay${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$。我们将${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$类查询称为可处理查询，该类之外的查询视为难处理查询。

Results from Bagan et al. [4] and Brault-Baron [9] form a dichotomy that fully classifies which self-join-free Conjunctive Queries (CQs) are in the class Delay ${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$ based on the structure of the query: the class of free-connex queries is exactly the class that admits tractable enumeration. In the years following this dichotomy, much work has been conducted to achieve similar results for other classes of queries [26]. Unions of CQs (UCQs) are a natural extension of CQs, as they describe the union of the answers to several CQs. UCQs form an important class of queries, as they capture the positive fragment of relational algebra. Previous work which implies results on the enumeration complexity of UCQs imposes strong restrictions on the underlying database [25, 27]. We aim to understand the enumeration complexity of UCQs without such restrictions and based solely on their structure.

Bagan等人[4]与Brault-Baron[9]的研究成果构成了一个二分法，基于查询结构完整划分了无自连接联合查询(CQs)在Delay${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$类中的归属：自由连接查询类正是允许可枚举性处理的精确分类。该二分法确立后，学界为在其他查询类别[26]实现类似结果开展了大量工作。CQs的联合(UCQs)作为CQs的自然扩展，描述了多个CQs答案的并集。UCQs构成了重要的查询类别，因其捕捉了关系代数的正片段。先前关于UCQs枚举复杂度的研究[25,27]都对底层数据库施加了严格限制。我们旨在无需此类限制、仅基于结构特征来理解UCQs的枚举复杂度。

Using known methods [28], it can be shown that a union of tractable problems is again tractable. However, what happens if some CQs of a union are tractable while others are not? Intuitively, one might be tempted to expect a union of enumeration problems to be harder than a single problem within the union, making such a UCQ intractable as well. As we will show, this is not necessarily the case.

运用已知方法[28]可证明，可解问题的并集仍具可解性。但若并集中部分CQs可解而其他不可解呢？直觉上可能认为枚举问题的并集会比单个问题更复杂，导致该UCQs同样不可解。但我们将证明事实未必如此。

Example 1. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

示例1. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$满足

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,z}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {z,x}\right) \text{and}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,z}\right) .
$$

Even though ${Q}_{1}$ is hard while ${Q}_{2}$ is easy,a closer look shows that ${Q}_{2}$ contains ${Q}_{1}$ . This means that ${Q}_{1}$ is redundant,and the entire union is equivalent to the easy ${Q}_{2}$ .

尽管${Q}_{1}$属困难问题而${Q}_{2}$属简单问题，细究可见${Q}_{2}$已包含${Q}_{1}$。这意味着${Q}_{1}$是冗余的，整个并集等价于简单的${Q}_{2}$。

To avoid cases like these, where the UCQ can be translated to a simpler one, it makes sense to consider non-redundant unions. It was claimed that, in all cases of a non-redundant union containing an intractable CQ, the UCQ is intractable too [8]. The following is a counter-example which refutes this claim.

为避免此类可简化的UCQs情形，考虑非冗余并集是合理的。曾有论断称：任何包含不可解CQ的非冗余并集，其UCQs同样不可解[8]。以下反例将驳斥该论断。

Example 2. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

示例2. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$满足

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right) \text{and}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,w}\right) .
$$

According to the dichotomy of Bagan et al. [4],the enumeration problem for ${Q}_{2}$ is in DelayClin, while ${Q}_{1}$ is intractable. Yet,it turns out that $Q$ is in fact in ${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$ . The reason is that,since ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are evaluated over the same database $I$ ,we can use ${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ to find ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$ . We can compute ${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ efficiently,and try to extend every such solution to solutions of ${Q}_{1}$ with a constant delay: for every new combination(a,c)of an output $\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ ,we find all $d$ values with $\left( {b,d}\right)  \in  {R}_{3}^{I}$ and then output the solution $\left( {a,c,d}\right)  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$ . The new combinations(a,c)can be detected by storing a lookup table containing(a,c)for every answer $\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ generated. This table can be accessed in constant time in the RAM model. Avoiding duplicates in case ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ share answers requires also storing the previously seen answers, and it is discussed in more details in Section 3. Intuitively,the source of intractability for ${Q}_{1}$ is the join of ${R}_{1}$ with ${R}_{2}$ as we need to avoid duplicates that originate in different $z$ values. The union is tractable since ${Q}_{2}$ returns exactly this join.

根据Bagan等人的二分法[4]，${Q}_{2}$的枚举问题属于DelayClin类，而${Q}_{1}$属不可解问题。然而事实上$Q$属于${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$类。原因在于：由于${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$在同一数据库$I$上求值，可利用${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$定位${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$。我们能高效计算${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$，并以恒定延迟尝试将每个解扩展为${Q}_{1}$的解：对于输出$\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$的每个新组合(a,c)，找出所有满足$\left( {b,d}\right)  \in  {R}_{3}^{I}$的$d$值后输出解$\left( {a,c,d}\right)  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$。通过存储含所有生成答案$\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$的(a,c)查找表，可检测新组合。在RAM模型中该表支持常数时间访问。为避免${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$共享答案时的重复输出，还需存储已见答案，第3节将详细讨论。直观而言，${Q}_{1}$的不可解根源在于${R}_{1}$与${R}_{2}$的连接操作——需规避源自不同$z$值的重复结果。而该并集的可解性源于${Q}_{2}$恰好返回了这个连接结果。

As the example illustrates, to compute the answers to a UCQ in an efficient way, it is not enough to view it as a union of isolated instances of CQ enumeration. In fact, this task requires an understanding of the interaction between several queries. Example 2 shows that the presence of an easy query within the union may give us enough time to compute auxiliary data structures, which we can then add to the hard queries in order to enumerate their answers as well. In Example 2, we can assume we have a ternary relation holding the result of ${Q}_{2}$ . Then,adding the auxiliary atom ${R}_{{Q}_{2}}\left( {x,z,y}\right)$ to ${Q}_{1}$ results in a tractable structure. We generalize this observation and introduce the concept of union-extended queries. We then use union extensions as a central tool for evaluating the enumeration complexity of UCQs, as the structure of such queries has implications on the tractability of the UCQ.

如示例所示，要高效计算UCQ的答案，仅将其视为孤立CQ枚举实例的联合是不够的。实际上，这需要理解多个查询间的交互作用。示例2表明，联合中存在简单查询可能为我们赢得足够时间计算辅助数据结构，进而将这些结构添加到复杂查询中以枚举其答案。在示例2中，我们可以假设拥有存储${Q}_{2}$结果的三元关系，此时向${Q}_{1}$添加辅助原子${R}_{{Q}_{2}}\left( {x,z,y}\right)$将生成可处理结构。我们推广这一观察并引入联合扩展查询概念，随后将联合扩展作为评估UCQ枚举复杂度的核心工具——此类查询的结构直接影响UCQ的可处理性。

Interestingly, this approach can be taken a step further: We show that the concept of extending the union by auxiliary atoms can even be used to efficiently enumerate the answers of UCQs that only contain hard queries. By lifting the concept of free-connex queries from CQs to UCQs via union-extensions, we show that free-connex UCQs are always tractable. This gives us a sufficient global condition for membership in ${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$ beyond any classification of individual CQs.

值得注意的是，该方法可进一步延伸：我们证明通过辅助原子扩展联合的概念，甚至能高效枚举仅含复杂查询的UCQ答案。通过将自由连接查询概念从CQ经联合扩展提升至UCQ，我们证实自由连接UCQ始终可处理。这为${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$成员资格提供了超越单个CQ分类的充分全局条件。

Finding a full characterization of the tractability of UCQs with respect to DelayClin remains an open problem. Nevertheless, we prove that for several classes of queries, free-connexity fully captures the tractable UCQs. A non-free-connex union of two CQs is intractable in the following cases: both CQs are intractable, or they both represent the same CQ up to a different projection. The hardness results presented here use problems with well-established assumptions on the lower bounds, such as Boolean matrix-multiplication [21] or finding a clique or a hyperclique in a graph [22].

关于UCQ在DelayClin下的可处理性完整表征仍是未解难题。然而我们证明，对于多类查询而言，自由连接性完全刻画了可处理UCQ。在以下情况中，两个CQ的非自由连接联合不可处理：二者均不可处理，或它们本质表示同一CQ的不同投影。本文提出的硬度结果基于布尔矩阵乘法[21]或图中寻找团/超团[22]等具有明确下界假设的问题。

Why is establishing lower bounds on UCQ evaluation, even when it contains only two CQs, a fundamentally more challenging problem than the one for CQs? In the case of CQs, hardness results are often shown by reducing a computationally hard problem to the task of answering a query. The reduction encodes the hard problem to the relations of a self-join-free CQ, such that the answers of the CQ correspond to an answer of this problem [4, 7, 9, 11]. However, using such an encoding for CQs within a union does not always work. Similarly to the case of CQs with self-joins, relational symbols that appear multiple times within a query can interfere with the reduction. Indeed, when encoding a hard problem to an intractable CQ within a union, a different CQ in the union evaluates over the same relations, and may also produce answers. A large number of such supplementary answers, with constant delay per answer, accumulates to a long delay until we obtain the answers that correspond to the computationally hard problem. If this delay is larger than the lower bound we assume for the hard problem, we cannot conclude that the UCQ is intractable.

为何建立UCQ评估下界（即使仅含两个CQ）比CQ更具挑战性？对CQ而言，硬度结果通常通过将计算难题归约为查询回答任务来证明。该归约将难题编码至无自连接CQ的关系中，使CQ答案对应难题解[4,7,9,11]。但将此编码用于联合中的CQ时往往失效——类似含自连接的CQ，查询中多次出现的关系符号会干扰归约。事实上，当将难题编码至联合中不可处理CQ时，联合内其他CQ可能基于相同关系生成答案。这些额外答案以恒定延迟累积，将大幅延迟对应计算难题的答案输出。若该延迟超过难题假设下界，则无法判定UCQ不可处理。

The lower bounds presented in this article are obtained either by identifying classes of UCQs for which we can use similar reductions to the ones used for CQs, or by introducing alternative reductions. As some cases remain unclassified, we devote a section to inspecting such UCQs, and describing the challenges that will need to be resolved in order to achieve a full classification.

本文下界结果通过识别可使用类似CQ归约的UCQ类别，或引入替代归约获得。针对部分未分类案例，我们专设章节分析此类UCQ，并阐述实现完整分类需解决的挑战。

Most of the results in this work refer to the classification of the hardness of UCQs over general database instances with respect to the time it takes to answer them with no space restrictions. Nonetheless, we also discuss the implications of these results when the settings are slightly modified. The first modification we consider is when the database instances are not general, as they are restricted by the schema. Cardinality dependencies are commonly given as part of the database schema to capture the fact that, since we know what our database represents, we know of restrictions on the combinations of values that the relations may hold. Functional Dependencies and key constraints are a special case of cardinality dependencies. In the presence of cardinality dependencies, additional CQs are tractable [11]. We show that by combining the approach introduced in this article with the one for identifying tractable CQs in the presence of cardinality dependencies, we can also find additional tractable UCQs over such schemas.

本研究多数结果涉及无空间限制时，UCQ在通用数据库实例上回答时间硬度的分类。但我们也探讨了设置微调时的影响：首先考虑数据库实例受模式约束的非通用情况。基数依赖常作为模式组成部分，用于体现关系可能取值组合的限制（因我们知晓数据库所表示的内容）。函数依赖与键约束是基数依赖的特例。存在基数依赖时，更多CQ变得可处理[11]。我们证明，通过结合本文方法与基数依赖下可处理CQ识别方法，还能在此类模式中发现更多可处理UCQ。

We also inspect UCQs with disequalities. In the case of CQs with disequalities, the disequalities have no effect on the enumeration complexity [4]. That is, one can simply ignore the disequalities, and the remaining CQ is free-connex if and only if the query is tractable (under the same assumptions as in Theorem 3). This irrelevance of disequalities to the enumeration complexity holds also in the case that there are cardinality dependencies in the schema [11]. A natural question is: Does this happen also with UCQs? We show that the answer is no: a tractable UCQ may become intractable when adding disequalities. We then show how to identify easy UCQs with disequalities using the same techniques we introduce for UCQs without them.

我们还研究了带有不等式的联合合取查询(UCQ)。对于含不等式的合取查询(CQ)，这些不等式对枚举复杂度没有影响[4]。也就是说，可以直接忽略不等式部分，剩余CQ是自由连接的当且仅当该查询是可处理的（在定理3的相同假设下）。这种不等式与枚举复杂度的无关性在模式中存在基数依赖时也成立[11]。一个自然的问题是：这种情况是否也适用于UCQ？我们证明答案是否定的：一个可处理的UCQ在添加不等式后可能变得不可处理。随后我们展示了如何运用针对无不等式UCQ提出的技术，来识别含不等式的简单UCQ。

The final modification we consider is that of restricted space. We introduce the time complexity measure of linear partial time, which is more relaxed compared to linear preprocessing and constant delay, and discuss the connection between the two measures. We argue that from a theoretical point of view, when given access to enough space, the two time complexity measures are equivalent, and that from a practical point of view, an algorithm with only the relaxed requirements is sometimes preferable. We discuss the space consumption used in our approach, and demonstrate that in some cases it can be reduced to constant additional space. The question of when exactly this can be done remains open, but we claim in favor of using the measure of linear partial time as an intermediate step for future research.

我们考虑的最终变体是空间受限场景。本文引入了线性部分时间复杂度度量，该度量比线性预处理和恒定延迟更宽松，并探讨了两类度量间的关联。从理论角度论证，当获得足够空间时，这两种时间复杂度度量是等价的；从实践角度说明，有时仅满足宽松要求的算法更具优势。我们分析了所提方法的空间消耗，并证实在某些情况下可降至恒定附加空间。关于精确实现条件的课题仍待探索，但我们主张将线性部分时间度量作为未来研究的中间步骤。

This article is organized as follows: In Section 2, we provide definitions and state results that we use. Section 3 formalizes how CQs within a union can make each other easier, defines free-connex UCQs,and proves that free-connex UCQs are in DelayClin. In Section 4,we prove conditional lower bounds and conclude a dichotomy for some classes of UCQs. Section 5 discusses the future steps required for a full classification, and demonstrates examples of queries of unknown complexity. Section 6 inspects the implications of this work with modified settings: in the presence of cardinality dependencies, with disequalities and with restricted space. Concluding remarks are given in Section 7.

本文结构如下：第2节提供基础定义和引用的结论；第3节形式化描述联合查询中CQ间如何相互简化，定义自由连接UCQ，并证明自由连接UCQ属于DelayClin类；第4节证明条件性下界，推导出部分UCQ类的二分性；第5节讨论完整分类所需的后续工作，展示复杂度未知的查询实例；第6节探究本研究在基数依赖、不等式和空间受限等变体场景下的应用；第7节给出结论性评述。

A short version of this article appeared in the Proceedings of the 38th ACM SIGMOD-SIGACT-SIGAI Symposium on Principles of Database Systems (PODS19) [12]. This article has the following additions. First, it closes some gaps by presenting full proofs to the results stated in the conference version. Second, it includes Section 6, which discusses the implications of this work in additional settings.

本文简短版曾发表于第38届ACM SIGMOD-SIGACT-SIGAI数据库系统原理研讨会(PODS19)[12]。本版本新增内容包含：第一，通过补充会议版中结论的完整证明填补若干空白；第二，新增第6节探讨本研究在扩展场景下的应用价值。

## 2 PRELIMINARIES

## 2 预备知识

In this section, we provide preliminary definitions as well as state results that we will use throughout this article.

本节将给出全文使用的基础定义和引理结论。

Unions of Conjunctive Queries. A schema $\mathcal{S}$ is a set of relational symbols $\left\{  {{R}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{n}}\right\}$ . We denote the arity of a relational symbol ${R}_{i}$ as arity $\left( {R}_{i}\right)$ . Let $\operatorname{dom}$ be a finite set of constants. A database $I$ over schema $\mathcal{S}$ is called an instance of $\mathcal{S}$ ,and it consists of a finite relation ${R}_{i}^{I} \subseteq  {\operatorname{dom}}^{\operatorname{arity}\left( {R}_{i}\right) }$ for every relational symbol ${R}_{i} \in  \mathcal{R}$ .

合取查询的联合。模式$\mathcal{S}$是关系符号集$\left\{  {{R}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{n}}\right\}$。用arity$\left( {R}_{i}\right)$表示关系符号${R}_{i}$的元数。设$\operatorname{dom}$为有限常量集，模式$\mathcal{S}$上的数据库<b5></b0>称为$\mathcal{S}$的实例，由每个关系符号${R}_{i} \in  \mathcal{R}$对应的有限关系${R}_{i}^{I} \subseteq  {\operatorname{dom}}^{\operatorname{arity}\left( {R}_{i}\right) }$组成。

Let var be a set of variables disjoint from dom. A Conjunctive Query (CQ) over schema $\mathcal{S}$ is an expression of the form $Q\left( \overrightarrow{p}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{m}\right)$ ,where ${R}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{m}$ are relational symbols of $\mathcal{S}$ , the tuples $\overrightarrow{p},{\overrightarrow{v}}_{1},\ldots ,{\overrightarrow{v}}_{m}$ hold variables,and every variable in $\overrightarrow{p}$ appears in at least one of ${\overrightarrow{v}}_{1},\ldots ,{\overrightarrow{v}}_{m}$ . We often denote this query as $Q$ . Define the variables of $Q$ as $\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{m}{\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}$ ,and define the free variables of $Q$ as $\operatorname{free}\left( Q\right)  = \overrightarrow{p}$ . The variables of $Q$ that are not free are called existential. We call $Q\left( \overrightarrow{p}\right)$ the head of $Q$ ,and the atomic formulas ${R}_{i}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}\right)$ are called atoms. We further use atoms(Q)to denote the set of atoms of $Q$ . We denote by $\operatorname{full}\left( Q\right)$ the full version of a ${CQQ}$ obtained by adding all variables to the head of $Q$ . A CQ is said to be self-join-free if no relational symbol appears in more than one atom.

设var为与dom互不相交的一组变量。模式$\mathcal{S}$上的合取查询(CQ)是形如$Q\left( \overrightarrow{p}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{m}\right)$的表达式，其中${R}_{1},\ldots ,{R}_{m}$是$\mathcal{S}$的关系符号，元组$\overrightarrow{p},{\overrightarrow{v}}_{1},\ldots ,{\overrightarrow{v}}_{m}$包含变量，且$\overrightarrow{p}$中的每个变量至少出现在${\overrightarrow{v}}_{1},\ldots ,{\overrightarrow{v}}_{m}$之一。我们常将该查询记作$Q$。定义$Q$的变量为$\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{m}{\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}$，自由变量为$\operatorname{free}\left( Q\right)  = \overrightarrow{p}$。$Q$中非自由的变量称为存在变量。$Q\left( \overrightarrow{p}\right)$称为$Q$的头部，原子公式${R}_{i}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}\right)$称为原子。我们用atoms(Q)表示$Q$的原子集合。$\operatorname{full}\left( Q\right)$表示通过将所有变量加入$Q$头部得到的${CQQ}$完整版本。若关系符号不重复出现在多个原子中，则称该CQ为无自连接的。

<!-- Media -->

<!-- figureText: $x$ $y$ $x,y$ $y,z$ $w,y,z$ $v,w$ $\mathcal{H}$ : $z$ W -->

Fig. 1. $T$ is an ext- $\{ x,y,z\}$ -connex tree for hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$ .

图1.$T$是超图$\mathcal{H}$的ext-$\{ x,y,z\}$-连通树。

<!-- Media -->

The evaluation $Q\left( I\right)$ of a $\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$ over a database $I$ is the set of all mappings ${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( Q\right) }$ such that $\mu$ is a homomorphism from ${R}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{m}\right)$ into $I$ ,and ${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( Q\right) }$ is the restriction (or projection) of $\mu$ to the variables free(Q). We refer to such mappings as answers to $Q$ over $I$ . To ease notation, we sometimes specify an answer as a tuple $\overrightarrow{u}$ ,and mean that the answer is the mapping that maps free(Q)to $\overrightarrow{v}$ . A CQ ${Q}_{1}$ is contained in a CQ ${Q}_{2}$ ,denoted ${Q}_{1} \subseteq  {Q}_{2}$ ,if for every instance $I$ , ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \subseteq  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ . A homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ is a mapping $h : \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ such that: (1) for every atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ of ${Q}_{2},R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)$ is an atom in ${Q}_{1};\left( 2\right) h\left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) }\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ .

在数据库$I$上评估$\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$的结果$Q\left( I\right)$是所有映射${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( Q\right) }$的集合，其中$\mu$是从${R}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{m}\right)$到$I$的同态，且${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( Q\right) }$是$\mu$对自由变量free(Q)的限制（或投影）。我们称此类映射为$I$上$Q$的答案。为简化表示，有时将答案指定为元组$\overrightarrow{u}$，意为该映射将free(Q)映射到$\overrightarrow{v}$。若对每个实例$I$都有${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \subseteq  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$，则称CQ${Q}_{1}$包含于CQ${Q}_{2}$，记作${Q}_{1} \subseteq  {Q}_{2}$。从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的同态是满足以下条件的映射$h : \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$：(1)对${Q}_{2},R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)$的每个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$，在${Q}_{1};\left( 2\right) h\left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) }\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$中都是原子。

A Union of Conjunctive Queries $\left( {UCQ}\right) Q$ is a finite set of $\mathrm{{CQs}}$ ,denoted $Q = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{\ell }{Q}_{i}$ ,where free $\left( {Q}_{{i}_{1}}\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{{i}_{2}}\right)$ for all $1 \leq  {i}_{1},{i}_{2} \leq  \ell$ . Semantically, $Q\left( I\right)  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{\ell }{Q}_{i}\left( I\right)$ . We say that $Q$ is non-redundant if there are no two different $\mathrm{{CQs}}{Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ in $Q$ such that there is a homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . We assume that UCQs are non-redundant; otherwise,an equivalent non-redundant UCQ can be obtained by removing the redundant CQs. Given a UCQ $Q$ and a database instance $I$ , we denote by $\operatorname{DECIDE}\langle Q\rangle$ the problem of deciding whether $Q\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ .

合取查询的并集(Union of Conjunctive Queries) $\left( {UCQ}\right) Q$ 是一个有限集合$\mathrm{{CQs}}$，记作$Q = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{\ell }{Q}_{i}$，其中所有$1 \leq  {i}_{1},{i}_{2} \leq  \ell$的自由变量$\left( {Q}_{{i}_{1}}\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{{i}_{2}}\right)$。从语义上讲，$Q\left( I\right)  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{\ell }{Q}_{i}\left( I\right)$。若不存在两个不同的$\mathrm{{CQs}}{Q}_{1}$和${Q}_{2}$使得存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的同态映射，则称$Q$是非冗余的。我们假定UCQ都是非冗余的；否则可通过移除冗余CQ得到等价非冗余UCQ。给定UCQ$Q$和数据库实例$I$，用$\operatorname{DECIDE}\langle Q\rangle$表示判定$Q\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$的问题。

Hypergraphs. A hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {V,E}\right)$ is a set $V$ of vertices and a set $E$ of non-empty subsets of $V$ called hyperedges (sometimes edges). A join tree of a hypergraph $\mathcal{H} = \left( {V,E}\right)$ is a tree $T$ where the nodes are the hyperedges of $\mathcal{H}$ ,and the running intersection property holds,namely: for all $u \in  V$ ,the set $\{ e \in  E \mid  u \in  e\}$ forms a connected subtree in $T$ . A hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$ is acyclic if there exists a join tree for $\mathcal{H}$ .

超图(Hypergraphs)。超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {V,E}\right)$由顶点集$V$和非空边集$E$（称为超边）构成。超图$\mathcal{H} = \left( {V,E}\right)$的连接树(join tree)是棵树$T$，其节点为$\mathcal{H}$的超边，且满足运行交集性质：对所有$u \in  V$，集合$\{ e \in  E \mid  u \in  e\}$在$T$中形成连通子树。若存在连接树，则称超图$\mathcal{H}$是无环的。

Two vertices in a hypergraph are neighbors if they appear in a common edge. A cycle in a hyper-graph is a sequence of vertices that starts and ends in the same vertex and every two consecutive vertices are neighbors. A hypergraph is chordal if every cycle with at least four distinct vertices has an edge connecting two nonconsecutive vertices of the cycle. Every acyclic graph is chordal [5].

超图中两个顶点若出现在同一条边中则互为邻居。超图中的环是指首尾顶点相同且每两个连续顶点都是邻居的顶点序列。若每个含至少四个不同顶点的环都有连接非连续顶点的边，则称该超图是弦的。所有无环图都是弦图[5]。

A clique of a hypergraph is a set of vertices,which are pairwise neighbors in $\mathcal{H}$ . If every edge in $\mathcal{H}$ has exactly $k$ vertices,we call $\mathcal{H}k$ -uniform. An l-hyperclique in a $k$ -uniform hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$ is a set ${V}^{\prime }$ of $l > k$ vertices,such that every subset of ${V}^{\prime }$ of size $k$ forms a hyperedge.

超图的团(clique)是两两互为邻居的顶点集。若$\mathcal{H}$中每条边恰好含$k$个顶点，则称$\mathcal{H}k$为均匀的。在$k$-均匀超图$\mathcal{H}$中，l-超团是由$l > k$个顶点构成的集合${V}^{\prime }$，其每个大小为$k$的子集都形成超边。

A hypergraph ${\mathcal{H}}^{\prime }$ is an inclusive extension of $\mathcal{H}$ if every edge of $\mathcal{H}$ appears in ${\mathcal{H}}^{\prime }$ ,and every edge of ${\mathcal{H}}^{\prime }$ is a subset of some edge in $\mathcal{H}$ . A tree $T$ is an ext-S-connex tree for a hypergraph $\mathcal{H}$ if: (1) $T$ is a join-tree of an inclusive extension of $\mathcal{H}$ ,and (2) there is a subtree ${T}^{\prime }$ of $T$ that contains exactly the variables $S\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack$ (see Figure 1).

若超图$\mathcal{H}$的每条边都出现在${\mathcal{H}}^{\prime }$中，且${\mathcal{H}}^{\prime }$的每条边都是$\mathcal{H}$某条边的子集，则称${\mathcal{H}}^{\prime }$是$\mathcal{H}$的包容扩展。树$T$称为超图$\mathcal{H}$的ext-S-connex树，当且仅当：(1)$T$是$\mathcal{H}$某包容扩展的连接树；(2)存在$T$的子树${T}^{\prime }$恰好包含变量$S\left\lbrack  4\right\rbrack$（见图1）。

Classes of ${CQ}$ s. We associate a hypergraph $\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)  = \left( {V,E}\right)$ to a $\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$ where the vertices are the variables of $Q$ ,and every hyperedge is a set of variables occurring in a single atom of $Q$ . That is, $E = \left\{  {\left\{  {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}}\right\}   \mid  {R}_{i}\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }\right\}$ . With a slight abuse of notation,we identify atoms of $Q$ with edges of $\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$ . A CQ $Q$ is said to be acyclic if $\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$ is acyclic.

${CQ}$的类。我们将一个超图$\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)  = \left( {V,E}\right)$关联到$\mathrm{{CQ}}Q$，其中顶点是$Q$的变量，每条超边是出现在$Q$单个原子中的一组变量。即$E = \left\{  {\left\{  {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}}\right\}   \mid  {R}_{i}\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right) }\right\}$。略微滥用符号的情况下，我们将$Q$的原子与$\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$的边等同起来。若$\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$是无环的，则称CQ $Q$为无环的。

A CQ $Q$ is $S$ -connex if $\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$ has an ext- $S$ -connex tree,and it is free-connex if it has an ext-free(Q)- connex tree [4]. Equivalently, $Q$ is free-connex if both $Q$ and $\left( {V,E\cup \{ \operatorname{free}\left( Q\right) \} }\right)$ are acyclic [9]. A free-path in a CQ $Q$ is a sequence of variables $\left( {x,{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k},y}\right)$ with $k \geq  1$ ,such that: $\left( 1\right) \{ x,y\}  \subseteq$ free $\left( Q\right) ,\left( 2\right) \left\{  {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k}}\right\}   \subseteq  V \smallsetminus  \operatorname{free}\left( Q\right)$ ,and (3) It is a chordless path in $\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$ : that is,every two succeeding variables are neighbors in $\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$ ,but no two non-succeeding variables are neighbors. An acyclic CQ has a free-path iff it is not free-connex [4].

若$\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$具有ext-$S$-连接树，则CQ $Q$是$S$-连通的；若其具有ext-free(Q)-连接树[4]，则称其为自由连通的。等价地，若$Q$和$\left( {V,E\cup \{ \operatorname{free}\left( Q\right) \} }\right)$均为无环的[9]，则$Q$是自由连通的。CQ $Q$中的自由路径是变量序列$\left( {x,{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k},y}\right)$满足$k \geq  1$，且：(1) $\left( 1\right) \{ x,y\}  \subseteq$自由$\left( Q\right) ,\left( 2\right) \left\{  {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k}}\right\}   \subseteq  V \smallsetminus  \operatorname{free}\left( Q\right)$；(3) 该路径在$\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$中是无弦的：即每对相邻变量在$\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$中是邻居，但非相邻变量间不存在邻居关系。无环CQ存在自由路径当且仅当它不是自由连通的[4]。

Computational Model. In this article, we adopt the Random Access Machine (RAM) model with uniform-cost measure and word length $\Theta \left( {\log \left( n\right) }\right)$ on input of size $n$ . Operations such as addition of the values of two registers or concatenation can be performed in constant time. In contrast to the Turing model of computation, the RAM model with uniform-cost measure can retrieve the content of any register via its unique address in constant time.

计算模型。本文采用随机存取存储器(RAM)模型，其具有均匀成本度量及字长$\Theta \left( {\log \left( n\right) }\right)$（输入规模为$n$）。诸如寄存器值相加或连接等操作可在常数时间内完成。与图灵计算模型不同，采用均匀成本度量的RAM模型可通过唯一地址在常数时间内访问任意寄存器的内容。

The input to our problems is a database instance $I$ . We denote by $\parallel o\parallel$ the size of an object $o$ (i.e.,the number of integers required to store it),whereas $\left| o\right|$ is its cardinality. Let $I$ be a database over a schema $\mathcal{S} = \left( {\mathcal{R},\Delta }\right)$ . We assume the input database is given by the reasonable encoding suggested by Flum et al. [17]. Thus,the input is of size $\left| \right| I\left| \right|  = 1 + \left| {dom}\right|  + \left| \mathcal{R}\right|  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R \in  \mathcal{R}}}\operatorname{arity}\left( R\right) \left| {R}^{I}\right|$ over integers bounded by $\max \left\{  {\left| {dom}\right| ,\mathop{\max }\limits_{{R \in  \mathcal{R}}}\left| {R}^{I}\right| }\right\}$ .

问题输入为数据库实例$I$。用$\parallel o\parallel$表示对象$o$的大小（即存储所需整数个数），而$\left| o\right|$表示其基数。设$I$为模式$\mathcal{S} = \left( {\mathcal{R},\Delta }\right)$上的数据库。我们采用Flum等人[17]提出的合理编码假设，因此输入规模为$\left| \right| I\left| \right|  = 1 + \left| {dom}\right|  + \left| \mathcal{R}\right|  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{R \in  \mathcal{R}}}\operatorname{arity}\left( R\right) \left| {R}^{I}\right|$，整数边界为$\max \left\{  {\left| {dom}\right| ,\mathop{\max }\limits_{{R \in  \mathcal{R}}}\left| {R}^{I}\right| }\right\}$。

As we assume that the word length is $\Theta \left( {\log \left( {\left| \right| I\left| \right| }\right) }\right)$ ,the values stored in registers are at most $\left| \right| I{\left| \right| }^{c}$ for some fixed integer $c$ . As a consequence,and since we assume that the values may correspond to addresses,the amount of available memory is polynomial in $\parallel I\parallel$ . Note that,even in more restrictive variants of the RAM model, the size of the registers and the available memory depend on the size of the input [18]. In particular, since we assume that the input fits in memory and each address fits in a register,the registers must contain at least $\log \left( n\right)$ bits for an input that requires $n$ registers.

假设字长为$\Theta \left( {\log \left( {\left| \right| I\left| \right| }\right) }\right)$，则寄存器中存储的值最多为某个固定整数$c$的$\left| \right| I{\left| \right| }^{c}$。因此，考虑到这些值可能对应内存地址，可用内存量是$\parallel I\parallel$的多项式。请注意，即使在更严格的RAM模型变体中，寄存器大小和可用内存量仍取决于输入规模[18]。特别地，由于我们假设输入数据可完全载入内存且每个地址都能存入寄存器，对于需要$n$个寄存器的输入，寄存器必须至少包含$\log \left( n\right)$位。

The RAM model enables the construction of large lookup tables that can be queried within constant time. If the available memory is as large as the number of different possible keys, the lookup table can be as simple as initializing the memory to False, and setting the addresses matching the keys to be True. Note that the time for initialization is not an issue due to constant time initialization techniques [23]. Solutions such as binary search trees and hash tables can reduce the memory consumption significantly. For example, a lookup table based on a binary search tree only requires linear memory in its content at the cost of a logarithmic factor to the time. In this work, we wish to identify what can and cannot be done within certain time bounds without restrictions on the memory consumption. Thus, we assume in our analysis that the available memory is very large and that we can access lookup tables of polynomial size in constant time.

RAM模型支持构建可在恒定时间内查询的大型查找表。若可用内存量与不同键值数量相当，查找表只需将内存初始化为False，并将匹配键值的地址设为True即可。注意，借助恒定时间初始化技术[23]，初始化耗时不成问题。二叉搜索树和哈希表等方案能显著降低内存消耗。例如，基于二叉搜索树的查找表仅需线性内存，但会引入对数级时间开销。本研究旨在确定在特定时间界限内，不受内存限制时可实现与不可实现的任务。因此，我们在分析中假设可用内存极大，且能在恒定时间内访问多项式规模的查找表。

Enumeration Complexity. Given a finite alphabet $\sum$ and binary relation $R \subseteq  {\sum }^{ * } \times  {\sum }^{ * }$ ,the enumeration problem $\operatorname{Enum}\langle R\rangle$ is: given an instance $x \in  {\sum }^{ * }$ ,output all $y \in  {\sum }^{ * }$ such that $\left( {x,y}\right)  \in  R$ . Such $y$ values are often called solutions or answers to $\operatorname{ENUM}\langle R\rangle$ . An enumeration algorithm $\mathcal{A}$ for $\operatorname{ENUM}\langle R\rangle$ is a RAM that solves Enum $\langle R\rangle$ without repetitions. We say that $\mathcal{A}$ enumerates Enum $\langle R\rangle$ with delay $d\left( \left| x\right| \right)$ if the time before the first output,the time between any two consecutive outputs,and the time between the last output and termination are each bounded by $d\left( \left| x\right| \right)$ . Sometimes we wish to relax the requirements of the delay before the first answer,and specify a preprocessing time $p\left( \left| x\right| \right)$ . In this case,the time before the first output is only required to be bounded by $p\left( \left| x\right| \right)$ . The enumeration class ${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$ is defined as the class of all enumeration problems $\operatorname{EnuM}\langle R\rangle$ which have an enumeration algorithm $\mathcal{A}$ with preprocessing $p\left( \left| x\right| \right)  \in  O\left( \left| x\right| \right)$ and delay $d\left( \left| x\right| \right)  \in  O\left( 1\right)$ . Note that we do not impose a restriction on the memory used. In particular, such an algorithm may use additional constant memory for writing between two consecutive answers.

枚举复杂性。给定有限字母表$\sum$和二元关系$R \subseteq  {\sum }^{ * } \times  {\sum }^{ * }$，枚举问题$\operatorname{Enum}\langle R\rangle$定义为：对于实例$x \in  {\sum }^{ * }$，输出所有满足$\left( {x,y}\right)  \in  R$的$y \in  {\sum }^{ * }$。此类$y$值通常称为$\operatorname{ENUM}\langle R\rangle$的解或答案。枚举算法$\mathcal{A}$指能无重复解决Enum$\langle R\rangle$的RAM。若首次输出前耗时、任意两连续输出间耗时、末次输出与终止间耗时均不超过$d\left( \left| x\right| \right)$，则称$\mathcal{A}$以延迟$d\left( \left| x\right| \right)$枚举Enum$\langle R\rangle$。有时需放宽首次答案前的延迟要求，指定预处理时间$p\left( \left| x\right| \right)$，此时仅要求首次输出前耗时不超过$p\left( \left| x\right| \right)$。枚举类${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$定义为所有具有预处理$p\left( \left| x\right| \right)  \in  O\left( \left| x\right| \right)$和延迟$d\left( \left| x\right| \right)  \in  O\left( 1\right)$的枚举算法$\mathcal{A}$所能解决的枚举问题$\operatorname{EnuM}\langle R\rangle$集合。需注意，我们未对内存使用设限，特别地，此类算法可在两连续答案间使用额外恒定内存进行写入操作。

Let $\operatorname{Enum}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle$ and $\operatorname{Enum}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle$ be enumeration problems. There is an exact reduction from $\operatorname{ENUM}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle$ to $\operatorname{ENUM}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle$ ,denoted as $\operatorname{ENUM}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle  { \leq  }_{e}\operatorname{ENUM}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle$ ,if there exist mappings $\sigma$ and $\tau$ such that: (1) for every $x \in  {\sum }^{ * },\sigma \left( x\right)$ is computable in $O\left( \left| x\right| \right)$ time; (2) for every $y$ such that $\left( {\sigma \left( x\right) ,y}\right)  \in$ ${R}_{2},\tau \left( y\right)$ is computable in $O\left( 1\right)$ time; and (3) in multiset notation, $\left\{  {\tau \left( y\right)  \mid  \left( {\sigma \left( x\right) ,y}\right)  \in  {R}_{2}}\right\}   = \left\{  {y}^{\prime }\right.$ $\left. {\left( {x,{y}^{\prime }}\right)  \in  {R}_{1}}\right\}$ . Intuitively, $\sigma$ maps instances of $\operatorname{Enum}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle$ to instances of $\operatorname{Enum}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle$ ,and $\tau$ maps solutions of $\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle$ to solutions of $\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle$ . If $\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle  { \leq  }_{e}\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle$ and $\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle   \in  \operatorname{Delay}{\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$ , then $\operatorname{Enum}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle   \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ as well [4].

设$\operatorname{Enum}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle$和$\operatorname{Enum}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle$为枚举问题。若存在映射$\sigma$和$\tau$满足：(1) 对于每个$x \in  {\sum }^{ * },\sigma \left( x\right)$可在$O\left( \left| x\right| \right)$时间内计算；(2) 对于每个满足$\left( {\sigma \left( x\right) ,y}\right)  \in$的$y$，${R}_{2},\tau \left( y\right)$可在$O\left( 1\right)$时间内计算；(3) 在多重集表示法中$\left\{  {\tau \left( y\right)  \mid  \left( {\sigma \left( x\right) ,y}\right)  \in  {R}_{2}}\right\}   = \left\{  {y}^{\prime }\right.$$\left. {\left( {x,{y}^{\prime }}\right)  \in  {R}_{1}}\right\}$，则称存在从$\operatorname{ENUM}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle$到$\operatorname{ENUM}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle$的精确归约，记作$\operatorname{ENUM}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle  { \leq  }_{e}\operatorname{ENUM}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle$。直观上，$\sigma$将$\operatorname{Enum}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle$的实例映射为$\operatorname{Enum}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle$的实例，$\tau$将$\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle$的解映射为$\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle$的解。若$\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle  { \leq  }_{e}\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle$且$\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {R}_{2}\right\rangle   \in  \operatorname{Delay}{\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$，则$\operatorname{Enum}\left\langle  {R}_{1}\right\rangle   \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$也成立[4]。

Computational Hypotheses. We use the following well-known hypotheses for lower bounds on certain computational problems:

计算假设。我们采用以下知名假设来界定特定计算问题的下界：

MAT-MUL: Two Boolean $n \times  n$ matrices cannot be multiplied in time $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ . This problem is equivalent to the evaluation of the query $\Pi \left( {x,y}\right)  \leftarrow  A\left( {x,z}\right) ,B\left( {z,y}\right)$ over the schema $\{ A,B\}$ where $A,B \subseteq  \{ 1,\ldots ,n{\} }^{2}$ . It is conjectured that this problem cannot be solved in $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ time,and the best algorithms today require $O\left( {n}^{\omega }\right)$ time,where ${2.37} < \omega  < {2.38}$ is the matrix multiplication exponent [2].

矩阵乘法假设(MAT-MUL)：两个布尔型$n \times  n$矩阵无法在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内完成乘法运算。该问题等价于在模式$\{ A,B\}$（其中$A,B \subseteq  \{ 1,\ldots ,n{\} }^{2}$）上评估查询$\Pi \left( {x,y}\right)  \leftarrow  A\left( {x,z}\right) ,B\left( {z,y}\right)$。据推测此问题无法在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内求解，目前最佳算法需要$O\left( {n}^{\omega }\right)$时间，其中${2.37} < \omega  < {2.38}$为矩阵乘法指数[2]。

Remark: MAT-MUL is the assumption originally used by Bagan et al. [4] to show the hardness for CQs. It was later suggested to use a sparse variant of this hypothesis to show the same results [6]. Here, we use the original variant, as the sparse variant is not enough to handle cases like Lemma 27.

注记：MAT-MUL是Bagan等人[4]最初用于证明CQs问题难度的假设。后续研究建议采用该假设的稀疏变体来达成相同结论[6]。本文采用原始变体，因稀疏变体不足以处理引理27等情况。

HYPERCLIQUE: For all $k \geq  3$ ,finding a $k$ -hyperclique in a(k - 1)-uniform hypergraph with $n$ vertices is not possible in time $O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$ . When $k = 3$ ,this is the assumption that we cannot detect a triangle in a graph in $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ time [3]. When $k > 3$ ,this is a special case of the $\left( {\ell ,k}\right)$ - Hyperclique Hypothesis [22],which states that,in a $k$ -uniform hypergraph of $n$ vertices, ${n}^{k - o\left( 1\right) }$ time is required to find a set of $\ell$ vertices such that each of it subsets of size $k$ forms a hyperedge. The HYPERCLIQUE hypothesis is sometimes called Tetra $\langle k\rangle$ [9].

超团假设(HYPERCLIQUE)：对于所有$k \geq  3$，在具有$n$个顶点的(k-1)-均匀超图中，无法在$O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$时间内找到$k$-超团。当$k = 3$时，这相当于我们无法在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内检测图中三角形的假设[3]。当$k > 3$时，这是$\left( {\ell ,k}\right)$-超团假设[22]的特例，该假设指出：在具有$n$个顶点的$k$-均匀超图中，需要${n}^{k - o\left( 1\right) }$时间才能找到$\ell$个顶点的集合，使得其每个大小为$k$的子集都构成超边。该假设有时被称为四面体假设(Tetra $\langle k\rangle$)[9]。

4-CLIQUE: It is not possible to determine the existence of a 4-clique in a graph with $n$ nodes in time $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ . This is a special case of the $k$ -Clique Hypothesis [22],which states that detecting a clique in a graph with $n$ nodes requires ${n}^{\frac{\omega k}{3} - o\left( 1\right) }$ time,where $\omega$ is again the matrix multiplication exponent.

4-团问题(4-CLIQUE)：无法在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$时间内确定具有$n$个节点的图中是否存在4-团。这是$k$-团假设[22]的特例，该假设指出：在具有$n$个节点的图中检测团需要${n}^{\frac{\omega k}{3} - o\left( 1\right) }$时间，其中$\omega$仍是矩阵乘法指数。

Enumerating Answers to UCQs. Given a UCQ $Q$ over some schema $\mathcal{S}$ ,we denote by $\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle$ the enumeration problem $\operatorname{ENUM}\langle R\rangle$ ,where $R$ is the binary relation between instances $I$ over $\mathcal{S}$ and sets of mappings $Q\left( I\right)$ . We consider the size of the query as well as the size of the schema to be fixed. In the case of CQs,Bagan et al. [4] showed that a self-join-free acyclic CQ is in DelayClin iff it is free-connex. In addition, Brault-Baron [9] showed that self-join-free cyclic queries are not in Delay ${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$ . In fact,the existence of a single answer to a cyclic $\mathrm{{CQ}}$ cannot be determined in linear time. We call a CQ difficult if it is self-join-free and not free-connex. Difficult CQs are intractable according to the following dichotomy.

联合查询答案枚举(UCQs)：给定模式$\mathcal{S}$上的联合查询$Q$，我们用$\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle$表示枚举问题$\operatorname{ENUM}\langle R\rangle$，其中$R$是模式$\mathcal{S}$上的实例$I$与映射集$Q\left( I\right)$之间的二元关系。我们认为查询大小和模式大小是固定的。对于连接查询(CQs)，Bagan等人[4]证明：当且仅当无自连接的非循环连接查询是自由连接时，其属于DelayClin类。此外，Brault-Baron[9]证明含自连接的循环查询不属于Delay${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$类。事实上，循环查询$\mathrm{{CQ}}$的单个答案也无法在线性时间内确定。我们将无自连接且非自由连接的连接查询称为困难查询。根据下述二分法定理，困难查询具有计算复杂性。

THEOREM 3 ([4,9]). Let $Q$ be a self-join-free ${CQ}$ .

定理3([4,9])：设$Q$为无自连接的${CQ}$。

(1) If $Q$ is free-connex,then $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

(1) 若$Q$是自由连接的，则$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

(2) If $Q$ is acyclic and not free-connex,then $\operatorname{ENUM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ ,assuming MAT-MUL.

(2) 若$Q$是非循环但非自由连接的，则在MAT-MUL假设下，$\operatorname{ENUM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

(3) If $Q$ is cyclic,then $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$ ,as $\operatorname{DecIDE}\langle Q\rangle$ cannot be solved in linear time, assuming HYPERCLIQUE.

(3) 若$Q$是循环的，则在HYPERCLIQUE假设下，$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$，因为$\operatorname{DecIDE}\langle Q\rangle$无法在线性时间内求解。

The positive case of this dichotomy can be shown using the Constant Delay Yannakakis (CDY) algorithm [19]. It uses an ext-free(Q)-connex tree $T$ for $Q$ . First,it performs the classical Yannakakis preprocessing [29] over $T$ to obtain a relation for each node in $T$ ,where all tuples can be used for some answer in $Q\left( I\right)$ . Then,it considers only the subtree of $T$ containing free(Q),and joins the relations corresponding to this subtree with constant delay.

该二分法的正面情况可通过常数延迟Yannakakis(CDY)算法[19]证明。该算法使用$Q$的ext-free(Q)-连接树$T$：首先对$T$执行经典Yannakakis预处理[29]，为$T$中每个节点获得关系，其中所有元组都可用于$Q\left( I\right)$中的某些答案；然后仅考虑包含free(Q)的$T$子树，并以常数延迟连接该子树对应的关系。

## 3 UPPER BOUNDS VIA UNION EXTENSIONS

## 3 通过并集扩展实现上界

In this section, we identify tractable UCQs. Section 3.1 discusses unions that contain only tractable CQs. Section 3.2 inspects the requirements from UCQs in DelayClin and proves the Cheater’s Lemma: a tool that allows us to compile several enumeration algorithms into one. In Section 3.3, we introduce the concepts of union extensions and variable sets that a CQ can supply in order to help the evaluation of another CQ in the union. We generalize the notion of free-connexity to UCQs and show that such queries are in Delay ${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$ in Section 3.4.

本节我们识别可处理的联合查询(UCQs)。3.1节讨论仅包含可处理连接查询(CQs)的联合查询。3.2节检视DelayClin中对UCQs的要求，并证明"作弊者引理"——这个工具让我们能将多个枚举算法编译为一个。3.3节引入联合扩展和变量集的概念，这些概念描述了一个CQ如何通过提供变量集来协助评估联合查询中的另一个CQ。我们在3.4节将自由连接性概念推广到UCQs，并证明此类查询属于Delay${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$类。

### 3.1 Unions of Tractable CQs

### 3.1 可处理CQs的联合查询

Using known techniques [28, Proposition 2.38] a union of tractable CQs is also tractable.

运用已知技术[28，命题2.38]，可处理CQs的联合查询也是可处理的。

THEOREM 4. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$ be a UCQ for some fixed $n \geq  1$ . If all ${CQ}$ s in $Q$ are free-connex, then $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

定理4. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$为某个固定$n \geq  1$的UCQ。若$Q$中所有${CQ}$都是自由连接的，则$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

Proof. Algorithm 1 evaluates a union of two CQs. In case of a union $Q = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{\ell }{Q}_{i}$ of more CQs, we can use this recursively by treating the second query as ${Q}_{2} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{\ell }$ .

证明. 算法1评估两个CQs的联合查询。对于更多CQs的联合查询$Q = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{i = 1}}^{\ell }{Q}_{i}$，可通过将第二个查询视为${Q}_{2} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{\ell }$来递归应用本算法。

<!-- Media -->

ALGORITHM 1: Answering a Union of Two Tractable CQs

算法1: 求解两个可处理CQs的联合查询

---

while $a \leftarrow  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$ .next(   ) do

当$a \leftarrow  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$.next()时执行

	if $a \notin  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ then

	若$a \notin  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$成立则

		print $a$

		输出$a$

	else

	否则

		print ${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ .next(   )

		输出${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$.next()

while $a \leftarrow  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ .next(   ) do

当$a \leftarrow  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$.next()时执行

	print $a$

	输出$a$

---

<!-- Media -->

By the end of the run,the algorithm prints ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \smallsetminus  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ over all iterations of line 3,and it prints ${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ in lines 5 and 7. Line 5 is called ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \cap  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ times,so the command ${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ .next(   ) always succeeds there. For free-connex CQs after linear preprocessing time, the answers can be enumerated in constant delay, and testing whether a given mapping is an answer can be done in constant time [6]. Thus, this algorithm runs within the required time bounds.

运行结束时，算法在第3行所有迭代中输出${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \smallsetminus  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$，并在第5、7行输出${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$。第5行被调用${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \cap  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$次，因此${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$.next()命令在此总能成功。对于自由连接CQs，经过线性预处理时间后，可在常数延迟下枚举答案，且测试给定映射是否为答案可在常数时间内完成[6]。因此本算法在要求的时间界限内运行。

The technique presented in the proof of Theorem 4 has the advantage that it does not require more than constant memory available for writing in the enumeration phase. Alternatively, this theorem is a consequence of the following lemma, which we prove next and gives us a general approach to compile several enumeration algorithms into one.

定理4证明中提出的技术具有以下优势：在枚举阶段不需要超过常数的内存空间用于写入。另外，该定理也是下述引理的推论，这个引理为我们提供了一种将多个枚举算法编译为一体的通用方法。

### 3.2 The Cheater's Lemma

### 3.2 作弊者引理

In this section, we prove a lemma that is useful to show upper bounds for UCQs even in cases not covered by Theorem 4. To get a clearer notion of what it means for a problem to be in the class Delay ${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$ ,we first define linear partial time.

本节将证明一个引理，该引理有助于为UCQs（联合查询）建立上界，即使在定理4未涵盖的情况下亦然。为更清晰地理解问题属于Delay${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$类的含义，我们首先定义线性部分时间。

Definition 5 (Linear Partial Time). An algorithm runs in linear partial time if,for every input $x$ , the time before the $n$ th output is $O\left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)$ .

定义5（线性部分时间）。若算法对每个输入$x$，其第$n$个输出前的处理时间为$O\left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)$，则称该算法运行于线性部分时间。

We claim next that if we relax the requirement of linear preprocessing and constant delay to allow a constant number of linear delay steps, we get linear partial time.

我们接下来主张：若放宽线性预处理和恒定延迟的要求，允许出现恒定次数的线性延迟步骤，则可得到线性部分时间。

Proposition 6. Let $\mathcal{A}$ be an algorithm. If there exist constants $a,b$ and $c$ such that,on any input $x$ ,the time between successive outputs of $\mathcal{A}$ is bounded by $a\left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)$ at most $b$ times,where $n$ is the number of answers printed up to that point,and bounded by $c$ otherwise,then $\mathcal{A}$ runs in linear partial time.

命题6。设$\mathcal{A}$为算法。若存在常数$a,b$和$c$使得：对任意输入$x$，算法$\mathcal{A}$连续输出间的时间最多有$b$次受$a\left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)$约束（其中$n$为当前已输出答案数），其余情况受$c$约束，则$\mathcal{A}$运行于线性部分时间。

Proof. Before the $n$ th answer,there are at most $b$ steps with delay larger than constant,and in these cases the delay is bounded by $a\left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)$ . Thus,the time in which the $n$ th answer is produced is bounded by ${ba}\left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)  + {cn} \leq  \left( {{ab} + c}\right) \left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)$ for every $n$ .

证明。在第$n$个答案输出前，至多有$b$个步骤的延迟超过常数，这些情况下的延迟受$a\left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)$约束。因此，对任意$n$，生成第$n$个答案的时间受${ba}\left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)  + {cn} \leq  \left( {{ab} + c}\right) \left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)$约束。

If space is not restricted (as in our case), this relaxation in the phrasing of the requirements does not change the requirements themselves, as we show that any algorithm that runs in linear partial time can be modified to achieve linear preprocessing and constant delay. This can be done using the known technique [10, Proposition 12] of delaying the results to regularize the delay. In fact, we can further relax the phrasing by allowing a constant number of duplicates per answer.

当空间不受限时（如本研究所设），这种要求表述的放宽并不会改变实质要求——我们证明任何线性部分时间算法均可被改进为具有线性预处理和恒定延迟。这可通过延迟结果以规整化延迟的已知技术[10，命题12]实现。事实上，我们还可进一步放宽表述，允许每个答案出现恒定次数的重复。

LEMMA 7 (THE CHEATER's LEMMA). Let $P$ be an enumeration problem. The following are equivalent:

引理7（取巧者引理）。设$P$为枚举问题，以下陈述等价：

(1) $P \in  {\operatorname{DelayClin}}_{\text{(in) }}$

(2) There exist an algorithm $\mathcal{A}$ and constants $c$ and $d$ such that: $\mathcal{A}$ outputs the solutions to $P$ , the time before the nth answer is bounded by $c\left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)$ ,and every result is produced at most d times.

(2) 存在算法$\mathcal{A}$及常数$c$、$d$满足：$\mathcal{A}$输出$P$的解，第n个答案前的耗时受$c\left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)$约束，且每个结果至多输出d次。

Proof. As any algorithm that runs in linear preprocessing and constant delay also runs in linear partial time,the direction $1 \Rightarrow  2$ is trivial. We now show the opposite direction.

证明。由于任何具有线性预处理和恒定延迟的算法必然运行于线性部分时间，方向$1 \Rightarrow  2$的证明是平凡的。现证明其逆方向。

We describe an algorithm ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ that simulates $\mathcal{A}$ ,stores all generated results to prevent duplicates and holds back generated results to regularize the delay. ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ maintains a lookup table containing the results that $\mathcal{A}$ generated and a queue containing those that were not yet printed. Both are initialized as empty. ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ calls $\mathcal{A}$ . When $\mathcal{A}$ returns a result, ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ checks the lookup table to determine whether it was found before. If it was not, the result is added to both the lookup table and the queue. Otherwise,it is ignored. Since $\mathcal{A}$ runs in linear partial time,there exists a constant $c$ such that the $n$ th answer to $\mathcal{A}$ is obtained after $c\left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)$ operations. ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ first performs $c\left| x\right|$ computation steps, and then after every ${cd}$ computation steps,it outputs a result from the queue. The queue is never empty when used: ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ returns its $i$ th result after $c\left| x\right|  + \left( {cd}\right) i = c\left( {\left| x\right|  + {di}}\right)$ computation steps; At this time, $\mathcal{A}$ produced at least ${di}$ results,which contain at least $i$ unique results. When it is done simulating ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime },\mathcal{A}$ outputs all remaining results in the queue. By definition, ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ operates with linear preprocessing time and constant delay. It outputs all results of $\mathcal{A}$ with no duplicates since,due to the lookup table, every result enters the queue exactly once.

我们描述了一种算法${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$，该算法通过模拟$\mathcal{A}$、存储所有生成结果以避免重复，并暂缓输出结果以调节延迟。${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$维护一个包含$\mathcal{A}$生成结果的查找表和一个存放未输出结果的队列，两者初始为空。当$\mathcal{A}$返回结果时，${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$检查查找表判定是否重复。若为新结果，则同时加入查找表和队列；反之则忽略。由于$\mathcal{A}$以线性时间运行，存在常数$c$使得第$n$次调用$\mathcal{A}$可在$c\left( {\left| x\right|  + n}\right)$次操作后获得结果。${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$先执行$c\left| x\right|$次计算步骤，之后每进行${cd}$次计算步骤便从队列输出一个结果。队列始终非空：${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$在第$c\left| x\right|  + \left( {cd}\right) i = c\left( {\left| x\right|  + {di}}\right)$次计算步骤后返回其第$i$个结果；此时$\mathcal{A}$已生成至少${di}$个结果，其中包含不少于$i$个唯一结果。模拟完成后${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime },\mathcal{A}$会输出队列中所有剩余结果。根据定义，${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$具有线性预处理时间和恒定延迟特性，通过查找表机制确保$\mathcal{A}$的每个结果仅进入队列一次，从而实现无重复输出。

The Cheater's Lemma formalizes how much we are allowed to "cheat" in order to show that a problem can be solved with linear preprocessing and constant delay by only showing an algorithm with relaxed requirements. To show that a problem is in ${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$ ,it suffices to find an algorithm for this problem where the delay is usually constant, but it may be linear a constant number of times, and the number of times every result is produced is bounded by a constant. The allowed linear delay is not only with respect to the input, but also with respect to the already produced answers.

作弊者引理（Cheater's Lemma）形式化地规定了为证明某问题可通过放宽要求的算法实现线性预处理和恒定延迟，所允许的"作弊"程度。要证明问题属于${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$类，只需找到满足以下条件的算法：延迟通常为常数，但允许出现常数次的线性延迟，且每个结果的生成次数受常数限制。所允许的线性延迟不仅针对输入规模，还涉及已生成答案的数量。

### 3.3 Union Extensions

### 3.3 并集扩展

As Example 2 shows, Theorem 4 does not cover all tractable UCQs. We now define union extensions and address the other cases. We first define body-homomorphisms between CQs to have the standard meaning of homomorphism, but without the restriction on the heads of the queries.

如例2所示，定理4并未覆盖所有易处理的UCQ。现在我们定义并集扩展来处理其他情况。首先定义CQ间的体同态（body-homomorphism），其具有标准同态含义，但不施加查询头部的限制。

Definition 8. Let ${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2}$ be CQs.

定义8. 设${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2}$为CQ。

- A body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ is a mapping $h : \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ such that for every atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ of ${Q}_{2},R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {Q}_{1}$ .

- 从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态是映射$h : \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$，使得对于${Q}_{2},R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {Q}_{1}$的每个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$成立。

- If there exists a body-homomorphism $h$ from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ and vice-versa,we say that ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are body-homomorphically equivalent.

- 若存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态$h$且反之亦然，则称${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$体同态等价。

- A body-isomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ is a bijective mapping $h$ such that $h$ is a body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ and ${h}^{-1}$ is a body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{1}$ to ${Q}_{2}$ .

- 从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同构（body-isomorphism）是双射映射$h$，使得$h$构成${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态，且${h}^{-1}$构成${Q}_{1}$到${Q}_{2}$的体同态。

- If there is a body-isomorphism between ${Q}_{2}$ and ${Q}_{1}$ ,we say that ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are body-isomorphic.

若${Q}_{2}$与${Q}_{1}$之间存在本体同构（body-isomorphism），则称${Q}_{1}$和${Q}_{2}$是本体同构的。

The standard definition of a homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ is then a body-homomorphism $h$ from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ such that $h\left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) }\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ . To demonstrate the definition of a body-homomorphism,consider the UCQ $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ from Example 2 with the CQs ${Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow$ ${R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right)$ and ${Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,w}\right)$ . There is a body-homomorphism $h : {Q}_{2} \rightarrow  {Q}_{1}$ with $h\left( x\right)  = x,h\left( y\right)  = z$ ,and $h\left( w\right)  = y$ . However,this is not a body-isomorphism. Body-isomorphic CQs have the same body up to variable renaming (and the renaming is given by the body-isomorphism). Note that body-isomorphic CQs are necessarily body-homomorphically equivalent. The opposite direction holds for self-join-free CQs; that is, if two CQs are self-join-free and body-homomorphically equivalent, then they are necessarily body-isomorphic. We now formalize the way that one CQ can help with evaluating another CQ in the union by supplying variables.

从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的同态（homomorphism）标准定义是满足$h\left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) }\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$条件的本体同态$h$。为说明本体同态的定义，考虑示例2中由CQ${Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow$、${R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right)$和${Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,w}\right)$组成的UCQ$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$。存在一个满足$h\left( x\right)  = x,h\left( y\right)  = z$和$h\left( w\right)  = y$的本体同态$h : {Q}_{2} \rightarrow  {Q}_{1}$，但这并非本体同构。本体同构的CQ在变量重命名后具有相同的本体结构（且重命名由该本体同构给出）。需注意本体同构的CQ必然具有本体同态等价性。对于无自连接的CQ，逆命题也成立：若两个CQ无自连接且本体同态等价，则它们必然本体同构。下面我们形式化描述一个CQ如何通过提供变量来协助评估并集中的另一个CQ。

Definition 9. We say that a CQ $Q$ supplies a set $V$ of variables if there exists $S$ such that $V \subseteq  S \subseteq$ free(Q)and $Q$ is $S$ -connex. ${}^{1}$

定义9. 当存在满足$V \subseteq  S \subseteq$ free(Q)且$Q$是$S$-连通（-connex）的$S$时，称CQ$Q$提供了变量集$V$。${}^{1}$

When considering again Example 2,the query ${Q}_{2}$ supplies $\{ x,y,w\}  \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ as ${Q}_{2}$ is $\{ x,y,w\}$ - connex. The following lemma shows why body-homomorphisms and supplying variables play an important role in UCQ enumeration. In case of a body-homomorphism from a supplying $\mathrm{{CQ}}{Q}_{2}$ to another CQ ${Q}_{1}$ ,we can produce an auxiliary relation that contains all possible value combinations of the matching variables in ${Q}_{1}$ . This can be done efficiently while producing some answers to ${Q}_{2}{}^{2}$

再次考察示例2，由于查询${Q}_{2}$是$\{ x,y,w\}$-连通的，故其提供了变量集$\{ x,y,w\}  \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$。以下引理揭示了为何本体同态和变量提供在UCQ枚举中起关键作用：当存在从提供变量的$\mathrm{{CQ}}{Q}_{2}$到另一个CQ${Q}_{1}$的本体同态时，可生成包含${Q}_{1}$中匹配变量所有可能值组合的辅助关系。该操作可在为${Q}_{2}{}^{2}$生成部分答案时高效完成。

LEMMA 10. Let ${Q}_{2}$ be a ${CQ}$ that supplies the variables ${\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}$ . Given an instance $I$ ,one can compute with linear-time preprocessing and constant delay a set of mappings $M$ from free $\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ to the domain such that:

引理10. 设${Q}_{2}$为提供变量集${\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}$的${CQ}$。给定实例$I$，通过线性时间预处理和常数延迟可计算出从free$\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$到值域的映射集$M$，其满足：

- $M \subseteq  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$

- $M$ can be translated in time $O\left( \left| M\right| \right)$ to a relation ${R}^{M}$ such that: for every ${CQ}{Q}_{1}$ with a body-homomorphism $h$ from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ and for every answer ${\mu }_{1} \in  \operatorname{full}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) \left( I\right)$ ,there is the tuple ${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{M}$ .

- 可在$O\left( \left| M\right| \right)$时间内将$M$转换为关系${R}^{M}$，使得：对于每个存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的本体同态$h$的${CQ}{Q}_{1}$，以及每个答案${\mu }_{1} \in  \operatorname{full}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) \left( I\right)$，都存在元组${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{M}$。

Proof. According to Definition 9,there exists ${\overrightarrow{v}}_{2} \subseteq  S \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ such that ${Q}_{2}$ is $S$ -connex. Take an ext- $S$ -connex tree $T$ for ${Q}_{2}$ ,and perform the CDY algorithm [19] on ${Q}_{2}$ while treating $S$ as the free variables. This results in a set $N$ of mappings from the variables of $S$ to the domain such that $N = {\left. {Q}_{2}\left( I\right) \right| }_{S}$ . Note that,as we mentioned following Theorem 3,the CDY algorithm has a preprocessing stage that removes dangling tuples and guarantees that, at its end, there is a relation for each vertex of the tree such that each tuple of such a relation can be used for some answer.

证明。根据定义9，存在${\overrightarrow{v}}_{2} \subseteq  S \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$使得${Q}_{2}$是$S$-连通的。选取${Q}_{2}$的一个外部$S$-连通树$T$，并在将$S$视为自由变量的情况下对${Q}_{2}$执行CDY算法[19]。这将生成一组从$S$变量到值域的映射$N$，满足$N = {\left. {Q}_{2}\left( I\right) \right| }_{S}$。需注意的是，如定理3后文所述，CDY算法在预处理阶段会移除悬空元组，并确保最终树中每个顶点对应的关系都包含可参与答案构建的元组。

For every mapping $\mu  \in  N$ ,we extend it once to obtain a mapping from all variables of ${Q}_{2}$ as follows: Go over all vertices of $T$ starting from the connected subtree containing $S$ and treating a neighbor of an already treated vertex at every step. Consider a step where in its beginning $\mu$ is a homomorphism from a set ${S}_{1}$ ,and we are treating an atom $R\left( {\overrightarrow{v},\overrightarrow{u}}\right)$ where $\overrightarrow{v} \subseteq  {S}_{1}$ and $\overrightarrow{u} \cap  {S}_{1} = \varnothing$ . We take some tuple in $R$ of the form $\left( {\mu \left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) ,\overrightarrow{t}}\right)$ and extend $\mu$ to also map $\mu \left( \overrightarrow{u}\right)  = \overrightarrow{t}$ . Such a tuple exists since the dangling tuples were removed. This extension takes constant time, and, in its end, we have that ${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) } \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ . Using these extensions,we set $M = \left\{  {{\left. {\mu }^{ + }\right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) } \mid  {\mu }^{ + }}\right.$ as an extension of $\mu  \in  N\}$ . We have that $M \subseteq  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ . We also have that ${\left. M\right| }_{S} = N = {\left. {Q}_{2}\left( I\right) \right| }_{S}$ ,and since ${\overrightarrow{v}}_{2} \subseteq  S$ ,this means that ${\left. M\right| }_{{\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}} = {\left. {Q}_{2}\left( I\right) \right| }_{{\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}}$ . The mappings $M$ are computed with linear preprocessing and constant delay as this is the complexity of the CDY algorithm and the manipulations we describe only require constant time per answer.

对于每个映射$\mu  \in  N$，我们通过以下方式将其扩展为${Q}_{2}$所有变量的映射：从包含$S$的连通子树开始遍历$T$的顶点，每步处理一个已处理顶点的邻接顶点。假设某步开始时存在从集合${S}_{1}$出发的同态映射$\mu$，此时处理原子$R\left( {\overrightarrow{v},\overrightarrow{u}}\right)$（其中$\overrightarrow{v} \subseteq  {S}_{1}$且$\overrightarrow{u} \cap  {S}_{1} = \varnothing$）。我们选取$R$中形如$\left( {\mu \left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) ,\overrightarrow{t}}\right)$的元组，将$\mu$扩展至$\mu \left( \overrightarrow{u}\right)  = \overrightarrow{t}$。由于悬空元组已被移除，此类元组必然存在。此扩展过程耗时恒定，最终得到${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) } \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$。通过这类扩展操作，我们将$M = \left\{  {{\left. {\mu }^{ + }\right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) } \mid  {\mu }^{ + }}\right.$设为$\mu  \in  N\}$的扩展映射。由此可得$M \subseteq  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$，且因${\overrightarrow{v}}_{2} \subseteq  S$，故有${\left. M\right| }_{{\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}} = {\left. {Q}_{2}\left( I\right) \right| }_{{\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}}$。这些映射$M$的计算具有线性预处理时间和恒定延迟，此复杂度与CDY算法一致，且所述操作对每个答案仅需恒定时间。

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{1}$ The conference version of this article contains a definition of providing variables instead of that of supplying variables. The difference is that the supplied variables are specified in terms of the giving CQ, while the providing variables are specified in terms of the receiving CQ. We need the terminology to refer to the giving CQ to be able to specify a variable set that one CQ supplies and several CQs use, and so we introduced this minor change of naming with respect to the previous version in order to avoid confusion.

${}^{1}$本文会议版原采用"提供变量"定义而非现行"供给变量"定义。二者区别在于：供给变量是依据给予方CQ定义的，而提供变量是依据接收方CQ定义的。为准确描述一个CQ供给、多个CQ使用的变量集，我们需要基于给予方CQ的术语体系，故对前版命名作出微调以避免混淆。

${}^{2}$ The version of Lemma 10 as it was phrased in the conference version of this article was incorrect. In fact,it was too strong, and the slightly weaker version given here describes more accurately what we need.

${}^{2}$本文会议版中引理10的表述存在错误。原版本断言过强，此处给出的稍弱版本更准确反映了我们的实际需求。
