We define ${R}^{M} = \left\{  {{\mu }^{ + }\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)  \mid  {\mu }^{ + } \in  M}\right\}$ . Since ${\left. M\right| }_{{\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}} = {\left. {Q}_{2}\left( I\right) \right| }_{{\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}}$ ,this is the same as $\left\{  {{\mu }_{2}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)  \mid  {\mu }_{2} \in  }\right.$ $\left. {{Q}_{2}\left( I\right) }\right\}$ . Let ${Q}_{1}$ be a CQ such that there is a body-homomorphism $h$ from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ ,and let ${\mu }_{1} \in  \operatorname{full}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) \left( I\right)$ . Since $h$ is a body-homomorphism,for every atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ in ${Q}_{2},R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)$ is an atom in ${Q}_{1}$ . Since ${\mu }_{1}$ forms an answer to the full ${Q}_{1}$ ,for every such atom ${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{I}$ . This means that ${\mu }_{1} \circ  {\left. h\right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) }$ is an answer to ${Q}_{2}$ ,so there exists ${\mu }_{2} \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ such that ${\mu }_{1} \circ  {\left. h\right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) } = {\mu }_{2}$ . By construction, ${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right) }\right)  = {\mu }_{2}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)  \in  {R}^{M}$ .

我们定义${R}^{M} = \left\{  {{\mu }^{ + }\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)  \mid  {\mu }^{ + } \in  M}\right\}$。由于${\left. M\right| }_{{\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}} = {\left. {Q}_{2}\left( I\right) \right| }_{{\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}}$，这等同于$\left\{  {{\mu }_{2}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)  \mid  {\mu }_{2} \in  }\right.$$\left. {{Q}_{2}\left( I\right) }\right\}$。设${Q}_{1}$为一个CQ（Conjunctive Query，合取查询），存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态映射$h$，并令${\mu }_{1} \in  \operatorname{full}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) \left( I\right)$。由于$h$是体同态映射，对于${Q}_{2},R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)$中的每个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$，在${Q}_{1}$中都有对应原子。由于${\mu }_{1}$构成完整${Q}_{1}$的答案，对于每个这样的原子${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{I}$成立。这意味着${\mu }_{1} \circ  {\left. h\right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) }$是${Q}_{2}$的答案，因此存在${\mu }_{2} \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$使得${\mu }_{1} \circ  {\left. h\right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) } = {\mu }_{2}$。根据构造，${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right) }\right)  = {\mu }_{2}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)  \in  {R}^{M}$。

Note that if the mapping $h$ is not a body-homomorphism,Lemma 10 does not hold in general.

注意：若映射$h$不是体同态映射，则引理10通常不成立。

Example 11. Consider $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ ,which is a slight modification of Example 2,with

示例11. 考虑$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$，这是对示例2的轻微修改，其中

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right) \text{and}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,w}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( y\right) .
$$

Since ${R}_{4}$ is not a relational symbol in ${Q}_{1}$ ,there is no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . If ${R}_{4}^{I} =$ dom,then we can take the same approach as in Example 2,as the answers of ${Q}_{2}$ form ${\left. {Q}_{1}\left( I\right) \right| }_{\{ x,z,y\} }$ . However,if ${R}_{4}^{I}$ is smaller,this extra atom may filter the answers to ${Q}_{2}$ ,and we do not obtain all of ${\left. {Q}_{1}\left( I\right) \right| }_{\{ x,z,y\} }$ in general.

由于${R}_{4}$不是${Q}_{1}$中的关系符号，不存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态映射。若${R}_{4}^{I} =$属于定义域，则可采取与示例2相同的方法，因为${Q}_{2}$的答案构成${\left. {Q}_{1}\left( I\right) \right| }_{\{ x,z,y\} }$。然而当${R}_{4}^{I}$较小时，这个额外原子可能过滤${Q}_{2}$的答案，通常无法获得完整的${\left. {Q}_{1}\left( I\right) \right| }_{\{ x,z,y\} }$。

During evaluation, a set of supplied variables can form an auxiliary relation, accessible by an auxiliary atom. We call the query with its auxiliary atoms a union extension.

求值过程中，一组提供的变量可构成辅助关系，通过辅助原子访问。我们将带有辅助原子的查询称为联合扩展。

Definition 12. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$ be a UCQ. An extension sequence for $Q$ is a sequence ${Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{N}$ where $Q = {Q}^{1}$ and for all $1 < j \leq  N,{Q}^{j}$ is a UCQ of the form ${Q}_{1}^{j} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}^{j}$ such that the following holds. For some relational symbol ${R}_{j}$ that does not appear in ${Q}_{j - 1}$ and a sequence ${\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}$ of variables supplied by a previous ${Q}_{p\left( j\right) }^{\ell }$ (i.e., $\ell  \leq  j$ and $1 \leq  p\left( j\right)  \leq  n$ ) we have the following for all $i = 1,\ldots ,n$ :

定义12. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$为联合查询(UCQ)。$Q$的扩展序列是指满足以下条件的序列${Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{N}$：其中$Q = {Q}^{1}$且对所有$1 < j \leq  N,{Q}^{j}$，存在形如${Q}_{1}^{j} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}^{j}$的联合查询，使得对于某个未出现在${Q}_{j - 1}$中的关系符号${R}_{j}$，以及由前驱${Q}_{p\left( j\right) }^{\ell }$提供的变量序列${\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}$（即$\ell  \leq  j$和$1 \leq  p\left( j\right)  \leq  n$），以下条件对所有$i = 1,\ldots ,n$成立：

- ${Q}_{i}^{j} = {Q}_{i}^{j - 1}$ ; or

- ${Q}_{i}^{j} = {Q}_{i}^{j - 1}$；或

- there is a body-homomorphism ${h}_{p\left( j\right) ,i}$ from ${Q}_{p\left( j\right) }^{1}$ to ${Q}_{i}^{1}$ ,and ${Q}_{i}^{j}$ is obtained by adding the atom ${R}_{j}\left( {{h}_{p\left( j\right) ,i}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}\right) }\right)$ to ${Q}_{i}^{j - 1}$ .

- 存在从${Q}_{p\left( j\right) }^{1}$到${Q}_{i}^{1}$的体同态映射${h}_{p\left( j\right) ,i}$，且${Q}_{i}^{j}$是通过将原子式${R}_{j}\left( {{h}_{p\left( j\right) ,i}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}\right) }\right)$添加到${Q}_{i}^{j - 1}$而获得

If such an extension sequence exists,we call ${Q}^{N}$ a union extension of ${Q}^{1}$ . Atoms that appear in ${Q}^{N}$ but not in ${Q}^{1}$ are called virtual atoms.

若存在此类扩展序列，则称${Q}^{N}$为${Q}^{1}$的联合扩展。出现在${Q}^{N}$但未出现在${Q}^{1}$中的原子式称为虚拟原子

Consider Example 2 again. We already established that ${Q}_{2}$ supplies $\{ x,y,w\}  \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ and that there is a body-homomorphism $h : \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ with $h\left( \left( {x,y,w}\right) \right)  = \left( {x,z,y}\right)$ . Thus, $Q$ has the union extension that contains ${Q}_{2}$ and ${Q}_{1}^{ + }\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right) ,{R}^{\prime }\left( {x,z,y}\right)$ ,where ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ is obtained by adding the virtual atom ${R}^{\prime }\left( {x,z,y}\right)$ to ${Q}_{1}$ .

再次考虑示例2。我们已确认${Q}_{2}$提供$\{ x,y,w\}  \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$，且存在满足$h\left( \left( {x,y,w}\right) \right)  = \left( {x,z,y}\right)$的体同态映射$h : \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$。因此，$Q$具有包含${Q}_{2}$和${Q}_{1}^{ + }\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right) ,{R}^{\prime }\left( {x,z,y}\right)$的联合扩展，其中${Q}_{1}^{ + }$是通过将虚拟原子${R}^{\prime }\left( {x,z,y}\right)$添加至${Q}_{1}$获得

### 3.4 Extension-Based Tractability

### 3.4 基于扩展的可追踪性

Union extensions can transform an intractable query to a free-connex one.

联合扩展可将难解查询转化为自由连接查询

Definition 13. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$ be a UCQ.

定义13. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$为联合查询

- ${Q}_{1}$ is said to be union-free-connex with respect to $Q$ if it has a free-connex union extension.

- 若${Q}_{1}$具有自由连接联合扩展，则称其相对于$Q$是联合自由连接的

- $Q$ is free-connex if all CQs in $Q$ are union-free-connex.

- 若$Q$中所有联合查询均为联合自由连接，则称该查询为自由连接查询

Note that the term free-connex for UCQs is a generalization of that for CQs: If a UCQ $Q$ contains only one CQ,then $Q$ is free-connex iff the CQ it contains is free-connex. We next show that tractability of free-connex queries also carries over to UCQs.

需注意，联合查询中"自由连接"这一术语是单查询情形的推广：若联合查询$Q$仅包含单个查询，则$Q$为自由连接当且仅当其包含的查询本身是自由连接的。下文将证明自由连接查询的可追踪性同样适用于联合查询

THEOREM 14. Let $Q$ be a UCQ. If $Q$ is free-connex,then $\operatorname{ENUM}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

定理14. 设$Q$为一个UCQ（联合查询）。若$Q$满足自由连接性，则$\operatorname{ENUM}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

Proof. We begin by sketching the proof. For answering $Q$ ,we describe an algorithm $\mathcal{A}$ which is comprised of two phases: a provision phase and a final results phase. In the provision phase, a free-connex union-extension is instantiated for each CQ in the union. In the final results phase, the answers of every CQ in the union are enumerated via their free-connex union-extensions. These answers can be enumerated efficiently using the CDY algorithm (see Theorem 3). We will use Lemma 10 to generate some answers to $Q$ during the provision phase; this permits us to use more than linear time before the final results phase while still achieving an enumeration algorithm with only linear preprocessing time. We will use the Cheater's Lemma (Lemma 7) to remove duplicates and obtain the time bounds we want.

证明. 我们首先概述证明思路。为求解$Q$，我们设计了一个由两个阶段组成的算法$\mathcal{A}$：预备阶段和最终结果阶段。预备阶段会为联合查询中的每个CQ（合取查询）实例化一个自由连接联合扩展。最终结果阶段通过各CQ的自由连接联合扩展来枚举联合查询的所有答案。这些答案可通过CDY算法高效枚举（见定理3）。我们将利用引理10在预备阶段生成部分$Q$的答案，这使得最终结果阶段前可突破线性时间限制，同时仍保证枚举算法仅需线性预处理时间。通过"作弊者引理"（引理7）消除重复结果，最终获得目标时间复杂度。

Initial Algorithm. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$ ,and take an extension sequence ${Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{N}$ where ${Q}^{1} = Q$ and ${Q}^{N}$ comprises of free-connex CQs. The provision phase consists of $N - 1$ provision steps. Let $I = {I}^{1}$ be the input database instance. During the $j$ th provision step (with $1 < j \leq  N$ ),we extend the database instance ${I}_{j - 1}$ into an instance ${I}_{j}$ that matches ${Q}^{j}$ . We use Lemma 10 to generate a set of answers ${M}_{j} \subseteq  {Q}_{p\left( j\right) }^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)$ while also computing a relation ${R}^{{M}_{j}}$ . We set ${\left( {R}_{j}\right) }^{{I}_{j}} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}^{{M}_{j}}$ . The other relations remain as they were; that is, ${R}^{{I}_{j}} = {R}^{{I}_{j - 1}}$ for every relational symbol in the UCQ except for ${R}_{j}$ . By the end of the provision phase,the algorithm computes an instance ${I}_{N}$ that matches ${Q}^{N}$ . Finally,we perform the final results phase where we compute ${Q}_{i}^{N}\left( {I}_{N}\right)$ for every ${Q}_{i}$ in the union using the CDY algorithm.

初始算法. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$，取扩展序列${Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{N}$，其中${Q}^{1} = Q$且${Q}^{N}$由自由连接CQ组成。预备阶段包含$N - 1$个预备步骤。令$I = {I}^{1}$为初始数据库实例。在第$j$个预备步骤（$1 < j \leq  N$）时，我们将数据库实例${I}_{j - 1}$扩展为与${Q}^{j}$匹配的实例${I}_{j}$。运用引理10生成答案集${M}_{j} \subseteq  {Q}_{p\left( j\right) }^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)$，同时计算关系${R}^{{M}_{j}}$。设定${\left( {R}_{j}\right) }^{{I}_{j}} \mathrel{\text{:=}} {R}^{{M}_{j}}$，其余关系保持不变，即除${R}_{j}$外，UCQ中每个关系符号满足${R}^{{I}_{j}} = {R}^{{I}_{j - 1}}$。预备阶段结束时，算法将生成与${Q}^{N}$匹配的实例${I}_{N}$。最后执行最终结果阶段，使用CDY算法计算联合查询中每个${Q}_{i}$对应的${Q}_{i}^{N}\left( {I}_{N}\right)$。

Correctness. We prove that for all $1 \leq  i \leq  n$ and $1 \leq  j \leq  N$ we have that ${Q}_{i}\left( I\right)  = {Q}_{i}^{j}\left( {I}_{j}\right)$ by induction on $j$ . The base case trivially holds as ${Q}_{i}\left( I\right)  = {Q}_{i}^{1}\left( {I}_{1}\right)$ by definition. Now consider the $j$ th provision step. Intuitively, answers to an extended CQ are by definition all answers to its previous version that agree with some tuple in the new atom. Since the new atom contains a projection of the answers, the extension has exactly the same answers as its previous version. More formally, let ${Q}_{e\left( j\right) }$ be a CQ extended in step $j$ . For every mapping $\mu$ ,by definition of the extension we have that $\mu  \in  {Q}_{e\left( j\right) }^{j}\left( {I}_{j}\right)$ if and only if both $\mu  \in  {Q}_{e\left( j\right) }^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)$ and $\mu \left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}_{j}^{{I}_{j}}$ ,and these two conditions hold if and only if $\mu  \in  {Q}_{e\left( j\right) }^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)$ since for all $\mu  \in  {Q}_{e\left( j\right) }^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)$ we have that $\mu \left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}_{j}^{{I}_{j}}$ . This proves that ${Q}_{e\left( j\right) }^{j}\left( {I}_{j}\right)  = {Q}_{e\left( j\right) }^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)$ . This shows that for every ${Q}_{i}$ in the union, ${Q}_{i}^{j}\left( {I}_{j}\right)  = {Q}_{i}^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)$ . By the induction hypothesis, ${Q}_{i}^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)  = {Q}_{i}\left( I\right)$ . This concludes the proof that ${Q}_{i}^{j}\left( {I}_{j}\right)  = {Q}_{i}\left( I\right)$ for all $j \leq  N$ ,and in particular, ${Q}^{N}\left( {I}_{N}\right)  = Q\left( I\right)$ . Since ${Q}^{N}\left( {I}_{N}\right)  = Q\left( I\right)$ ,the final results phase computes all answers to $Q$ . Since ${Q}_{i}\left( I\right)  = {Q}_{i}^{j}\left( {I}_{j}\right)$ for all $i$ and $j$ ,the mappings generated by Lemma 10 during the provision phase are also answers to $Q$ .

正确性。我们通过归纳法证明，对于所有$1 \leq  i \leq  n$和$1 \leq  j \leq  N$，均有${Q}_{i}\left( I\right)  = {Q}_{i}^{j}\left( {I}_{j}\right)$。根据定义，基本情况显然成立，因为${Q}_{i}\left( I\right)  = {Q}_{i}^{1}\left( {I}_{1}\right)$。现考虑第$j$次扩展步骤。直观而言，根据定义，扩展后的合取查询（CQ）的答案是其先前版本中与新原子元组相容的所有答案。由于新原子包含答案的投影，扩展后的查询与其先前版本具有完全相同的答案。更形式化地说，设${Q}_{e\left( j\right) }$为第$j$步扩展的CQ。根据扩展定义，对于每个映射$\mu$，当且仅当同时满足$\mu  \in  {Q}_{e\left( j\right) }^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)$和$\mu \left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}_{j}^{{I}_{j}}$时，才有$\mu  \in  {Q}_{e\left( j\right) }^{j}\left( {I}_{j}\right)$；而这两个条件成立的充要条件是$\mu  \in  {Q}_{e\left( j\right) }^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)$，因为对于所有$\mu  \in  {Q}_{e\left( j\right) }^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)$都有$\mu \left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}_{j}^{{I}_{j}}$。由此证得${Q}_{e\left( j\right) }^{j}\left( {I}_{j}\right)  = {Q}_{e\left( j\right) }^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)$。这表明并集中的每个${Q}_{i}$都满足${Q}_{i}^{j}\left( {I}_{j}\right)  = {Q}_{i}^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)$。根据归纳假设，${Q}_{i}^{j - 1}\left( {I}_{j - 1}\right)  = {Q}_{i}\left( I\right)$。至此我们证明了对于所有$j \leq  N$都有${Q}_{i}^{j}\left( {I}_{j}\right)  = {Q}_{i}\left( I\right)$，特别地，${Q}^{N}\left( {I}_{N}\right)  = Q\left( I\right)$。由于${Q}^{N}\left( {I}_{N}\right)  = Q\left( I\right)$，最终结果阶段会计算出$Q$的所有答案。又因对于所有$i$和$j$都有${Q}_{i}\left( I\right)  = {Q}_{i}^{j}\left( {I}_{j}\right)$，引理10在扩展阶段生成的映射也是$Q$的答案。

The algorithm $\mathcal{A}$ we presented so far produces the results we want,but it is not a constant delay enumeration algorithm. It is left to show that $\mathcal{A}$ conforms to the conditions of the Cheater’s lemma. This would mean that we can apply the lemma,and conclude that $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  \operatorname{DelayClin}$ .

目前提出的算法$\mathcal{A}$虽能生成预期结果，但并非恒定延迟枚举算法。尚需证明$\mathcal{A}$符合作弊者引理的条件，这意味着我们可以应用该引理，从而得出$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  \operatorname{DelayClin}$的结论。

Duplicates. Overall the algorithm produces results in $N$ provision steps during the provision phase and in $n$ CQ evaluation steps during the final results phase. The results produced at each individual step contain no duplicates. Therefore,every result appears at most $N + n$ times. Since $N$ and $n$ are constants,this is a constant number of duplicates per value.

重复项。总体而言，该算法在供应阶段产生$N$个供应步骤的结果，在最终结果阶段产生$n$个CQ评估步骤的结果。每个独立步骤产生的结果不包含重复项。因此，每个结果最多出现$N + n$次。由于$N$和$n$是常数，这意味着每个值的重复次数是恒定的。

Delay. Consider the $j$ th provision step. It starts with a preprocessing of time $O\left( \left| {I}_{j - 1}\right| \right)$ followed by a constant delay enumeration of a set ${M}_{j}$ of answers. At its end, $O\left( \left| {M}_{j}\right| \right)$ time is required to compute the new relation. This means that the delay before the first answer of the $j$ th provision step is $O\left( {\left| {I}_{j - 1}\right|  + \left| {M}_{j - 1}\right| }\right)$ ,and the delay before answers in the provision phase that are not first in their step is constant. During the final results phase,we have $n$ steps in which we apply the CDY algorithm on an extended CQ. Each such step starts with $O\left( \left| {I}_{N}\right| \right)$ preprocessing time followed by constant delay. Thus,during the final results phase,there are $n$ times where the delay is $O\left( \left| {I}_{N}\right| \right)$ , and in other times the delay is constant.

延迟。考虑第$j$个供应步骤。它始于耗时$O\left( \left| {I}_{j - 1}\right| \right)$的预处理，随后是对答案集${M}_{j}$的恒定延迟枚举。步骤结束时，需要$O\left( \left| {M}_{j}\right| \right)$时间计算新关系。这意味着第$j$个供应步骤首个答案前的延迟为$O\left( {\left| {I}_{j - 1}\right|  + \left| {M}_{j - 1}\right| }\right)$，而供应阶段非首答案的延迟为恒定值。在最终结果阶段，我们通过$n$个步骤对扩展CQ应用CDY算法。每个此类步骤始于$O\left( \left| {I}_{N}\right| \right)$预处理时间，随后是恒定延迟。因此，在最终结果阶段存在$n$次$O\left( \left| {I}_{N}\right| \right)$延迟，其余时刻延迟均为恒定值。

Fig. 2. A $\{ x,y,w\}$ -connex tree for ${Q}_{2}$ and a $\{ x,y,w\}$ -connex tree for ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ of Example 2.

图2. 示例2中${Q}_{2}$的$\{ x,y,w\}$-连通树及${Q}_{1}^{ + }$的$\{ x,y,w\}$-连通树。

<!-- Media -->

We have that $\left| {I}_{j}\right|  = \left| {I}_{j - 1}\right|  + \left| {R}_{j}^{{M}_{j}}\right|  \leq  \left| {I}_{j - 1}\right|  + \left| {M}_{j}\right|$ for all $1 < j \leq  N$ ,and $\left| {I}_{1}\right|  = \left| I\right|$ . By induction, this shows that $\left| {I}_{j}\right|  \leq  \left| I\right|  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 2}}^{j}\left| {M}_{i}\right|$ . This means that whenever the delay is not constant,it is linear in the input size plus the number of (not necessarily unique) answers produces thus far. Since there are $n + N$ such times where the delay is not constant,according to Proposition 6,the algorithm $\mathcal{A}$ runs in linear partial time,and the requirements of the Cheater’s Lemma on the delay are met.

我们得出对于所有$1 < j \leq  N$，$\left| {I}_{j}\right|  = \left| {I}_{j - 1}\right|  + \left| {R}_{j}^{{M}_{j}}\right|  \leq  \left| {I}_{j - 1}\right|  + \left| {M}_{j}\right|$成立，且$\left| {I}_{1}\right|  = \left| I\right|$。通过归纳法可证$\left| {I}_{j}\right|  \leq  \left| I\right|  + \mathop{\sum }\limits_{{i = 2}}^{j}\left| {M}_{i}\right|$。这意味着当延迟非常数时，其与输入规模加上当前产生的（非唯一）答案数量呈线性关系。根据命题6，由于存在$n + N$次非常数延迟的情况，算法$\mathcal{A}$以线性部分时间运行，满足Cheater引理对延迟的要求。

We can now conclude the tractability of Example 2. In Section 3.3,we saw that ${Q}_{1}$ has the union extension ${Q}_{1}^{ + }\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right) ,{R}^{\prime }\left( {x,z,y}\right)$ . Since ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ is free-connex (see Figure 2),the CQ ${Q}_{1}$ is union-free-connex. Since every query in $Q$ is union-free-connex,we have that $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ by Theorem 14.

现在可以得出示例2的可处理性结论。在第3.3节中，我们观察到${Q}_{1}$具有联合扩展${Q}_{1}^{ + }\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right) ,{R}^{\prime }\left( {x,z,y}\right)$。由于${Q}_{1}^{ + }$是自由连通的（见图2），CQ${Q}_{1}$属于无联合连通类型。鉴于$Q$中每个查询都是无联合连通的，根据定理14可得$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

Remark 1. Example 2 is a counter example to a past made claim [8, Theorem 4.2b]. The claim is that if a UCQ contains an intractable CQ and does not contain redundant CQs (a CQ contained in another CQ in the union), then the union is intractable. In contrast, none of the CQs in Example 2 is redundant, ${Q}_{1}$ is intractable,and yet the UCQ is tractable.

注1. 示例2是对既往论断[8，定理4.2b]的反例。原论断称：若UCQ包含一个难解CQ且不含冗余CQ（即联合中某CQ被其他CQ包含），则该联合是难解的。然而示例2中的CQ均非冗余，${Q}_{1}$虽属难解，但整个UCQ却是可解的。

The intuition behind the proof of the past claim is reducing the hard CQ ${Q}_{1}$ to $Q$ . This can be done by assigning each variable of ${Q}_{1}$ with a different and disjoint domain (e.g.,by concatenating the variable names to the values in the relations corresponding to the atoms), and leaving the relations that do not appear in the atoms of ${Q}_{1}$ empty. It is well known that ${Q}_{1} \subseteq  {Q}_{2}$ iff there exists a homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}\left\lbrack  {13}\right\rbrack$ . The claim is that since there is no homomorphism from another CQ in the union to ${Q}_{1}$ ,then there are no answers to the other CQs with this reduction. However, it is possible that there is a body-homomorphism from another CQ to ${Q}_{1}$ even if it is not a full homomorphism (the free variables do not map to each other). Therefore,in cases of a body-homomorphism,the reduction from ${Q}_{1}$ to $Q$ does not work. In such cases, the union may be tractable, as we show in Theorem 14. In Lemma 18, we use the same proof described here, but restrict it to UCQs where there is no body-homomorphism from other CQs to ${Q}_{1}$ .

证明前述主张的直观思路是将困难的CQ ${Q}_{1}$归约到$Q$。具体操作是为${Q}_{1}$的每个变量分配互不相交的域（例如通过将变量名与对应原子谓词关系中值进行拼接），并将未出现在${Q}_{1}$原子谓词中的关系置空。众所周知，当且仅当存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}\left\lbrack  {13}\right\rbrack$的同态映射时，${Q}_{1} \subseteq  {Q}_{2}$成立。该主张认为，由于并集中其他CQ到${Q}_{1}$不存在同态映射，因此这种归约下其他CQ不会产生答案。然而可能出现这种情况：即使不是完全同态映射（自由变量未能相互映射），其他CQ到${Q}_{1}$仍存在体同态映射。因此当存在体同态映射时，从${Q}_{1}$到$Q$的归约将失效。如定理14所示，此类情况下并集可能是易处理的。在引理18中，我们沿用此处的证明方法，但将其限制于不存在其他CQ到${Q}_{1}$体同态映射的UCQ情形。

The tractability result in Theorem 14 is based on the structure of the union-extended queries. This means that the intractability of any query within a UCQ can be resolved as long as another query can supply the right variables. The following example shows that this can even be the case for a UCQ only consisting of non-free-connex CQs. It also illustrates why the definition of union extensions needs to be recursive.

定理14中的易处理性结果基于联合扩展查询的结构特性。这意味着只要UCQ中任一查询能提供正确变量，即可解决该UCQ内任何查询的难处理性问题。下例表明，这种情况甚至可能出现在仅由非自由连接CQ组成的UCQ中，同时也解释了为何联合扩展的定义需要采用递归形式。

Example 15. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2} \cup  {Q}_{3}$ with

示例15. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2} \cup  {Q}_{3}$满足

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,v,u}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,{z}_{1}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{z}_{1},{z}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {{z}_{2},{z}_{3}}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {{z}_{3},y}\right) ,{R}_{5}\left( {y,v,u}\right) \text{,}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,v,u}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,v}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {v,{z}_{1}}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {{z}_{1},u}\right) ,{R}_{5}\left( {u,{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right) \text{,}
$$

$$
{Q}_{3}\left( {x,y,v,u}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,{z}_{1}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{z}_{1},y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,v}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {v,u}\right) ,{R}_{5}\left( {u,{t}_{1},{t}_{2}}\right) .
$$

Each of three CQs is difficult on its own: ${Q}_{1}$ has the free-path $\left( {x,{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{z}_{3},y}\right)$ ,while ${Q}_{2}$ has the free-path $\left( {v,{z}_{1},u}\right)$ ,and ${Q}_{3}$ has the free-path $\left( {x,{z}_{1},y}\right)$ . The CQ ${Q}_{2}$ supplies $\{ x,y,v\}$ as ${Q}_{2}$ is $\{ x,y,v\}$ - connex and $\{ x,y,v\}$ are free in ${Q}_{2}$ . Since there is a body-homomorphism ${h}_{2,3}$ from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{3}$ with ${h}_{2,3}\left( \left( {x,y,v}\right) \right)  = \left( {x,{z}_{1},y}\right)$ ,we can extend the body of ${Q}_{3}$ by the virtual atom ${R}^{\prime }\left( {x,{z}_{1},y}\right)$ ,which yields a free-connex extension ${Q}_{3}^{ + }$ of ${Q}_{3}$ . Similarly,we have that ${Q}_{3}$ supplies $\{ y,v,u\}$ ,and there is a body-homomorphism ${h}_{3,2}$ from ${Q}_{3}$ to ${Q}_{2}$ with ${h}_{3,2}\left( \left( {y,v,u}\right) \right)  = \left( {v,{z}_{1},u}\right)$ . Extending ${Q}_{2}$ by ${R}^{\prime \prime }\left( {v,{z}_{1},u}\right)$ yields the free-connex extension ${Q}_{2}^{ + }$ . Since ${Q}_{2}^{ + }$ and ${Q}_{3}^{ + }$ each supply $\{ x,y,v,u\}$ ,we can add virtual atoms with the variables $\left( {x,{z}_{1},{z}_{2},y}\right)$ and $\left( {x,{z}_{2},{z}_{3},y}\right)$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . This results in a free-connex extension ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ . The UCQ ${Q}_{1}^{ + } \cup  {Q}_{2}^{ + } \cup  {Q}_{3}^{ + }$ is a free-connex union extension of $Q$ . By Theorem 14,Enum $\langle Q\rangle  \in$ ${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

三个CQ各自都存在难点：${Q}_{1}$具有自由路径$\left( {x,{z}_{1},{z}_{2},{z}_{3},y}\right)$，而${Q}_{2}$具有自由路径$\left( {v,{z}_{1},u}\right)$，且${Q}_{3}$具有自由路径$\left( {x,{z}_{1},y}\right)$。CQ ${Q}_{2}$提供$\{ x,y,v\}$，因为${Q}_{2}$是$\{ x,y,v\}$-连通且$\{ x,y,v\}$在${Q}_{2}$中自由。由于存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{3}$的体同态${h}_{2,3}$（携带${h}_{2,3}\left( \left( {x,y,v}\right) \right)  = \left( {x,{z}_{1},y}\right)$），我们可以通过虚拟原子${R}^{\prime }\left( {x,{z}_{1},y}\right)$扩展${Q}_{3}$的体结构，从而得到${Q}_{3}$的自由连通扩展${Q}_{3}^{ + }$。同理，${Q}_{3}$提供$\{ y,v,u\}$，且存在从${Q}_{3}$到${Q}_{2}$的体同态${h}_{3,2}$（携带${h}_{3,2}\left( \left( {y,v,u}\right) \right)  = \left( {v,{z}_{1},u}\right)$）。通过${R}^{\prime \prime }\left( {v,{z}_{1},u}\right)$扩展${Q}_{2}$得到自由连通扩展${Q}_{2}^{ + }$。由于${Q}_{2}^{ + }$和${Q}_{3}^{ + }$均提供$\{ x,y,v,u\}$，我们可以向${Q}_{1}$添加含变量$\left( {x,{z}_{1},{z}_{2},y}\right)$和$\left( {x,{z}_{2},{z}_{3},y}\right)$的虚拟原子，最终形成自由连通扩展${Q}_{1}^{ + }$。UCQ ${Q}_{1}^{ + } \cup  {Q}_{2}^{ + } \cup  {Q}_{3}^{ + }$是$Q$的自由连通联合扩展。根据定理14，枚举$\langle Q\rangle  \in$${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$。

## 4 LOWER BOUNDS

## 4 下界证明

In this section,we prove lower bounds for evaluating UCQs within the time bounds of DelayClin. Since for CQs we currently only have hardness result when they are self-join free, we focus on unions of self-join-free CQs. We begin with some general observations regarding cases where a UCQ is at least as hard as a single CQ it contains, and then continue to handle other cases. In Section 4.1 we discuss unions containing only difficult CQs, and in Section 4.2 we discuss unions containing two body-isomorphic CQs. In both cases such UCQs may be tractable, and in case of such a union of size two, we show that our results from Section 3 capture all tractable unions.

本节将证明在DelayClin时间范围内评估UCQ的下界。鉴于目前对无自连接CQ仅获得硬度结果，我们重点研究无自连接CQ的并集。首先概述UCQ至少与其包含的单个CQ难度相当的一般情况，继而处理其他情形。第4.1节讨论仅含困难CQ的并集，第4.2节讨论包含两个体同构CQ的并集。这两种UCQ都可能存在易处理情况，对于规模为二的并集，我们证明第3节结果已涵盖所有易处理情形。

In order to provide some intuition for the choices we make throughout this section, we first explain where the approach used for proving the hardness of single CQs fails. Consider Example 2. The original proof that shows that ${Q}_{1}$ is hard describes a reduction from Boolean matrix multiplication [4,Lemma 26]. Let $A$ and $B$ be binary representations of Boolean $n \times  n$ matrices, i.e., $\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A$ corresponds to a 1 in the first matrix at index(a,b). Define a database instance $I$ as ${R}_{1}^{I} = A,{R}_{2}^{I} = B$ ,and ${R}_{3}^{I} = \{ 1,\ldots ,n\}  \times  \{  \bot  \}$ . One can show that ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$ corresponds to the answers of ${AB}$ . If $\operatorname{Enum}\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle   \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ ,we can solve matrix multiplication in time $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ ,in contradiction to MAT-MUL. Since ${Q}_{2}$ evaluates over the same relations, ${Q}_{2}$ also produces answers over this construction. Since the number of results for ${Q}_{2}$ might reach up to ${n}^{3}$ ,evaluating $Q$ in constant delay does not necessarily compute the answers to ${Q}_{1}$ in $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ time,and does not contradict the complexity assumption.

为了给本节中的选择提供直观解释，我们首先说明为何证明单个CQ硬度的原有方法会失效。以示例2为例，原始证明${Q}_{1}$难解性的方法采用了布尔矩阵乘法的归约[4,Lemma 26]。设$A$和$B$是布尔$n \times  n$矩阵的二进制表示，即$\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A$对应第一个矩阵中(a,b)位置为1的情况。定义数据库实例$I$为${R}_{1}^{I} = A,{R}_{2}^{I} = B$，且${R}_{3}^{I} = \{ 1,\ldots ,n\}  \times  \{  \bot  \}$。可以证明${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$对应着${AB}$的答案。若$\operatorname{Enum}\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle   \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$，我们就能在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内解决矩阵乘法问题，这与MAT-MUL假设矛盾。由于${Q}_{2}$在相同关系上求值，该构造下${Q}_{2}$也会产生答案。当${Q}_{2}$的结果数量可能高达${n}^{3}$时，以恒定延迟求值$Q$并不必然能在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内计算出${Q}_{1}$的答案，因此不违背复杂性假设。

So, in general, whenever we show a lower bound to a UCQ by computing a hard problem through answering one CQ in the union, we need to ensure that the other CQs cannot have too many answers over this construction. The following lemma formalizes the idea that by assigning variables of a CQ with different domains, we can restrict the answers obtained by other CQs.

因此，一般而言，当我们通过回答UCQ中某个CQ来计算难题以证明下界时，必须确保其他CQ在该构造下不会产生过多答案。以下引理形式化地阐述了这一思想：通过为CQ变量分配不同域，可以限制其他CQ获得的答案。

LEMMA 16. Given a ${CQ}{Q}_{1}$ over a schema $\mathcal{S}$ ,there exist mappings ${\sigma }_{ \neq  }$ and ${\tau }_{ \neq  }$ such that for every database instance I over $\mathcal{S}$ :

引理16. 给定模式$\mathcal{S}$上的${CQ}{Q}_{1}$，存在映射${\sigma }_{ \neq  }$和${\tau }_{ \neq  }$使得对于$\mathcal{S}$上的每个数据库实例I：

- ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  = {\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)$ in multiset notation.

- 多重集表示法中的${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  = {\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)$。

- ${\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right)$ can be computed in linear time.

- ${\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right)$可在线性时间内计算。

- For every ${CQ}{Q}_{i}$ over $\mathcal{S}$ :

- 对于$\mathcal{S}$上的每个${CQ}{Q}_{i}$：

$- {\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( \mu \right)$ can be computed in constant time for every answer $\mu  \in  {Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$ .

每个答案$\mu  \in  {Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$对应的$- {\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( \mu \right)$可在常数时间内计算。

-If there is no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{i}$ to ${Q}_{1},{Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \varnothing$ .

- 若不存在从${Q}_{i}$到${Q}_{1},{Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \varnothing$的体同态。

-If there is no homomorphism from ${Q}_{i}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ ,given $\mu  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  \cup  {Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$ ,it is possible to determine in constant time whether $\mu  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$ .

- 若给定$\mu  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  \cup  {Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$时不存在从${Q}_{i}$到${Q}_{1}$的同态，则可在常数时间内判定$\mu  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$。

Proof. We define ${\sigma }_{ \neq  }$ to assign each variable of ${Q}_{1}$ with a different and disjoint domain by concatenating the variable names to the values in their corresponding relations. For every atom $R\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{m}}\right)$ in ${Q}_{1}$ and tuple $\left( {{c}_{1},\ldots ,{c}_{m}}\right)  \in  {R}^{I}$ ,we add the tuple $\left( {\left( {{c}_{1},{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{c}_{m},{v}_{m}}\right) }\right)$ to ${R}^{{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }$ . All relations that do not appear in ${Q}_{1}$ are left empty. We claim that the results of ${Q}_{1}$ over the original instance are exactly the same as over our construction if we omit the variable names. That is, we define ${\tau }_{ \neq  } : \operatorname{dom} \times  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}$ as ${\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( \left( {c,v}\right) \right)  = c$ ,and show that ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  = {\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {Q\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)$ . Note the ${\sigma }_{ \neq  }$ and ${\tau }_{ \neq  }$ can be computed in linear and constant time,respectively.

证明。我们定义${\sigma }_{ \neq  }$为：通过将变量名与其对应关系中的值进行拼接，为${Q}_{1}$的每个变量分配互不重叠的不同域。对于${Q}_{1}$中的每个原子$R\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{m}}\right)$和元组$\left( {{c}_{1},\ldots ,{c}_{m}}\right)  \in  {R}^{I}$，我们将元组$\left( {\left( {{c}_{1},{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{c}_{m},{v}_{m}}\right) }\right)$加入${R}^{{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }$。未出现在${Q}_{1}$中的所有关系皆留空。我们断言：若忽略变量名，${Q}_{1}$在原实例上的查询结果与在我们构造的实例上完全一致。具体而言，我们将${\tau }_{ \neq  } : \operatorname{dom} \times  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom}$定义为${\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( \left( {c,v}\right) \right)  = c$，并证明${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  = {\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {Q\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)$。需注意${\sigma }_{ \neq  }$和${\tau }_{ \neq  }$可分别在线性时间和常数时间内完成计算。

We first prove that ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  = {\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)$ . The first direction is trivial: if ${\left. v\right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$ , then for every atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ in ${Q}_{1},v\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  {R}^{{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }$ . By construction, ${\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {v\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{I}$ ,and therefore ${\tau }_{ \neq  } \circ  v{\left| {}_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right) \text{. We now show the opposite direction. If}\mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$ ,then for every atom $R\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{m}}\right)$ in ${Q}_{1},\left( {\mu \left( {v}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,\mu \left( {v}_{m}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{I}$ . By construction, $\left( {\left( {\mu \left( {v}_{1}\right) ,{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {\mu \left( {v}_{m}\right) ,{v}_{m}}\right) }\right)  \in$ ${R}^{{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }$ . By defining ${f}_{\mu } : \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom} \times  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ as ${f}_{\mu }\left( u\right)  = \left( {\mu \left( u\right) ,u}\right)$ ,we have ${f}_{\mu } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$ . Since ${\tau }_{ \neq  } \circ  {f}_{\mu } = \mu$ ,we have that ${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)$ ,and this concludes that ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \subseteq$ ${\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)$ .

我们首先证明${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  = {\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)$。第一个方向是显然的：若${\left. v\right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$，则对于${Q}_{1},v\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  {R}^{{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }$中的每个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$。根据构造，${\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {v\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{I}$，因此${\tau }_{ \neq  } \circ  v{\left| {}_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right) \text{. We now show the opposite direction. If}\mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$，那么对于${Q}_{1},\left( {\mu \left( {v}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,\mu \left( {v}_{m}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{I}$中的每个原子$R\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{m}}\right)$。通过构造，$\left( {\left( {\mu \left( {v}_{1}\right) ,{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {\mu \left( {v}_{m}\right) ,{v}_{m}}\right) }\right)  \in$${R}^{{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }$。通过将${f}_{\mu } : \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{dom} \times  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$定义为${f}_{\mu }\left( u\right)  = \left( {\mu \left( u\right) ,u}\right)$，我们得到${f}_{\mu } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$。由于${\tau }_{ \neq  } \circ  {f}_{\mu } = \mu$，我们得出${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)$，由此证得${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \subseteq$${\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)$。

We now show that ${Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \varnothing$ if there is no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{i}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . Assume by contradiction that there exists such ${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right) } \in  {Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$ . This means that,for every atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ in ${Q}_{i},\mu \left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  {R}^{{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }$ . By construction, $\mu \left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ ,like all tuples in ${R}^{{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }$ ,is of the form $\left( {\left( {{c}_{1},{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{c}_{m},{v}_{m}}\right) }\right)$ such that $R\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{m}}\right)$ is an atom in ${Q}_{1}$ . Define $\eta  : \operatorname{dom} \times  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)  \rightarrow$ $\operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ as $\eta \left( {c,v}\right)  = v$ . We have that for every atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ in ${Q}_{i},R\left( {\eta \left( {\mu \left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right) }\right)$ is an atom in ${Q}_{1}$ . This means that $\eta  \circ  \mu$ is a body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{i}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ ,which is a contradiction.

我们现在证明，${Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \varnothing$若不存在从${Q}_{i}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态。假设存在这样的${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right) } \in  {Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$，则意味着对于${Q}_{i},\mu \left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  {R}^{{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }$中的每个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$。根据构造，$\mu \left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$如同${R}^{{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }$中所有元组，其形式为$\left( {\left( {{c}_{1},{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,\left( {{c}_{m},{v}_{m}}\right) }\right)$，使得$R\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{m}}\right)$是${Q}_{1}$中的一个原子。定义$\eta  : \operatorname{dom} \times  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)  \rightarrow$$\operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$为$\eta \left( {c,v}\right)  = v$。我们得出：对于${Q}_{i},R\left( {\eta \left( {\mu \left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right) }\right)$中的每个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$，都是${Q}_{1}$中的一个原子。这意味着$\eta  \circ  \mu$是从${Q}_{i}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态，导致矛盾。

It is left to show that,if there is no homomorphism from ${Q}_{i}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ ,given $\mu  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  \cup$ ${Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$ ,it is possible to determine in constant time whether $\mu  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$ . As we showed, ${Q}_{i}$ has answers over ${\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right)$ only if there is a body-homomorphism $h$ from it to ${Q}_{1}$ . Since this is not a full homomorphism, $h\left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right) }\right)  \neq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ . Note that by definition of UCQs,the free variables are the same for all CQs in the union,and in particular free $\left( {Q}_{i}\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ . If $\mu$ is an answer to ${Q}_{1}$ , then applying $\eta  \circ  \mu$ on free $\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ will result in free $\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ . Otherwise (if $\mu$ is an answer to ${Q}_{i}$ ),then applying $\eta  \circ  \mu$ on $\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right)$ will result in $h\left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right) }\right)$ . Since $h\left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right) }\right)  \neq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ ,this helps us distinguish the answers: apply $\eta  \circ  \mu$ on the free $\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ ; if this results in free $\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ ,then $\mu$ is an answer to ${Q}_{1}$ ; otherwise, $\mu$ is an answer to ${Q}_{i}$ .

余下需证明的是，若不存在从${Q}_{i}$到${Q}_{1}$的同态映射，在给定$\mu  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  \cup$${Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$的情况下，可在常数时间内判定$\mu  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)$。如前所述，${Q}_{i}$仅在存在到${Q}_{1}$的体同态映射$h$时对${\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right)$有解。由于此非完全同态，$h\left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right) }\right)  \neq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$。需注意根据UCQs定义，所有联合查询中自由变量相同，尤其自由$\left( {Q}_{i}\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$。若$\mu$是${Q}_{1}$的解，则对自由$\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$施加$\eta  \circ  \mu$将得到自由$\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$；反之（若$\mu$是${Q}_{i}$的解），对$\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right)$施加$\eta  \circ  \mu$将得到$h\left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right) }\right)$。鉴于$h\left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right) }\right)  \neq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$，此法可区分解：对自由$\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$施加$\eta  \circ  \mu$，若得自由$\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$则$\mu$属${Q}_{1}$，否则属${Q}_{i}$。

Example 17. Consider the union of ${Q}_{1}\left( x\right)  \leftarrow  R\left( {x,y}\right)$ with ${Q}_{2}\left( x\right)  \leftarrow  R\left( {y,x}\right)$ and ${Q}_{3}\left( x\right)  \leftarrow$ $R\left( {x,x}\right)$ . If the input database $I$ has ${R}^{I} = \{ \left( {a,a}\right) ,\left( {a,b}\right) \}$ ,then all three queries have $a$ as an answer,and ${Q}_{2}$ also has $b$ . Applying the construction from Lemma 16 results in ${R}^{{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) } = \{ \left( {\left( {a,x}\right) ,\left( {a,y}\right) }\right) ,\left( {\left( {a,x}\right) ,\left( {b,y}\right) }\right) \}$ . Then,we have that ${Q}_{3}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \varnothing$ as there is no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{3}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . There is a body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ but no such full homomorphism. We have that ${Q}_{2}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \{ \left( {a,y}\right) ,\left( {b,y}\right) \}$ and ${Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \{ \left( {a,x}\right) \}$ ,and so the answers can be distinguished by the concatenated variable name. Thus, if we are interested only in answers to ${Q}_{1}$ but we only have the ability to compute the entire UCQ,we can use this construction,ignore the answers containing $y$ ,and project out the variable names. This will result in the answer alone.

示例17. 考虑将${Q}_{1}\left( x\right)  \leftarrow  R\left( {x,y}\right)$与${Q}_{2}\left( x\right)  \leftarrow  R\left( {y,x}\right)$及${Q}_{3}\left( x\right)  \leftarrow$$R\left( {x,x}\right)$进行并集运算。若输入数据库$I$包含${R}^{I} = \{ \left( {a,a}\right) ,\left( {a,b}\right) \}$，则三个查询都将$a$作为答案，且${Q}_{2}$还包含$b$。应用引理16的构造方法将生成${R}^{{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) } = \{ \left( {\left( {a,x}\right) ,\left( {a,y}\right) }\right) ,\left( {\left( {a,x}\right) ,\left( {b,y}\right) }\right) \}$。此时，由于不存在从${Q}_{3}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态映射，故${Q}_{3}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \varnothing$。虽然存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态映射，但不存在完全同态映射。鉴于${Q}_{2}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \{ \left( {a,y}\right) ,\left( {b,y}\right) \}$和${Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \{ \left( {a,x}\right) \}$的特性，可通过连接变量名区分答案。因此，若仅需获取${Q}_{1}$的答案而只能计算整个联合查询时，可运用此构造方法，忽略包含$y$的答案，并投影掉变量名，最终仅得到目标答案。

The following lemma identifies cases where we can encode any arbitrary instance of a CQ to an instance of the union containing it, such that no other CQ in the union returns results.

以下引理明确了可将任意CQ实例编码到包含它的联合查询实例中的情况，确保联合查询中其他CQ不会返回结果。

LEMMA 18. Let $Q$ be a UCQ,and let ${Q}_{1} \in  Q$ such that for all ${Q}_{i} \in  Q \smallsetminus  \left\{  {Q}_{1}\right\}$ there is no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{i}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . Then $\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle  { \leq  }_{e}\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle$ .

引理18. 设$Q$为联合查询，且${Q}_{1} \in  Q$满足对所有${Q}_{i} \in  Q \smallsetminus  \left\{  {Q}_{1}\right\}$都不存在从${Q}_{i}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态映射，则$\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle  { \leq  }_{e}\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle$成立。

Proof. This reduction can be performed using the mappings ${\sigma }_{ \neq  }$ and ${\tau }_{ \neq  }$ defined in Lemma 16. For every ${Q}_{i} \in  Q \smallsetminus  \left\{  {Q}_{1}\right\}$ ,there is no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{i}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ ,and therefore ${Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \varnothing$ . We now have that ${\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {Q\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{{Q}_{i} \in  Q}}{\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)  = {\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)$ . Since we also know that ${\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)  = {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$ ,this concludes our reduction.

证明。该归约可通过引理16中定义的映射${\sigma }_{ \neq  }$和${\tau }_{ \neq  }$实现。对于每个${Q}_{i} \in  Q \smallsetminus  \left\{  {Q}_{1}\right\}$，不存在从${Q}_{i}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态，因此${Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \varnothing$。现可得${\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {Q\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)  = \mathop{\bigcup }\limits_{{{Q}_{i} \in  Q}}{\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)  = {\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)$。由于已知${\tau }_{ \neq  }\left( {{Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right) }\right)  = {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$，至此完成归约。

The lemma above implies that if there is an intractable CQ in a union where no other CQ maps to it via a body-homomorphism, then the entire union is intractable. This also captures cases such as a union of CQs where one of them is hard, and the others contain a relation that does not appear in the hard CQ.

上述引理表明：若并集中存在一个难解的CQ（合取查询），且其他CQ均无法通过体同态映射到该查询，则整个并集都是难解的。这也适用于诸如一个并集中某个CQ是难解的，而其他CQ包含该难解CQ中未出现的关系等情况。

Using the same reduction, a similar statement with relaxed requirements can be made in case it is sufficient to consider the decision problem.

使用相同归约方法，若仅需考虑判定问题，则可放宽条件给出类似结论。

LEMMA 19. Let $Q$ be a UCQ,and let ${Q}_{1} \in  Q$ such that for all ${Q}_{i} \in  Q$ ,either there exists no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{i}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ ,or ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{i}$ are body-isomorphic. Then,DECIDE $\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle   \leq  \operatorname{DECIDE}\langle Q\rangle$ via a linear-time many-one reduction.

引理19。设$Q$为UCQ（联合合取查询），且${Q}_{1} \in  Q$满足：对所有${Q}_{i} \in  Q$，要么不存在从${Q}_{i}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态，要么${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{i}$是体同构的。则可通过线性时间多一归约判定$\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle   \leq  \operatorname{DECIDE}\langle Q\rangle$。

Proof. We use the encoding from Lemma 16. We know that ${Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \varnothing$ for every CQ ${Q}_{i}$ with no body-homomorphism to ${Q}_{1}$ . We claim now that a $\mathrm{{CQ}}{Q}_{j}$ which is body-isomorphic to ${Q}_{1}$ has an answer iff ${Q}_{1}$ has an answer. Therefore, $Q\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ iff ${Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ . We know that ${Q}_{1}$ retains the same answers under this encoding,so in particular ${Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \varnothing$ if and only if ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  = \varnothing$ . Overall,this shows that $Q\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ if and only if ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ .

证明。采用引理16的编码方式。已知对于每个不存在到${Q}_{1}$的体同态的CQ${Q}_{i}$，有${Q}_{i}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \varnothing$。现断言：与${Q}_{1}$体同构的$\mathrm{{CQ}}{Q}_{j}$有解当且仅当${Q}_{1}$有解。因此，$Q\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  \neq  \varnothing$当且仅当${Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  \neq  \varnothing$。已知该编码下${Q}_{1}$保持解不变，故特别地，${Q}_{1}\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  = \varnothing$当且仅当${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  = \varnothing$。综上表明$Q\left( {{\sigma }_{ \neq  }\left( I\right) }\right)  \neq  \varnothing$当且仅当${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$。

We now show formally that,for body-isomorphic CQs ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ and database $I,{Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ iff ${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ . Let $h$ be a body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . That is,for every atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in$ ${Q}_{2}$ ,we have $R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {Q}_{1}$ . If ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ ,then there exists ${\left. \mu \right| }_{\text{free }\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$ ,and for every atom $R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {Q}_{1}$ ,we have $\mu \left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{I}$ . This means that $\mu  \circ  h$ is a homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to $I$ ,and $\mu  \circ  {\left. h\right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) } \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ . So, ${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ . Since there is also a body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{1}$ to ${Q}_{2}$ ,we can show in the same way that if ${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ then ${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$ .

我们现正式证明，对于体同构的合取查询${Q}_{1}$和${Q}_{2}$及数据库$I,{Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$，当且仅当${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$时成立。设$h$为从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态映射，即对于每个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in$${Q}_{2}$，都有$R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {Q}_{1}$。若${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$，则存在${\left. \mu \right| }_{\text{free }\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$，且对每个原子$R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {Q}_{1}$都有$\mu \left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{I}$。这意味着$\mu  \circ  h$是从${Q}_{2}$到$I$的同态映射，且$\mu  \circ  {\left. h\right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) } \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$。因此，${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$。由于还存在从${Q}_{1}$到${Q}_{2}$的体同态映射，同理可证若${Q}_{2}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$则${Q}_{1}\left( I\right)  \neq  \varnothing$。

From Theorem 3, it follows that deciding whether a cyclic CQ has any answers cannot be done in linear time (assuming HYPERCLIQUE). Following Lemma 19,if a UCQ $Q$ contains a cyclic ${Q}_{1}$ ,and the conditions of Lemma 19 are satisfied with respect to this CQ, then the entire union cannot be decided in linear time,and thus $\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

由定理3可知，判定循环合取查询是否有解不能在线性时间内完成（假设HYPERCLIQUE成立）。根据引理19，若并查集$Q$包含循环${Q}_{1}$且满足该引理条件，则整个并集无法线性判定，因此$\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

### 4.1 Unions of Intractable CQs

### 4.1 难解合取查询的并集

We now discuss unions containing only CQs classified as hard according to Theorem 3. Recall that these are called difficult ${CQs}$ ,and they are self-join-free ${CQs}$ and not free-connex. The following lemma can be used to identify a CQ on which we can apply Lemma 18 or Lemma 19.

本节讨论仅包含被定理3判定为困难的合取查询的并集。这类查询称为困难${CQs}$，它们无自连接${CQs}$且非自由连接。以下引理可用于识别可应用引理18或19的合取查询。

LEMMA 20. Let $Q$ be a UCQ. There exists a query ${Q}_{1} \in  Q$ such that for all ${Q}_{i} \in  Q$ either there is no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{i}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ or ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{i}$ are body-homomorphically equivalent.

引理20. 设$Q$为并查集。存在查询${Q}_{1} \in  Q$使得对所有${Q}_{i} \in  Q$，要么不存在从${Q}_{i}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态映射，要么${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{i}$体同态等价。

Proof. Consider a longest sequence $\left( {{Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{m}}\right)$ of CQs from $Q$ such that for every $2 \leq  j \leq  m$ there is a body-homomorphism from ${Q}^{j}$ to ${Q}^{j - 1}$ ,but no body-homomorphism in the opposite direction. We claim that ${Q}_{1} = {Q}^{m}$ satisfies the conditions of the lemma.

证明. 考虑$Q$中最长的合取查询序列$\left( {{Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{m}}\right)$，其中对每个$2 \leq  j \leq  m$存在从${Q}^{j}$到${Q}^{j - 1}$的体同态映射，但反向不存在。我们断言${Q}_{1} = {Q}^{m}$满足引理条件。

First,we show that such a sequence exists. We denote the body-homomorphism from ${Q}^{j}$ to ${Q}^{j - 1}$ by ${\mu }^{j}$ . It is not possible that the same query appears twice in the sequence: if ${Q}^{i} = {Q}^{j}$ where $j > i$ ,then there is a mapping ${\mu }^{i + 2} \circ  \ldots  \circ  {\mu }^{j}$ from ${Q}^{j} = {Q}^{i}$ to ${Q}^{i + 1}$ ,in contradiction to the definition of the sequence. Therefore, $m \leq  \left| Q\right|$ ,and a longest sequence exists. We now show that ${Q}^{m}$ satisfies the requirements. First consider some ${Q}^{j} \in  \left\{  {{Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{m - 1}}\right\}$ . There is a body-homomorphism from ${Q}^{m}$ to ${Q}^{j}$ which is the composition ${\mu }^{j + 1} \circ  \ldots  \circ  {\mu }^{m}$ . Therefore,either there is no body-homomorphism from ${Q}^{j}$ to ${Q}^{m}$ ,or ${Q}^{m}$ and ${Q}^{j}$ are body-homomorphically equivalent. In addition, ${Q}^{m}$ is body-homomorphically equivalent to itself with the identity mapping serving as the body-homomorphisms. It is left to consider CQs that are not on the sequence. Let ${Q}_{i} \in  Q \smallsetminus  \left\{  {{Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{m}}\right\}$ . If there is no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{i}$ to ${Q}^{m}$ ,then we are done. Otherwise,if there is also no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{1}$ to ${Q}^{m}$ ,then $\left( {{Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{m},{Q}_{i}}\right)$ is a longer sequence, contradicting the maximality of the sequence we started with. Therefore, in this case, ${Q}^{m}$ and ${Q}_{i}$ are body-homomorphically equivalent. Overall,we showed that for each query in $Q$ either there is no body-homomorphism from this query to ${Q}^{m}$ or these two CQs are body-homomorphically equivalent.

首先，我们证明此类序列存在。将${Q}^{j}$到${Q}^{j - 1}$的体同态记为${\mu }^{j}$。该序列中同一查询不可能出现两次：若${Q}^{i} = {Q}^{j}$其中$j > i$，则存在从${Q}^{j} = {Q}^{i}$到${Q}^{i + 1}$的映射${\mu }^{i + 2} \circ  \ldots  \circ  {\mu }^{j}$，这与序列定义矛盾。因此，$m \leq  \left| Q\right|$，最长序列存在。现证明${Q}^{m}$满足要求。先考虑某个${Q}^{j} \in  \left\{  {{Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{m - 1}}\right\}$。存在从${Q}^{m}$到${Q}^{j}$的体同态，即复合映射${\mu }^{j + 1} \circ  \ldots  \circ  {\mu }^{m}$。故要么不存在从${Q}^{j}$到${Q}^{m}$的体同态，要么${Q}^{m}$与${Q}^{j}$体同态等价。此外，${Q}^{m}$通过恒等映射与自身构成体同态等价。现考虑不在序列中的CQ。设${Q}_{i} \in  Q \smallsetminus  \left\{  {{Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{m}}\right\}$。若不存在从${Q}_{i}$到${Q}^{m}$的体同态，则得证。否则，若也不存在从${Q}_{1}$到${Q}^{m}$的体同态，则$\left( {{Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{m},{Q}_{i}}\right)$构成更长序列，与我们初始序列的极大性矛盾。因此此时${Q}^{m}$与${Q}_{i}$体同态等价。综上，对$Q$中每个查询，要么不存在到${Q}^{m}$的体同态，要么这两个CQ体同态等价。

Using the results obtained so far, we deduce a characterization of all cases of a union of difficult CQs, except those that contain a pair of body-isomorphic acyclic CQs.

基于现有结果，我们推导出所有困难CQ并集情形的特征描述，但包含成对体同构无环CQ的情况除外。

THEOREM 21. Let $Q$ be a UCQ of difficult ${CQ}$ s that does not contain two body-isomorphic acyclic CQs. Then, $Q \notin  {\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$ ,assuming MAT-MUL and HYPERCLIQUE.

定理21. 设$Q$为不包含两个体同构无环CQ的困难${CQ}$的UCQ。则在MAT-MUL和HYPERCLIQUE假设下，$Q \notin  {\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$。

Proof. Let ${Q}_{1}$ be a CQ in $Q$ given by Lemma 20. By definition,difficult CQs are self-join-free, and so if two CQs in $Q$ are body-homomorphically equivalent,they are also body-isomorphic. We treat the two possible cases of the structure of ${Q}_{1}$ . In case ${Q}_{1}$ is acyclic,since we know that $Q$ does not contain body-isomorphic acyclic $\mathrm{{CQs}}$ ,then,for all ${Q}_{i} \in  Q \smallsetminus  \left\{  {Q}_{1}\right\}$ ,there is no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{i}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . According to Lemma 18, $\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle  { \leq  }_{e}\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle$ . Since ${Q}_{1}$ is self-join-free acyclic non-free-connex,we have that $\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle   \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ assuming MAT-MUL. Therefore $\operatorname{ENUM}\langle Q\rangle$ is not in ${\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ either. In case ${Q}_{1}$ is cyclic,we use Lemma 19 to conclude that $\operatorname{DecIDE}\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle   \leq  \operatorname{DecIDE}\langle Q\rangle$ . According to Theorem 3,since ${Q}_{1}$ is self-join-free cyclic, DECIDE $\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle$ cannot be solved in linear time assuming HYPERCLIQUE. Therefore,DECIDE $\langle Q\rangle$ cannot be solved in linear time either,and $\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

证明。设${Q}_{1}$为引理20给出的$Q$中的CQ。根据定义，困难CQ是无自连接的，因此若$Q$中两个CQ存在体同态等价，则它们必然体同构。我们分两种情况讨论${Q}_{1}$的结构：当${Q}_{1}$为无环时，由于已知$Q$不包含体同构的无环$\mathrm{{CQs}}$，则对所有${Q}_{i} \in  Q \smallsetminus  \left\{  {Q}_{1}\right\}$，不存在从${Q}_{i}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态。根据引理18，$\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle  { \leq  }_{e}\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle$。由于${Q}_{1}$是无自连接、无环且非自由连接的，在MAT-MUL假设下可得$\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle   \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$，因此$\operatorname{ENUM}\langle Q\rangle$也不属于${\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。当${Q}_{1}$为循环时，运用引理19可推出$\operatorname{DecIDE}\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle   \leq  \operatorname{DecIDE}\langle Q\rangle$。根据定理3，由于${Q}_{1}$是无自连接的循环CQ，在HYPERCLIQUE假设下DECIDE$\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle$无法在线性时间内求解，故DECIDE$\langle Q\rangle$同样无法在线性时间内求解，且$\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

In the next example, we demonstrate how the reductions described in Lemma 19 and Theorem 3 combine in Theorem 21.

下例将展示引理19与定理3所述的归约方法如何在定理21中结合运用。

Example 22. Consider the UCQ $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2} \cup  {Q}_{3}$ with

示例22. 考虑包含$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2} \cup  {Q}_{3}$的UCQ：

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,u}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {x,u}\right) ,
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {y,v}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {v,x}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,x}\right) \text{,}
$$

$$
{Q}_{3}\left( {x,y}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,z}\right) .
$$

The queries ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are cyclic,and ${Q}_{3}$ is acyclic but not free-connex. This union is intractable according to Theorem 21. Note that ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are body-isomorphic,but there is no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{3}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . The proof of Theorem 3 states the following: If $\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle   \in$ Delay ${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$ ,then given an input graph $G$ ,we can use ${Q}_{1}$ to decide the existence of triangles in $G$ in time $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ ,in contradiction to HYPERCLIQUE. The same holds true for $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle$ . For every edge(u,v)in $G$ with $u < v$ we add $\left( {\left( {u,x}\right) ,\left( {v,y}\right) }\right)$ to ${R}_{1}^{I},\left( {\left( {u,y}\right) ,\left( {v,z}\right) }\right)$ to ${R}_{2}^{I}$ and $\left( {\left( {u,x}\right) ,\left( {v,z}\right) }\right)$ to ${R}_{3}^{I}$ . The query detects triangles: for every triangle $a,b,c$ in $G$ with $a < b < c$ ,the query ${Q}_{1}$ returns $\left( {\left( {a,x}\right) ,\left( {b,y}\right) }\right)$ . The union only returns answers corresponding to triangles:

查询${Q}_{1}$和${Q}_{2}$是循环的，而${Q}_{3}$是非循环但不满足自由连接。根据定理21，这种并集属于难解问题。注意${Q}_{1}$和${Q}_{2}$是体同构的，但不存在从${Q}_{3}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态。定理3的证明指出：若$\operatorname{EnuM}\left\langle  {Q}_{1}\right\rangle   \in$延迟${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$，则给定输入图$G$，我们可在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内利用${Q}_{1}$判定$G$中三角形存在性，这与HYPERCLIQUE假设矛盾。该结论对$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle$同样成立。对于$G$中每条满足$u < v$的边(u,v)，我们将$\left( {\left( {u,x}\right) ,\left( {v,y}\right) }\right)$添加至${R}_{1}^{I},\left( {\left( {u,y}\right) ,\left( {v,z}\right) }\right)$到${R}_{2}^{I}$，并将$\left( {\left( {u,x}\right) ,\left( {v,z}\right) }\right)$添加至${R}_{3}^{I}$。该查询可检测三角形：对于$G$中每个满足$a < b < c$的三角形$a,b,c$，查询${Q}_{1}$将返回$\left( {\left( {a,x}\right) ,\left( {b,y}\right) }\right)$。该并集仅返回与三角形对应的答案：

- For every answer $\left( {\left( {d,x}\right) ,\left( {e,y}\right) }\right)$ to ${Q}_{1}$ ,there exists some $f$ such that $d,e,f$ is a triangle in $G$ with $d < e < f$ .

- 对于${Q}_{1}$的每个答案$\left( {\left( {d,x}\right) ,\left( {e,y}\right) }\right)$，存在某个$f$使得$d,e,f$是$G$中满足$d < e < f$的三角形。

- For every answer $\left( {\left( {g,z}\right) ,\left( {h,x}\right) }\right)$ to ${Q}_{2}$ ,there exists some $i$ such that $g,h,i$ is a triangle in $G$ with $h < i < g$ .

- 对于${Q}_{2}$的每个答案$\left( {\left( {g,z}\right) ,\left( {h,x}\right) }\right)$，存在某个$i$使得$g,h,i$是$G$中满足$h < i < g$的三角形。

- The query ${Q}_{3}$ returns no answers over $I$ .

- 查询${Q}_{3}$在$I$上不返回任何答案。

Theorem 21 does not cover the case of a UCQ containing acyclic non-free-connex queries with isomorphic bodies. Since this requires a more intricate analysis, we first restrict ourselves to such unions of size two.

定理21未涵盖包含具有同构体的非自由连接非循环查询的UCQ情况。由于这需要更复杂的分析，我们首先将讨论限制在大小为二的此类并集上。

### 4.2 Unions of Two Body-Isomorphic CQs

### 4.2 两个体同构CQ的并集

Consider a set of body-isomorphic CQs. As all of them have the same structure, either every CQ in this set is cyclic, or every CQ is acyclic. In the case of a union of two cyclic CQs, the UCQ is intractable according to Theorem 21. So in this section, we discuss the union of body-isomorphic acyclic CQs. Note that, unlike the previous section, we allow a CQ in the union to be free-connex. We first introduce a new notation for body-isomorphic UCQs that we use hereafter in order to simplify the discussion on the query structure.

考虑一组体同构的CQ。由于它们具有相同结构，该集合中要么所有CQ都是循环的，要么都是非循环的。对于两个循环CQ的并集，根据定理21可知该UCQ是难解的。因此本节我们讨论体同构非循环CQ的并集。注意与前节不同，我们允许并集中的CQ满足自由连接。为简化查询结构讨论，我们首先为体同构UCQ引入新符号表示法。

Consider a UCQ of the form ${Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ ,where there exists a body-isomorphism $h$ from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . That is, the CQs have the structure:

考虑形如${Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$的UCQ，其中存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同构$h$。即这些CQ具有如下结构：

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{w}}_{1}\right) }\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{n}\left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{w}}_{n}\right) }\right) ,
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{w}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{n}\left( {\overrightarrow{w}}_{n}\right) .
$$

By applying ${h}^{-1}$ to the variables of ${Q}_{1}$ ,we can rewrite ${Q}_{1}$ as ${Q}_{1}\left( {{h}^{-1}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}\right) }\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{w}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{n}\left( {\overrightarrow{w}}_{n}\right)$ . Every mapping $\mu$ that is an answer to ${Q}_{1}$ in its new form can easily be converted to an answer to the original ${Q}_{1}$ by composing it with $h$ . So,for simplicity,we can assume that ${Q}_{1}$ is given in the modified form. Since now the two CQs have exactly the same body, we can treat the UCQ as a query with one body and two heads:

通过对${Q}_{1}$的变量应用${h}^{-1}$，我们可以将${Q}_{1}$重写为${Q}_{1}\left( {{h}^{-1}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}\right) }\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{w}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{n}\left( {\overrightarrow{w}}_{n}\right)$。每个作为新形式${Q}_{1}$答案的映射$\mu$，只需与$h$组合即可轻松转换为原${Q}_{1}$的答案。因此为简化起见，可假设${Q}_{1}$以修改后的形式给出。由于此时两个CQ具有完全相同的本体，可将该UCQ视为具有一个本体和两个头部的查询：

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {{h}^{-1}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}\right) }\right) ,{Q}_{2}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{w}}_{1}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{n}\left( {\overrightarrow{w}}_{n}\right) .
$$

We use this notation from now on for UCQs containing only body-isomorphic CQs. Note that, when treating a UCQ as one CQ with several heads,we can use the notation atoms(Q),as the atoms are the same for all CQs in the union,and the notation free $\left( {Q}_{i}\right)$ ,as the free variables may differ between different queries ${Q}_{i}$ in the union. With this notation at hand,we now inspect some examples of two body-isomorphic acyclic CQs.

下文对仅包含本体同构CQ的UCQ均采用此记法。需注意，当将UCQ视为具有多个头部的单个CQ时，可采用atoms(Q)记法（因为联合查询中所有CQ的原子相同），以及自由变量$\left( {Q}_{i}\right)$记法（因为不同查询${Q}_{i}$的自由变量可能不同）。基于此记法，现考察两个本体同构无环CQ的若干示例。

Example 23. Consider $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

示例23. 考虑具有以下形式的$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$：

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,v}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,v}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {v,w}\right) \text{and}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,v}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {w,v}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {v,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,z}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {z,x}\right) .
$$

Since ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are body-isomorphic, $Q$ can be rewritten as

由于${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$是本体同构的，$Q$可改写为：

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {w,y,z}\right) ,{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,v}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {w,v}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {v,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,z}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {z,x}\right) .
$$

In this case, we can use the same approach used for single CQs in Theorem 3, and show that this UCQ is not in Delay ${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$ assuming MAT-MUL. Let $A$ and $B$ be binary representations of Boolean $n \times  n$ matrices as explained in the beginning of this section. Define a database instance $I$ with ${R}_{1}^{I} = A,{R}_{2}^{I} = B,{R}_{3}^{I} = \{ 1,\ldots ,n\}  \times  \{  \bot  \}$ and ${R}_{4}^{I} = \{ \left( { \bot  , \bot  }\right) \} .{Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$ corresponds to the answers of ${AB}$ , and $\left| {{Q}_{2}\left( I\right) }\right|  = O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ . Assume by contradiction that $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ . Then,we find $Q\left( I\right)$ in $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ time. We can distinguish the answers of ${Q}_{1}$ from those of ${Q}_{2}$ since the possible assignments to the variables in different queries are from disjoint domains: given an answer $\mu  \in  Q\left( I\right)$ ,we have that $\mu  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$ if and only if $\mu \left( v\right)  =  \bot$ . This means we can solve matrix multiplication in time $\mathcal{O}\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ , which is a contradiction to MAT-MUL.

本例中可采用定理3中处理单个CQ的相同方法，在MAT-MUL假设下证明该UCQ不属于Delay${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$类。设$A$和$B$为本节开头所述的布尔$n \times  n$矩阵二进制表示，定义数据库实例$I$，其中${R}_{1}^{I} = A,{R}_{2}^{I} = B,{R}_{3}^{I} = \{ 1,\ldots ,n\}  \times  \{  \bot  \}$与${R}_{4}^{I} = \{ \left( { \bot  , \bot  }\right) \} .{Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$对应${AB}$的答案，且$\left| {{Q}_{2}\left( I\right) }\right|  = O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$。假设$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$不成立，则可在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内找到$Q\left( I\right)$。由于不同查询中变量的可能赋值来自不相交域，可区分${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$的答案：给定答案$\mu  \in  Q\left( I\right)$，当且仅当$\mu \left( v\right)  =  \bot$时$\mu  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$成立。这意味着我们能在$\mathcal{O}\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内完成矩阵乘法，这与MAT-MUL假设矛盾。

A union of two difficult body-isomorphic acyclic CQs may also be tractable. In fact, by adding a single variable to the heads in Example 23, we obtain a tractable UCQ.

两个困难的本体同构无环CQ的联合查询也可能是易处理的。实际上，通过在示例23的头部添加单个变量，即可获得易处理的UCQ。

Example 24. Let $Q$ be the UCQ

示例24. 设$Q$为如下UCQ：

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {w,y,x,z}\right) ,{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,w,v}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {w,v}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {v,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,z}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {z,x}\right) .
$$

<img src="https://cdn.noedgeai.com/019653fc-c0d4-76f6-8802-11c7426715d1_18.jpg?x=419&y=260&w=724&h=182&r=0"/>

Fig. 3. If ${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right) } \in  Q\left( I\right)$ ,the induced subgraph of $\mu \left( {w,x,y,z}\right)$ forms a clique where one edge might be missing.

图3. 若${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right) } \in  Q\left( I\right)$成立，则$\mu \left( {w,x,y,z}\right)$的诱导子图构成一个可能缺失一条边的团。

<!-- Media -->

Both CQs are acyclic non-free-connex. As ${Q}_{2}$ supplies the variables $\{ v,w,y\}$ and ${Q}_{1}$ supplies $\{ x,y,z\}$ ,both CQs have free-connex union extensions:

两个CQ（Conjunctive Queries，合取查询）均为无环非自由连接结构。由于${Q}_{2}$提供变量$\{ v,w,y\}$且${Q}_{1}$提供$\{ x,y,z\}$，这两个CQ都存在自由连接并集扩展：

$$
{Q}_{1}^{ + }\left( {w,y,x,z}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {w,v}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {v,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,z}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {z,x}\right) ,{P}_{1}\left( {v,w,y}\right) ,
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}^{ + }\left( {x,y,w,v}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {w,v}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {v,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,z}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {z,x}\right) ,{P}_{2}\left( {x,y,z}\right) \text{.}
$$

By Theorem 14,it follows that $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

根据定理14可得$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

Intuitively, the reason why the reduction of Example 23 fails in Example 24 is the fact that all the variables of the free-paths in one CQ, which are used to encode matrix multiplication, are free in the other CQ. Indeed,if we encode matrices $A$ and $B$ to the relations of the free path $w,v,y$ in ${Q}_{1}$ , there can be ${n}^{3}$ answers to ${Q}_{2}$ . The answer set in this case is too large to contradict the assumed lower bound for matrix multiplication. As it turns out, there are cases where we cannot reduce matrix multiplication to a union in this manner, and yet we can show that it is intractable using an alternative problem.

直观而言，例23的归约在例24中失效的原因在于：一个CQ中用于编码矩阵乘法的自由路径上的所有变量，在另一个CQ中都是自由变量。实际上，若将矩阵$A$和$B$编码到${Q}_{1}$中自由路径$w,v,y$的关系上，则${Q}_{2}$可能存在${n}^{3}$个解。这种情况下的解集规模过大，与假设的矩阵乘法下界相矛盾。事实证明，存在某些情况我们无法以此类方式将矩阵乘法归约为并集，但仍可通过替代问题证明其难解性。

Example 25. Let $Q$ be the UCQ

例25. 设$Q$为UCQ（Union of Conjunctive Queries，合取查询的并集）

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,u}\right) ,{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,z}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,u,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,u,z}\right) .
$$

This union is intractable under the 4-CLIQUE assumption. For a given graph $G = \left( {V,E}\right)$ with $\left| V\right|  = n$ ,we compute the set $T$ of all triangles in $G$ in time $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ . Define a database instance $I$ as ${R}_{1}^{I} = {R}_{2}^{I} = T$ . For every output ${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right) }$ in $Q\left( I\right)$ with $i \in  \{ 1,2\}$ ,we know that $\left( {\mu \left( x\right) ,\mu \left( u\right) ,\mu \left( z\right) }\right)$ and $\left( {\mu \left( y\right) ,\mu \left( u\right) ,\mu \left( z\right) }\right)$ are triangles. If $\mu \left( x\right)  \neq  \mu \left( y\right)$ ,this means that $\mu \left( \left( {x,y}\right) \right)  \in  E$ if and only if $\left( {\mu \left( x\right) ,\mu \left( y\right) ,\mu \left( u\right) ,\mu \left( z\right) }\right)$ forms a 4-clique (see Figure 3). Moreover,for every 4-clique(a,b,c,d)in the graph,we have $\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$ ,so this will detect all 4-cliques. Since there are $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ answers to $Q$ ,if $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ ,we can check whether $\mu \left( \left( {x,y}\right) \right)  \in  E$ for every answer in $Q\left( I\right)$ ,and determine whether a 4-clique appears in $G$ in time $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ .

在4-CLIQUE假设下，该并集具有不可解性。对于给定图$G = \left( {V,E}\right)$（含$\left| V\right|  = n$个顶点），我们可在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$时间内计算出$G$中所有三角形集合$T$。定义数据库实例$I$为${R}_{1}^{I} = {R}_{2}^{I} = T$。对于$Q\left( I\right)$中每个输出${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right) }$（满足$i \in  \{ 1,2\}$条件），可知$\left( {\mu \left( x\right) ,\mu \left( u\right) ,\mu \left( z\right) }\right)$与$\left( {\mu \left( y\right) ,\mu \left( u\right) ,\mu \left( z\right) }\right)$构成三角形。若$\mu \left( x\right)  \neq  \mu \left( y\right)$成立，则当且仅当$\left( {\mu \left( x\right) ,\mu \left( y\right) ,\mu \left( u\right) ,\mu \left( z\right) }\right)$形成4-团时$\mu \left( \left( {x,y}\right) \right)  \in  E$成立（见图3）。此外，图中每个4-团(a,b,c,d)都满足$\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$，故该方法可检测全部4-团。由于$Q$存在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$个解，若$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$成立，我们可检查$Q\left( I\right)$中每个解是否满足$\mu \left( \left( {x,y}\right) \right)  \in  E$，从而在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$时间内判定$G$中是否存在4-团。

Note that we can use the 4-CLIQUE assumption in Example 25, since, in addition to the free-path variables, there is another variable in both free-path relations. We now generalize the observations from the examples.

注意在示例25中可采用4-CLIQUE假设，因为除自由路径变量外，两个自由路径关系中还存在其他共有变量。下面我们将示例中的观察结果推广至一般情况。

Definition 26. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ be a UCQ where ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are body-isomorphic.

定义26. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$为UCQ查询，其中${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$具有同构查询体。

- ${Q}_{1}$ is said to be free-path guarded if for every free-path $P$ in ${Q}_{1}$ ,we have that $\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)  \subseteq$ free $\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ .

- 若UCQ${Q}_{1}$中每个自由路径$P$都满足$\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)  \subseteq$自由$\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$条件，则称其为自由路径防护型。

- Consider a path $P = \left( {{u}_{1},\ldots {u}_{k}}\right)$ in ${Q}_{1}$ . Two atoms ${R}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}\right)$ and ${R}_{2}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)$ of ${Q}_{1}$ are called subsequent $P$ -atoms if $\left\{  {{u}_{i - 1},{u}_{i}}\right\}   \subseteq  {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}$ and $\left\{  {{u}_{i},{u}_{i + 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}$ for some $1 < i < k$ .

- 考虑${Q}_{1}$中的路径$P = \left( {{u}_{1},\ldots {u}_{k}}\right)$。若存在$1 < i < k$使得$\left\{  {{u}_{i - 1},{u}_{i}}\right\}   \subseteq  {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}$且$\left\{  {{u}_{i},{u}_{i + 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}$，则称${Q}_{1}$的两个原子${R}_{1}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{1}\right)$与${R}_{2}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)$为连续$P$-原子。

- ${Q}_{1}$ is said to be bypass guarded if for every free-path $P$ in ${Q}_{1}$ and variable $u$ that appears in two subsequent $P$ -atoms,we have that $u \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ .

- 若UCQ${Q}_{1}$中每个自由路径$P$及其在两个连续$P$-原子中出现的变量$u$都满足$u \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$条件，则称其为旁路防护型。

Note that every free-connex CQ is trivially free-path guarded and bypass guarded.

注意所有自由连接CQ都天然满足自由路径防护和旁路防护条件。

Let us demonstrate the definitions on the recent examples. The CQ ${Q}_{1}$ of Example 24 is both free-path guarded and bypass guarded. The only free-path it contains is $P = \left( {w,v,y}\right)$ ,and ${Q}_{1}$ is free-path guarded since $\{ w,v,y\}  \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ . The only subsequent $P$ -atoms are ${R}_{1}\left( {w,v}\right)$ and ${R}_{2}\left( {v,y}\right)$ , and ${Q}_{1}$ is bypass guarded since $v \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ . The query ${Q}_{1}$ of Example 23 is not free-path guarded as the variables of the free-path ${P}^{\prime } = \left( {w,v,y}\right)$ of ${Q}_{1}$ are not contained in free $\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ . The query ${Q}_{1}$ of Example 25 is not bypass guarded. Consider the free-path ${P}^{\prime \prime } = \left( {x,z,y}\right)$ of ${Q}_{1}$ . The atoms ${R}_{1}\left( {x,u,z}\right)$ and ${R}_{2}\left( {y,u,z}\right)$ are subsequent ${P}^{\prime \prime }$ atoms. Since $u$ is contained in both atoms but not in free $\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ , we have that ${Q}_{1}$ is not bypass guarded.

让我们通过近期实例来阐释这些定义。例24中的CQ ${Q}_{1}$ 同时满足自由路径防护和旁路防护。其包含的唯一自由路径是$P = \left( {w,v,y}\right)$，且${Q}_{1}$因$\{ w,v,y\}  \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$而具有自由路径防护性。仅有的后续$P$原子是${R}_{1}\left( {w,v}\right)$和${R}_{2}\left( {v,y}\right)$，而${Q}_{1}$因$v \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$实现旁路防护。例23中的查询${Q}_{1}$不满足自由路径防护，因其自由路径${P}^{\prime } = \left( {w,v,y}\right)$的变量未被包含在自由$\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$中。例25的查询${Q}_{1}$则不具备旁路防护性，考察其自由路径${P}^{\prime \prime } = \left( {x,z,y}\right)$时可见：原子${R}_{1}\left( {x,u,z}\right)$与${R}_{2}\left( {y,u,z}\right)$是后续${P}^{\prime \prime }$原子，由于$u$同时存在于这两个原子却未出现在自由$\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$中，故${Q}_{1}$不满足旁路防护。

In the following two lemmas, we prove that if some CQ in a union is either not free-path guarded or bypass guarded, then the UCQ is intractable. As in the characterization for CQs in Theorem 3, our lower bounds apply only when the CQs are self-join-free; with this restriction, we can assign different atoms with different relations. The next lemma shows that the reduction in Example 23, where we can use the fact that ${Q}_{1}$ is not free-path guarded to compute matrix multiplication,can be constructed in the general case as well.

在以下两个引理中，我们将证明：若并集中某个CQ不满足自由路径防护或旁路防护，则该UCQ具有计算不可处理性。如定理3中CQ的特征描述所示，我们的下界约束仅适用于无自连接的CQ；在此限制下，可为不同原子分配相异关系。下一个引理表明，例23中利用${Q}_{1}$非自由路径防护性进行矩阵乘法计算的归约方法，在普遍情况下同样适用。

LEMMA 27. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ be a UCQ of self-join-free body-isomorphic acyclic CQs. If ${Q}_{1}$ is not free-path guarded,then $\operatorname{ENUM}\langle Q\rangle$ is not in ${\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$ ,assuming MAT-MUL.

引理27. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$为由无自连接、体同构的非循环CQ组成的UCQ。若${Q}_{1}$不满足自由路径防护，则在假设MAT-MUL成立的前提下，$\operatorname{ENUM}\langle Q\rangle$不属于${\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$。

Proof. Let $A$ and $B$ be Boolean $n \times  n$ matrices represented as binary relations,i.e., $A \subseteq$ $\{ 1,\ldots ,n{\} }^{2}$ ,where $\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A$ means that the entry in the $a$ th row and $b$ th column is 1 . Further, let $P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$ be a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ that is not guarded,and let $i$ be the minimal index such that ${z}_{i} \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ . We assign the path variables to three roles as follows: If $0 < i < k + 1$ , we define ${V}_{a} = \left\{  {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{i - 1}}\right\}  ,{V}_{b} = \left\{  {z}_{i}\right\}$ and ${V}_{c} = \left\{  {{z}_{i + 1}\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}$ . Otherwise (if $i$ is 0 or $k + 1$ ),we define ${V}_{a} = \left\{  {z}_{0}\right\}  ,{V}_{b} = \left\{  {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k}}\right\}$ and ${V}_{c} = \left\{  {z}_{k + 1}\right\}$ . Note that,in both cases,there exists some $\alpha  \in  \{ a,b,c\}$ such that ${V}_{\alpha } \cap  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  = \varnothing$ . Intuitively,that means that some role is not guarded. Note also that since $P$ is chordless and $k \geq  1$ ,there is no atom in $Q$ that contains both a variable from ${V}_{a}$ and a variable from ${V}_{c}$ . Thus,we can partition the atoms into nonempty sets ${\mathcal{R}}_{A} = \left\{  {R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)  \mid  {V}_{c} \cap  \overrightarrow{v} = \varnothing }\right\}$ and ${\mathcal{R}}_{B} = \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)  \smallsetminus  {\mathcal{R}}_{A}$ ,and we have that the atoms of ${\mathcal{R}}_{A}$ do not contain variables of ${V}_{c}$ ,and the atoms of ${\mathcal{R}}_{B}$ do not contain variables of ${V}_{a}$ .

证明。设$A$和$B$为表示为二元关系的布尔$n \times  n$矩阵，即$A \subseteq$$\{ 1,\ldots ,n{\} }^{2}$，其中$\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A$表示第$a$行第$b$列的元素为1。进一步，设$P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$为${Q}_{1}$中未被守卫的自由路径，并设$i$为满足${z}_{i} \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$的最小索引。我们将路径变量分配给三个角色如下：若$0 < i < k + 1$，则定义${V}_{a} = \left\{  {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{i - 1}}\right\}  ,{V}_{b} = \left\{  {z}_{i}\right\}$和${V}_{c} = \left\{  {{z}_{i + 1}\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}$；否则（当$i$为0或$k + 1$时），定义${V}_{a} = \left\{  {z}_{0}\right\}  ,{V}_{b} = \left\{  {{z}_{1},\ldots ,{z}_{k}}\right\}$和${V}_{c} = \left\{  {z}_{k + 1}\right\}$。注意在两种情况下，都存在某个$\alpha  \in  \{ a,b,c\}$使得${V}_{\alpha } \cap  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  = \varnothing$。直观上，这意味着某个角色未被守卫。还需注意，由于$P$是无弦的且$k \geq  1$，$Q$中不存在同时包含${V}_{a}$变量和${V}_{c}$变量的原子。因此，我们可以将原子划分为非空集合${\mathcal{R}}_{A} = \left\{  {R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)  \mid  {V}_{c} \cap  \overrightarrow{v} = \varnothing }\right\}$和${\mathcal{R}}_{B} = \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)  \smallsetminus  {\mathcal{R}}_{A}$，且${\mathcal{R}}_{A}$的原子不包含${V}_{c}$的变量，${\mathcal{R}}_{B}$的原子不包含${V}_{a}$的变量。

Given three values(a,b,c),we define a function ${\tau }_{\left( a,b,c\right) } : \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \rightarrow  \{ a,b,c, \bot  \}$ that assigns every variable with the value corresponding to its role:
