给定三元组(a,b,c)，我们定义函数${\tau }_{\left( a,b,c\right) } : \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \rightarrow  \{ a,b,c, \bot  \}$，该函数根据变量的角色分配对应值：

$$
{\tau }_{\left( a,b,c\right) }\left( v\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{ll} a & \text{ if }v \in  {V}_{a}, \\  b & \text{ if }v \in  {V}_{b}, \\  c & \text{ if }v \in  {V}_{c}, \\   \bot  & \text{ otherwise,} \end{array}\right. 
$$

For a vector $\overrightarrow{v}$ ,we denote by ${\tau }_{\left( a,b,c\right) }\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ the vector obtained by element-wise application of ${\tau }_{\left( a,b,c\right) }$ . We define a database instance $I$ over $Q$ as follows: For every atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ ,if $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  {\mathcal{R}}_{A}$ we set ${R}^{I} = \left\{  {{\tau }_{\left( a,b, \bot  \right) }\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \mid  \left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A}\right\}$ ,and if $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  {\mathcal{R}}_{B}$ we set ${R}^{I} = \left\{  {{\tau }_{\left(  \bot  ,b,c\right) }\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \mid  \left( {b,c}\right)  \in  B}\right\}$ . Note that every relation is defined only once since ${\mathcal{R}}_{A}$ and ${\mathcal{R}}_{B}$ are disjoint and $Q$ is self-join-free.

对于一个向量$\overrightarrow{v}$，我们用${\tau }_{\left( a,b,c\right) }\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$表示通过逐元素应用${\tau }_{\left( a,b,c\right) }$得到的向量。我们按如下方式定义在$Q$上的数据库实例$I$：对于每个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$，若$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  {\mathcal{R}}_{A}$则设定${R}^{I} = \left\{  {{\tau }_{\left( a,b, \bot  \right) }\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \mid  \left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A}\right\}$，若$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  {\mathcal{R}}_{B}$则设定${R}^{I} = \left\{  {{\tau }_{\left(  \bot  ,b,c\right) }\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \mid  \left( {b,c}\right)  \in  B}\right\}$。请注意，由于${\mathcal{R}}_{A}$与${\mathcal{R}}_{B}$互不相交且$Q$无自连接，每个关系仅被定义一次。

Consider an answer $\mu  \in  Q\left( I\right)$ . In the case that $0 < i < k + 1$ ,we have that $\mu \left( {z}_{0}\right)  = \cdots  = \mu \left( {z}_{i - 1}\right)  =$ $a,\mu \left( {z}_{i}\right)  = b$ and $\mu \left( {z}_{i + 1}\right)  = \cdots  = \mu \left( {z}_{k + 1}\right)  = c$ for some $\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A$ and $\left( {b,c}\right)  \in  B$ ,and in case that $i \in  \{ 0,k + 1\}$ ,we have that $\mu \left( {z}_{0}\right)  = a,\mu \left( {z}_{1}\right)  = \cdots  = \mu \left( {z}_{k}\right)  = b$ and $\mu \left( {z}_{k + 1}\right)  = c$ for some $\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A$ and $\left( {b,c}\right)  \in  B$ . This is since the variables ${z}_{i}$ are connected via the path in both CQs. In either case, $\mu \left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) }\right)$ is a tuple only containing the values $a,c$ ,and $\bot$ . So the answers to ${Q}_{1}$ represent the answer to the matrix multiplication task we started with. We now need to verify that the answers to ${Q}_{2}$ do not interfere with the reduction. If $0 < i < k + 1,\mu \left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) }\right)$ is a tuple only containing the values $\{ a,c, \bot  \}$ ; if $i = 0$ ,it only contains $\{ b,c, \bot  \}$ ; and if $i = k + 1$ ,it only contains $\{ a,b, \bot  \}$ . Thus,the number of answers to $Q$ is at most of size $2{n}^{2}$ . Assume by contradiction that we can enumerate the solutions of $Q\left( I\right)$ with linear preprocessing and constant delay. To distinguish the answers of ${Q}_{1}$ from those of ${Q}_{2}$ ,we can concatenate the variable names to the values,as described in Lemma 16. That way,we can ignore the answers that correspond to the values(a,b)or(b,c), and use the(a,c)pairs as the answers to matrix multiplication. This solves matrix multiplication in $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ time,in contradiction to MAT-MUL.

考虑一个答案$\mu  \in  Q\left( I\right)$。当$0 < i < k + 1$时，对于某些$\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A$和$\left( {b,c}\right)  \in  B$，我们有$\mu \left( {z}_{0}\right)  = \cdots  = \mu \left( {z}_{i - 1}\right)  =$$a,\mu \left( {z}_{i}\right)  = b$和$\mu \left( {z}_{i + 1}\right)  = \cdots  = \mu \left( {z}_{k + 1}\right)  = c$；而当$i \in  \{ 0,k + 1\}$时，对于某些$\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A$和$\left( {b,c}\right)  \in  B$，我们有$\mu \left( {z}_{0}\right)  = a,\mu \left( {z}_{1}\right)  = \cdots  = \mu \left( {z}_{k}\right)  = b$和$\mu \left( {z}_{k + 1}\right)  = c$。这是因为变量${z}_{i}$通过两个CQ中的路径相连。无论哪种情况，$\mu \left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) }\right)$都是仅包含值$a,c$和$\bot$的元组。因此，${Q}_{1}$的答案即代表我们最初矩阵乘法任务的解。现在需要验证${Q}_{2}$的答案不会干扰归约过程。若$0 < i < k + 1,\mu \left( {\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) }\right)$是仅含$\{ a,c, \bot  \}$的元组；若$i = 0$则仅含$\{ b,c, \bot  \}$；若$i = k + 1$则仅含$\{ a,b, \bot  \}$。因此，$Q$的答案数量最多为$2{n}^{2}$。假设反证法下，我们能用线性预处理和常数延迟枚举$Q\left( I\right)$的解。为区分${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$的答案，可按引理16所述将变量名与值拼接。如此可忽略对应值(a,b)或(b,c)的答案，而将(a,c)对作为矩阵乘法的解。这将在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内解决矩阵乘法，与MAT-MUL假设矛盾。

In Example 25,we encounter a UCQ where both CQs are free-path guarded,but ${Q}_{1}$ is not bypass guarded. We can encode 4-CLIQUE in every UCQ with this property.

在示例25中，我们遇到一个UCQ，其中两个CQ都是自由路径防护的，但${Q}_{1}$未被旁路防护。具有此性质的每个UCQ都可编码4-CLIQUE问题。

LEMMA 28. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ be a UCQ of self-join-free body-isomorphic acyclic CQs. If ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are free-path guarded and ${Q}_{1}$ is not bypass guarded,then $\operatorname{ENUM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ ,assuming 4-CLIQUE.

引理28. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$为无自连接且体同构的无环CQ组成的UCQ。若${Q}_{1}$和${Q}_{2}$是自由路径防护的且${Q}_{1}$未被旁路防护，则在4-CLIQUE假设下，$\operatorname{ENUM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$成立。

Proof. Let $G = \left( {V,E}\right)$ be a graph with $\left| V\right|  = n$ . We show how to solve the 4-clique problem on $G$ in time $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ if $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle$ is in ${\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ . Let $P$ be a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ and let $u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ such that $u$ appears in two subsequent P-atoms.

证明。设$G = \left( {V,E}\right)$为一个具有$\left| V\right|  = n$的图。若$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle$属于${\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$，我们将展示如何在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$时间内求解$G$上的4-团问题。令$P$为${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径，且存在$u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$使得$u$出现在两个连续的P-原子中。

We first claim that,under the conditions of this lemma, $P$ is of length 2 . Let $P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$ and $1 \leq  i \leq  k$ such that $\left\{  {u,{z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i}}\right\}$ and $\left\{  {u,{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1}}\right\}$ are contained in edges of $\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$ . As $P$ is chordless, there is no edge containing $\left\{  {{z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i + 1}}\right\}$ ,thus the path $\left( {{z}_{i - 1},u,{z}_{i + 1}}\right)$ is a chordless path. As ${Q}_{1}$ is free-path guarded, ${z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i + 1} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ and since $u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ ,this is a free-path of ${Q}_{2}$ . Since ${Q}_{2}$ is free-path guarded,we have that ${z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i + 1} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ . Since $P$ is a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ ,the only variables of $P$ that are free in ${Q}_{1}$ appear in the edges of the path,and so $i = k = 1$ ,and $P$ is of the form $\left( {{z}_{0},{z}_{1},{z}_{2}}\right)$ . Therefore,there exist atoms ${R}_{1}$ and ${R}_{2}$ with $\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{1},u}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$ and $\left\{  {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},u}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( {R}_{2}\right)$ .

我们首先断言：在本引理条件下，$P$的长度为2。设$P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$和$1 \leq  i \leq  k$使得$\left\{  {u,{z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i}}\right\}$与$\left\{  {u,{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1}}\right\}$包含于$\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$的边中。由于$P$无弦，故不存在包含$\left\{  {{z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i + 1}}\right\}$的边，因此路径$\left( {{z}_{i - 1},u,{z}_{i + 1}}\right)$为无弦路径。因${Q}_{1}$是自由路径防护的，且$u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$，故${z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i + 1} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$构成${Q}_{2}$的自由路径。根据${Q}_{2}$的自由路径防护性质，可得${z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i + 1} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$。由于$P$是${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径，其自由变量仅出现在路径边中，因此$i = k = 1$，且$P$具有$\left( {{z}_{0},{z}_{1},{z}_{2}}\right)$的形式。故存在原子${R}_{1}$和${R}_{2}$分别满足$\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{1},u}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( {R}_{1}\right)$与$\left\{  {{z}_{1},{z}_{2},u}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( {R}_{2}\right)$。

Given three values(a,b,c),we define a function ${\tau }_{\left( a,b,c\right) } : \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \rightarrow  \{ a,b,c\}$ as follows:

给定三元组(a,b,c)，我们如下定义函数${\tau }_{\left( a,b,c\right) } : \operatorname{var}\left( Q\right)  \rightarrow  \{ a,b,c\}$：

$$
{\tau }_{a,b,c}\left( v\right)  = \left\{  \begin{array}{ll} a & \text{ if }v \in  \left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{2}}\right\}  , \\  b & \text{ if }v = {z}_{1}, \\  c & \text{ if }v = u, \\   \bot  & \text{ otherwise. } \end{array}\right. 
$$

For every atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)$ ,we define ${R}^{I} = \left\{  {{\tau }_{a,b,c}\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \mid  \left( {a,b,c}\right) \text{ a triangle in }G}\right\}$ . Every relation is defined only once since $Q$ is self-join-free. Note that $\left| {R}^{I}\right|  \in  \mathcal{O}\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ ,as there are at most ${n}^{3}$ triangles in $G$ ,and that we can construct $I$ with $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ time.

对每个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)$，我们定义${R}^{I} = \left\{  {{\tau }_{a,b,c}\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \mid  \left( {a,b,c}\right) \text{ a triangle in }G}\right\}$。由于$Q$无自连接，每个关系仅定义一次。注意$\left| {R}^{I}\right|  \in  \mathcal{O}\left( {n}^{3}\right)$，因$G$中至多存在${n}^{3}$个三角形，且我们可在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$时间内构造$I$。

Consider an answer ${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$ . Since ${R}_{1}$ and ${R}_{2}$ are atoms in ${Q}_{1}$ ,we are guaranteed that $\left( {\mu \left( {z}_{0}\right) ,\mu \left( {z}_{1}\right) ,\mu \left( u\right) }\right)$ and $\left( {\mu \left( {z}_{1}\right) ,\mu \left( {z}_{2}\right) ,\mu \left( u\right) }\right)$ form triangles in $G$ . Therefore,the graph contains a 4- clique if and only if there is an edge $\left( {\mu \left( {z}_{0}\right) ,\mu \left( {z}_{2}\right) }\right)$ . As ${z}_{0},{z}_{2} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ ,it suffices to check every ${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$ for this property. Since ${Q}_{1}$ is free-path guarded,we know that ${z}_{1}$ is existential in ${Q}_{1}$ but free in ${Q}_{2}$ . This means that free $\left( {Q}_{1}\right)  \neq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ and so there is no homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . We can therefore use the mappings from Lemma 16 in order to distinguish the answers of ${Q}_{1}$ from those of ${Q}_{2}$ . We have that $\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{1},{z}_{2},u}\right\}$ is neither contained in free $\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ nor in $\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ . Thus, $\left| {Q\left( I\right) }\right|  \in  \mathcal{O}\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ . If $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle$ is in ${\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$ ,we can construct the database instance,compute $Q$ ,and check every answer to ${Q}_{1}$ for an edge of the form $\left( {\mu \left( {z}_{0}\right) ,\mu \left( {z}_{2}\right) }\right)$ in total time $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ ,which contradicts 4-CLIQUE.

考虑一个答案${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$。由于${R}_{1}$和${R}_{2}$是${Q}_{1}$中的原子，我们确保$\left( {\mu \left( {z}_{0}\right) ,\mu \left( {z}_{1}\right) ,\mu \left( u\right) }\right)$和$\left( {\mu \left( {z}_{1}\right) ,\mu \left( {z}_{2}\right) ,\mu \left( u\right) }\right)$在$G$中形成三角形。因此，当且仅当存在边$\left( {\mu \left( {z}_{0}\right) ,\mu \left( {z}_{2}\right) }\right)$时，该图包含一个4-团。鉴于${z}_{0},{z}_{2} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$，只需检查每个${\left. \mu \right| }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) } \in  {Q}_{1}\left( I\right)$是否满足此性质。由于${Q}_{1}$是自由路径保护的，我们知道${z}_{1}$在${Q}_{1}$中存在但在${Q}_{2}$中自由。这意味着自由的$\left( {Q}_{1}\right)  \neq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$，因此不存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的同态映射。因此，我们可以利用引理16中的映射来区分${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$的答案。已知$\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{1},{z}_{2},u}\right\}$既不包含在自由的$\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$中，也不在$\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$中。故而，$\left| {Q\left( I\right) }\right|  \in  \mathcal{O}\left( {n}^{3}\right)$。若$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle$位于${\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$中，我们可构建数据库实例，计算$Q$，并在总时间$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$内检查${Q}_{1}$的每个答案是否具有形式为$\left( {\mu \left( {z}_{0}\right) ,\mu \left( {z}_{2}\right) }\right)$的边，这与4-团假设矛盾。

In the next section, we show that Lemma 27 and Lemma 28 cover all intractable cases of the UCQs discussed in this section.

下一节我们将证明，引理27和引理28涵盖了本节讨论的所有UCQ难解情形。

### 4.3 Complete Classification of Fragments of UCQs

### 4.3 UCQ片段的完全分类

We show next that the hardness proofs given above complete our tractability results into a dichotomy for the fragments of UCQs we examined. We first establish this for unions of two body-isomorphic acyclic CQs, and then conclude the same for unions of two intractable CQs.

接下来我们将证明，上述硬度证明将我们的可处理性结果完善为所考察UCQ片段的二分法。我们首先针对两个体同构的无环CQ的并集建立这一结论，继而推导出两个难解CQ并集的相同结论。

To prove that any union of two body-isomorphic acyclic CQs that is not covered by Lemma 27 and Lemma 28 has a free-connex union extension, we need some observations regarding the place of appearance of relevant variables in join-trees. Recall that we call $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}}\right)$ a path of variables in a query $Q$ if,for all $1 \leq  i < n$ ,there exists an atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{{u}_{i}}\right)$ in $Q$ such that $\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{i + 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  \overrightarrow{v}$ .

为证明任何未被引理27和28覆盖的两个体同构无环CQ的并集都具有自由连接并扩展，我们需要观察相关变量在连接树中的出现位置。回忆中，若对所有$1 \leq  i < n$都存在$Q$中的原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{{u}_{i}}\right)$使得$\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{i + 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  \overrightarrow{v}$，则称$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}}\right)$为查询$Q$中的变量路径。

Proposition 29. Let $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}}\right)$ be a path of variables in an acyclic query $Q$ ,and let ${A}_{s}$ and ${A}_{t}$ be atoms containing $\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$ and $\left\{  {{v}_{n - 1},{v}_{n}}\right\}$ ,respectively. For all $1 \leq  i < n$ ,the simple path between ${A}_{s}$ and ${A}_{t}$ on a join-tree of $Q$ contains an atom ${R}_{i}\left( \overrightarrow{{u}_{i}}\right)$ such that $\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{i + 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  \overrightarrow{{u}_{i}}$ .

命题29. 设$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}}\right)$为无环查询$Q$中的变量路径，${A}_{s}$和${A}_{t}$分别为包含$\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$和$\left\{  {{v}_{n - 1},{v}_{n}}\right\}$的原子。对于所有$1 \leq  i < n$，在$Q$的连接树上，${A}_{s}$与${A}_{t}$之间的简单路径必包含一个满足$\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{i + 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  \overrightarrow{{u}_{i}}$的原子${R}_{i}\left( \overrightarrow{{u}_{i}}\right)$。

Proof. We prove this by induction on $n$ . If $n = 3$ ,this trivially holds as the endpoints, ${A}_{s}$ and ${A}_{t}$ , contain the required variables. We now assume this proposition holds for paths of length $n - 1$ and show that it also holds for paths of length $n$ where $n \geq  4$ . Consider the simple path between ${A}_{s}$ and a node containing $\left\{  {{v}_{n - 2},{v}_{n - 1}}\right\}$ . Let ${A}_{m}$ be the first node on that path that contains $\left\{  {{v}_{n - 2},{v}_{n - 1}}\right\}$ . Denote by ${P}_{s}$ the simple path between ${A}_{s}$ and ${A}_{m}$ . Note that ${A}_{m}$ is the only node on ${P}_{s}$ that contains $\left\{  {{v}_{n - 2},{v}_{n - 1}}\right\}$ . By the induction assumption, ${P}_{s}$ contains $\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{i + 1}}\right\}$ for all $1 \leq  i < n - 1$ ,and in particular it contains $\left\{  {{v}_{n - 3},{v}_{n - 2}}\right\}$ . Denote by ${P}_{t}$ the simple path between ${A}_{m}$ and ${A}_{t}$ ,and denote by $P$ the concatenation of ${P}_{s}$ and ${P}_{t}$ . The path $P$ goes between ${A}_{s}$ and ${A}_{t}$ and contains $\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{i + 1}}\right\}$ for all $1 \leq  i < n$ . We claim that $P$ is a simple path. Assume by contradiction that $P$ is not simple. This means that there is a node ${A}_{v}$ that ${P}_{s}$ and ${P}_{t}$ share other than ${A}_{m}$ . Due to the running intersection property,since ${A}_{v}$ is on the simple path between ${A}_{m}$ and ${A}_{t},{A}_{v}$ contains ${v}_{n - 1}$ . Denote by ${A}_{m - 1}$ the node preceding ${A}_{m}$ on ${P}_{s}$ . Due to the running intersection property,since ${A}_{m - 1}$ is on the simple path between ${A}_{v}$ and ${A}_{m},{A}_{m - 1}$ contains ${v}_{n - 1}$ too. Since a node containing $\left\{  {{v}_{n - 3},{v}_{n - 2}}\right\}$ is on ${P}_{s}$ , and since ${A}_{m - 1}$ is on the simple path between this node and ${A}_{m}$ ,we have that ${A}_{m - 1}$ contains ${v}_{n - 2}$ . Overall,we have that ${A}_{m - 1}$ contains $\left\{  {{v}_{n - 2},{v}_{n - 1}}\right\}$ ,which is a contradiction to the fact that ${A}_{m}$ is the only such node on ${P}_{s}$ .

证明。我们通过对$n$进行归纳来证明这一点。若$n = 3$，根据端点${A}_{s}$与${A}_{t}$已包含所需变量，命题显然成立。现假设该命题对长度为$n - 1$的路径成立，需证其对长度为$n$的路径（其中$n \geq  4$）亦成立。考虑${A}_{s}$与含$\left\{  {{v}_{n - 2},{v}_{n - 1}}\right\}$节点间的最短路径，设${A}_{m}$为该路径上首个含$\left\{  {{v}_{n - 2},{v}_{n - 1}}\right\}$的节点。记${P}_{s}$为${A}_{s}$与${A}_{m}$间的最短路径，注意${A}_{m}$是${P}_{s}$上唯一含$\left\{  {{v}_{n - 2},{v}_{n - 1}}\right\}$的节点。根据归纳假设，${P}_{s}$包含所有$1 \leq  i < n - 1$对应的$\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{i + 1}}\right\}$，特别地包含$\left\{  {{v}_{n - 3},{v}_{n - 2}}\right\}$。记${P}_{t}$为${A}_{m}$与${A}_{t}$间的最短路径，$P$为${P}_{s}$与${P}_{t}$的串联路径。$P$连接${A}_{s}$与${A}_{t}$且包含所有$1 \leq  i < n$对应的$\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{i + 1}}\right\}$。我们断言$P$为简单路径。反设$P$非简单，则存在节点${A}_{v}$同时位于${P}_{s}$与${P}_{t}$上且异于${A}_{m}$。根据运行相交性，因${A}_{v}$位于${A}_{m}$与${A}_{t},{A}_{v}$间的最短路径上，故含${v}_{n - 1}$。设${A}_{m - 1}$为${P}_{s}$上${A}_{m}$的前驱节点，同理因${A}_{m - 1}$位于${A}_{v}$与${A}_{m},{A}_{m - 1}$间的最短路径上，亦含${v}_{n - 1}$。由于含$\left\{  {{v}_{n - 3},{v}_{n - 2}}\right\}$的节点在${P}_{s}$上，且${A}_{m - 1}$位于该节点与${A}_{m}$的最短路径上，故${A}_{m - 1}$含${v}_{n - 2}$。综上，${A}_{m - 1}$含$\left\{  {{v}_{n - 2},{v}_{n - 1}}\right\}$，这与${A}_{m}$是${P}_{s}$上唯一含$\left\{  {{v}_{n - 2},{v}_{n - 1}}\right\}$节点的性质矛盾。

Proposition 30. Let $\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}}\right)$ be a path of variables in an acyclic query $Q$ ,and let ${A}_{s}$ and ${A}_{t}$ be atoms containing $\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$ and $\left\{  {{v}_{n - 1},{v}_{n}}\right\}$ ,respectively. If ${A}_{1}$ and ${A}_{2}$ are two subsequent nodes on the simple path $P$ between ${A}_{s}$ and ${A}_{t}$ on a join-tree of $Q$ ,then there exists some $1 \leq  i \leq  n$ such that ${v}_{i} \in  {A}_{1} \cap  {A}_{2}$ .

命题30. 设$\left( {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{n}}\right)$为无环查询$Q$中的变量路径，${A}_{s}$和${A}_{t}$分别为包含$\left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$和$\left\{  {{v}_{n - 1},{v}_{n}}\right\}$的原子。若${A}_{1}$与${A}_{2}$是$Q$连接树中${A}_{s}$与${A}_{t}$间简单路径$P$上的连续节点，则存在某个$1 \leq  i \leq  n$使得${v}_{i} \in  {A}_{1} \cap  {A}_{2}$。

Proof. Denote the subpath of $P$ between ${A}_{s}$ and ${A}_{1}$ by ${P}_{s}$ ,and the subpath between ${A}_{2}$ and ${A}_{t}$ by ${P}_{t}$ . Denote by $i$ the maximal index such that ${v}_{i} \in  \operatorname{var}\left( {P}_{s}\right)$ . If $i = n$ ,note that an atom in ${P}_{s}$ and an atom in ${P}_{t}$ both contain ${v}_{i}$ . Otherwise, $i < n$ . According to Proposition 29 and since all atoms of $P$ appear in either ${P}_{s}$ or ${P}_{t}$ ,an atom containing the variables $\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{i + 1}}\right\}$ must appear in ${P}_{s}$ or ${P}_{t}$ . It does not appear in ${P}_{s}$ because of the maximality of $i$ ,so it appears in ${P}_{t}$ . In this case too,an atom in ${P}_{s}$ and an atom in ${P}_{t}$ both contain ${v}_{i}$ . Since ${A}_{1}$ and ${A}_{2}$ are on the path between these atoms,due to the running intersection property, ${v}_{i} \in  {A}_{1} \cap  {A}_{2}$ .

证明. 记$P$在${A}_{s}$与${A}_{1}$间的子路径为${P}_{s}$，${A}_{2}$与${A}_{t}$间的子路径为${P}_{t}$。令$i$为满足${v}_{i} \in  \operatorname{var}\left( {P}_{s}\right)$的最大索引。若$i = n$，注意到${P}_{s}$中的原子与${P}_{t}$中的原子均包含${v}_{i}$。否则$i < n$。根据命题29且因$P$的所有原子均出现在${P}_{s}$或${P}_{t}$中，包含变量$\left\{  {{v}_{i},{v}_{i + 1}}\right\}$的原子必存在于${P}_{s}$或${P}_{t}$。由于$i$的极大性该原子不在${P}_{s}$中，故出现于${P}_{t}$。此时${P}_{s}$中的原子与${P}_{t}$中的原子仍共同包含${v}_{i}$。因${A}_{1}$与${A}_{2}$位于这些原子间的路径上，根据运行相交性质可得${v}_{i} \in  {A}_{1} \cap  {A}_{2}$。

Using this proposition, we can prove the structural property that we need.

运用该命题，可证得所需的结构性质。

LEMMA 31. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ be a UCQ of body-isomorphic acyclic CQs,where ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are free-path guarded,and ${Q}_{1}$ is bypass guarded,and let $P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$ be a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ . There exists a join-tree $T$ for $Q$ with a subtree ${T}_{P}$ such that:

引理31. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$为体同构无环CQ的UCQ，其中${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$是自由路径防护的，${Q}_{1}$是旁路防护的，且$P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$为${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径。存在$Q$的连接树$T$及其子树${T}_{P}$满足：

- $\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( {T}_{P}\right)$ .

- For every variable $u$ that appears in two different atoms of ${T}_{P}$ :

- 对于出现在${T}_{P}$两个不同原子中的每个变量$u$：

$- u \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ .

-There is an atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ in ${T}_{P}$ such that $u,{z}_{i} \in  \overrightarrow{v}$ for some $0 < i < k + 1$ .

-存在${T}_{P}$中的原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$使得对某个$0 < i < k + 1$有$u,{z}_{i} \in  \overrightarrow{v}$

Proof. Recall that according to our notation, we assume that body-isomorphic CQs have exactly the same body. Thus, $T$ is also a join-tree for ${Q}_{2}$ . In the following,we refer to the body of $Q$ when we make statements that apply to the bodies of both ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ .

证明. 根据我们的记法约定，体同构CQ具有完全相同的体。因此$T$也是${Q}_{2}$的连接树。下文所述适用于${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$两者之体的陈述，均指$Q$的体。

Consider a path ${P}_{A} = \left( {{A}_{1},\ldots ,{A}_{s}}\right)$ between two atoms on a join tree. We define a contraction step for a path of length 2 or more: if there exists $j$ such that ${A}_{j} \cap  {A}_{j + 1} \subseteq  {A}_{1} \cap  {A}_{s}$ ,then remove the edge $\left( {{A}_{j},{A}_{j + 1}}\right)$ and add the edge $\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{s}}\right)$ . A path on a join-tree is said to be fully contracted if none of its subpaths can be contracted. Given any two atoms on a join-tree, it is possible to fully contract the path between them by performing any arbitrary sequence of contraction steps until it is no longer possible: the process will end as every contraction step reduces the length of the path.

考虑连接树(join tree)上两个原子间的路径${P}_{A} = \left( {{A}_{1},\ldots ,{A}_{s}}\right)$。我们定义长度≥2路径的收缩步骤：若存在$j$使得${A}_{j} \cap  {A}_{j + 1} \subseteq  {A}_{1} \cap  {A}_{s}$，则移除边$\left( {{A}_{j},{A}_{j + 1}}\right)$并添加边$\left( {{A}_{1},{A}_{s}}\right)$。当连接树上的路径无法再进行任何子路径收缩时，称为完全收缩态。对于连接树上任意两个原子，通过执行任意顺序的收缩步骤直至不可操作，总能实现其路径的完全收缩——该过程必然终止，因为每次收缩都会缩短路径长度。

We now claim that the graph ${T}^{\prime }$ obtained from such a contraction step of a path ${P}_{A}$ on a join-tree $T$ ,remains a join-tree. It is still a tree since it remains connected and with the same number of edges as before. It is left to show the running intersection property. We start with some observations regarding $T$ . Since the running intersection property holds in $T$ ,for all $1 \leq  i \leq  s,{A}_{1} \cap  {A}_{s} \subseteq  {A}_{i}$ . Since ${A}_{j} \cap  {A}_{j + 1} \subseteq  {A}_{1} \cap  {A}_{s}$ ,we also have that ${A}_{j} \cap  {A}_{j + 1} \subseteq  {A}_{i}$ . Now consider two nodes $B$ and $C$ . We need to show that every node on the simple path between them in ${T}^{\prime }$ contains $B \cap  C$ . If it is the same path as in $T$ ,then we are done. Otherwise,a path between $B$ and $C$ in ${T}^{\prime }$ can be obtained by using the simple path between them in $T$ and replacing the edge $\left( {{A}_{j},{A}_{j + 1}}\right)$ with $\left( {{A}_{j + 1},\ldots ,{A}_{s},{A}_{1},\ldots ,{A}_{j}}\right)$ . The simple path between $B$ and $C$ is contained in this path. This means that atoms on the simple path between $B$ and $C$ in ${T}^{\prime }$ are either: (1) on the simple path between $B$ and $C$ in $T$ ,and therefore contain $B \cap  C$ ; (2) on the path ${P}_{A}$ and therefore contain ${A}_{j} \cap  {A}_{j + 1}$ . Since ${A}_{j}$ and ${A}_{j + 1}$ are on the path between $B$ and $C$ in $T$ ,we have that $B \cap  C \subseteq  {A}_{j} \cap  {A}_{j + 1}$ ,so in this case too,the atoms contain $B \cap  C$ . This proves that the contracted graph is indeed a join-tree. By induction,if we fully contract a path on a join-tree, we still have a join-tree.

现论证：对连接树(join-tree)$T$上的路径${P}_{A}$实施收缩步骤所得图${T}^{\prime }$仍为连接树。其仍保持树结构（边数不变且连通性保留），只需证明运行相交性(running intersection property)成立。基于$T$的若干观察：由于原树满足运行相交性，对所有$1 \leq  i \leq  s,{A}_{1} \cap  {A}_{s} \subseteq  {A}_{i}$成立。因${A}_{j} \cap  {A}_{j + 1} \subseteq  {A}_{1} \cap  {A}_{s}$，故${A}_{j} \cap  {A}_{j + 1} \subseteq  {A}_{i}$亦成立。考察节点$B$与$C$，需证其在${T}^{\prime }$中简单路径上的所有节点都包含$B \cap  C$。若该路径与$T$中相同，结论自明；否则可通过将$T$中对应路径的边$\left( {{A}_{j},{A}_{j + 1}}\right)$替换为$\left( {{A}_{j + 1},\ldots ,{A}_{s},{A}_{1},\ldots ,{A}_{j}}\right)$来构造。此构造路径包含原简单路径，故${T}^{\prime }$中原子节点要么(1)位于$T$原路径上（已含$B \cap  C$），要么(2)位于被收缩路径${P}_{A}$上（含${A}_{j} \cap  {A}_{j + 1}$）。由于${A}_{j}$和${A}_{j + 1}$位于$T$中原路径，故$B \cap  C \subseteq  {A}_{j} \cap  {A}_{j + 1}$，此时原子节点仍包含$B \cap  C$。由此得证收缩图仍为连接树，归纳可得：对连接树路径完全收缩后仍保持连接树性质。

Now let $T$ be a join-tree of $Q$ . We consider some path in $T$ between an atom containing $\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{1}}\right\}$ and an atom containing $\left\{  {{z}_{k},{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}$ . Take the unique subpath of it containing only one atom with $\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{1}}\right\}$ and one atom with $\left\{  {{z}_{k},{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}$ ,and fully contract it. We denote this fully contracted subpath as ${T}_{P} = \left( {{A}_{1},\ldots ,{A}_{s}}\right)$ . Due to Proposition 29, $\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( {T}_{P}\right)$ .

设$T$为$Q$的连接树(join-tree)。我们考察$T$中一条介于包含$\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{1}}\right\}$的原子(atom)与包含$\left\{  {{z}_{k},{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}$的原子之间的路径。取其唯一子路径，该子路径仅含一个具有$\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{1}}\right\}$的原子和一个具有$\left\{  {{z}_{k},{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}$的原子，并将其完全收缩。将此完全收缩后的子路径记为${T}_{P} = \left( {{A}_{1},\ldots ,{A}_{s}}\right)$。根据命题29，$\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{var}\left( {T}_{P}\right)$。

First,we claim that every variable $u$ that appears in two or more atoms of ${T}_{P}$ is part of a chord-less path from ${z}_{0}$ to ${z}_{k + 1}$ . We first show a chordless path from $u$ to ${z}_{k + 1}$ . Denote the last atom on ${T}_{P}$ containing $u$ by ${A}_{i}$ . If ${A}_{i}$ contains ${z}_{k + 1}$ ,we have found the chordless path $\left( {u,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$ ,and we are done. Otherwise, ${A}_{i}$ is not the last atom on ${T}_{P}$ . It is also not the first atom on ${T}_{P}$ ,as another atom contains $u$ . Consider the subpath ${A}_{i - 1},{A}_{i},{A}_{i + 1}$ . Since it is fully contracted, ${A}_{i} \cap  {A}_{i + 1} \nsubseteq  {A}_{i - 1} \cap  {A}_{i + 1}$ . This means that there is a variable ${u}_{+1}$ in ${A}_{i}$ and in ${A}_{i + 1}$ that does not appear in ${A}_{i - 1}$ . Now consider the last atom containing ${u}_{+1}$ ,and continue with the same process iteratively until reaching ${z}_{k + 1}$ . This results in a chordless path $u,{u}_{+1},\ldots ,{u}_{+m} = {z}_{k + 1}$ with $m \geq  1$ . Do the same symmetrically to find a chordless path ${z}_{0} = {u}_{-n},\ldots ,{u}_{-1},u$ with $n \geq  1$ . We now claim that the concatenation ${P}_{u} = \left( {{u}_{-n},\ldots ,{u}_{-1},u,{u}_{+1},\ldots ,{u}_{+m}}\right)$ is chordless. We prove that ${u}_{-t}$ and ${u}_{+\ell }$ are not neighbors for all $1 \leq  t \leq  n$ and $1 \leq  \ell  \leq  m$ by induction on $\ell  + t$ . Out of the atoms containing $u$ ,the variable ${u}_{+1}$ only appears in the last atom by construction: if ${u}_{+1} = {z}_{k + 1}$ ,this is true since ${z}_{k + 1}$ appears only in one atom,and otherwise it is true because this is how we chose ${u}_{+1}$ . Similarly ${u}_{-t}$ only appears in the first. Since there are at least two atoms of the path containing $u$ ,we have that ${u}_{-1}$ and ${u}_{+1}$ are not neighbors,and this proves the induction base. Next,assume by way of contradiction that ${u}_{-t}$ and ${u}_{+\ell }$ are neighbors. By using the induction assumption,we have that ${u}_{-t},\ldots ,{u}_{-1},u,{u}_{+1},\ldots ,{u}_{+\ell },{u}_{-t}$ is a chordless cycle of length four or more,contradicting the fact that $Q$ is acyclic and therefore chordal. This proves that $u$ is part of the chordless path ${P}_{u}$ from ${z}_{0}$ to ${z}_{k + 1}$ .

首先，我们主张每个出现在${T}_{P}$两个或更多原子中的变量$u$，都构成从${z}_{0}$到${z}_{k + 1}$的无弦路径的一部分。我们首先展示从$u$到${z}_{k + 1}$的无弦路径。将${T}_{P}$中包含$u$的最后一个原子记为${A}_{i}$。若${A}_{i}$包含${z}_{k + 1}$，则已找到无弦路径$\left( {u,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$，证明完成。否则，${A}_{i}$并非${T}_{P}$的最后一个原子，也非首个原子（因另有原子包含$u$）。考虑子路径${A}_{i - 1},{A}_{i},{A}_{i + 1}$，因其完全收缩，故${A}_{i} \cap  {A}_{i + 1} \nsubseteq  {A}_{i - 1} \cap  {A}_{i + 1}$。这意味着存在变量${u}_{+1}$同时出现在${A}_{i}$和${A}_{i + 1}$中，但未出现在${A}_{i - 1}$。现考察包含${u}_{+1}$的最后一个原子，并迭代此过程直至抵达${z}_{k + 1}$，最终形成具有$m \geq  1$的无弦路径$u,{u}_{+1},\ldots ,{u}_{+m} = {z}_{k + 1}$。对称性地找出具有$n \geq  1$的无弦路径${z}_{0} = {u}_{-n},\ldots ,{u}_{-1},u$。我们主张连接${P}_{u} = \left( {{u}_{-n},\ldots ,{u}_{-1},u,{u}_{+1},\ldots ,{u}_{+m}}\right)$仍为无弦路径。通过对$\ell  + t$的归纳法证明：对所有$1 \leq  t \leq  n$和$1 \leq  \ell  \leq  m$，${u}_{-t}$与${u}_{+\ell }$均不相邻。在包含$u$的原子中，变量${u}_{+1}$根据构造仅出现在末个原子——若${u}_{+1} = {z}_{k + 1}$成立，因${z}_{k + 1}$仅存于单一原子；否则源于${u}_{+1}$的选择方式。同理${u}_{-t}$仅出现于首个原子。由于至少存在两个包含$u$的路径原子，故${u}_{-1}$与${u}_{+1}$不相邻，此即归纳基础。接着假设${u}_{-t}$与${u}_{+\ell }$相邻，根据归纳假设将导致${u}_{-t},\ldots ,{u}_{-1},u,{u}_{+1},\ldots ,{u}_{+\ell },{u}_{-t}$形成长度≥4的无弦环，与$Q$的无环性（因而具弦性）矛盾。由此证得$u$属于从${z}_{0}$到${z}_{k + 1}$的无弦路径${P}_{u}$。

Assume by contradiction that a variable $u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ appears in two distinct atoms of ${T}_{P}$ . There is a chordless path from ${z}_{0}$ to ${z}_{k + 1}$ that contains $u$ . Denote this path ${P}_{u}$ . Take a subpath of ${P}_{u}$ starting with the last variable on ${P}_{u}$ before $u$ that is in free $\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ ,and ending with the first variable on ${P}_{u}$ after $u$ that is in free $\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ . Such variables exist on this path because ${z}_{0},{z}_{k + 1} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ . This subpath is a free-path in ${Q}_{2}$ ,and since ${Q}_{2}$ is free-path guarded, $u \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ . Next consider two neighboring atoms on ${T}_{P}$ that contain $u$ . According to Proposition 30,there exists some ${z}_{i}$ that appears in both atoms. Note that $i > 0$ and $i < k + 1$ since the path only contains one atom with ${z}_{0}$ and one atom with ${z}_{k + 1}$ . Since ${Q}_{1}$ is bypass-guarded and $u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ ,it is not possible that there is both an atom with $\left\{  {{z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i},u}\right\}$ and an atom with $\left\{  {{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},u}\right\}$ in $Q$ . Without loss of generality,assume there is no atom with $\left\{  {{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},u}\right\}$ . Since the query is acyclic,this means that $u$ and ${z}_{i + 1}$ are not neighbors (if the three variables ${z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},u$ are pairwise neighbors in an acyclic graph, then necessarily they all appear in the same hyperedge). Then, there is a chordless path $\left( {u,{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$ . Since $u \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ ,it is a free-path. This contradicts the fact that ${Q}_{1}$ is free-path guarded since $u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ .

假设存在矛盾，即变量$u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$出现在${T}_{P}$的两个不同原子中。存在一条从${z}_{0}$到${z}_{k + 1}$的无弦路径，该路径包含$u$。记此路径为${P}_{u}$。取${P}_{u}$的一个子路径，起始于$u$之前路径上最后一个处于自由$\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$中的变量，终止于$u$之后路径上第一个处于自由$\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$中的变量。由于${z}_{0},{z}_{k + 1} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$，路径上必然存在这样的变量。该子路径在${Q}_{2}$中为自由路径，又因${Q}_{2}$是自由路径保护的，故$u \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$。接着考虑${T}_{P}$中包含$u$的两个相邻原子。根据命题30，存在某个${z}_{i}$同时出现在这两个原子中。注意到$i > 0$且$i < k + 1$，因为该路径仅包含一个含${z}_{0}$的原子和一个含${z}_{k + 1}$的原子。由于${Q}_{1}$是旁路保护的且$u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$，$Q$中不可能同时存在含$\left\{  {{z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i},u}\right\}$的原子和含$\left\{  {{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},u}\right\}$的原子。不失一般性，假设不存在含$\left\{  {{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},u}\right\}$的原子。由于查询是无环的，这意味着$u$与${z}_{i + 1}$不相邻（若三个变量${z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},u$在无环图中两两相邻，则它们必属于同一超边）。于是存在无弦路径$\left( {u,{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$。因$u \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$，此路径为自由路径。这与${Q}_{1}$因$u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$而具有自由路径保护性质相矛盾。

It is left to show that there is an atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ in ${T}_{P}$ such that $u,{z}_{i} \in  \overrightarrow{v}$ for some $0 < i < k + 1$ . Since $u$ appears in two distinct atoms of ${T}_{P}$ ,and since ${T}_{P}$ is a join-tree, $u$ also appears in two adjacent atoms of ${T}_{P}$ . According to Proposition 30,there exists $0 \leq  i \leq  k + 1$ such that ${z}_{i}$ is in those atoms. This cannot be 0 or $k + 1$ because they appear only in one atom in ${T}_{P}$ .

尚需证明在${T}_{P}$中存在原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$使得对于某个$0 < i < k + 1$满足$u,{z}_{i} \in  \overrightarrow{v}$。由于$u$出现在${T}_{P}$的两个不同原子中，且${T}_{P}$为连接树，$u$亦会出现在${T}_{P}$的两个相邻原子中。根据命题30，存在$0 \leq  i \leq  k + 1$使得${z}_{i}$位于这些原子中。该变量不可能是0或$k + 1$，因为它们仅在${T}_{P}$的一个原子中出现。

We are now ready to show that the properties free-path guardedness and bypass guardedness imply free-connexity.

我们现在可以证明：自由路径保护性与旁路保护性这两个性质可推出自由连通性。

Lemma 32. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ be a UCQ of body-isomorphic acyclic CQs. If ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are both free-path guarded and bypass guarded,then $Q$ is free-connex.

引理32. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$为体同构无环连接查询的UCQ。若${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$同时满足自由路径保护与旁路保护，则$Q$是自由连通的。

Proof. We describe how to iteratively build a union extension for each CQ. In every step, we take one free-path among the queries in $Q$ and add a virtual atom in order to eliminate this free-path. More specifically,let $P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$ be a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ . Take ${T}_{P}$ according to Lemma 31, and denote by ${V}_{P}$ the variables of $P$ and all variables that appear in more than one atom of ${T}_{P}$ . We add the atom $R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$ to both ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ and obtain ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ and ${Q}_{2}^{ + }$ ,respectively. We will show that repeatedly applying such steps eventually leads to free-connex union extensions for ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ .

证明。我们阐述如何为每个CQ迭代构建联合扩展。在每一步中，我们从$Q$的查询中选取一条自由路径，并通过添加虚拟原子来消除该路径。具体而言，设$P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$为${Q}_{1}$中的一条自由路径。根据引理31选取${T}_{P}$，并用${V}_{P}$表示$P$的变量及${T}_{P}$中出现于多个原子的所有变量。我们将原子$R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$分别添加到${Q}_{1}$和${Q}_{2}$中，从而得到${Q}_{1}^{ + }$和${Q}_{2}^{ + }$。我们将证明，重复应用此类步骤最终将为${Q}_{1}$和${Q}_{2}$生成自由连接联合扩展。

First,we claim that ${Q}_{2}$ supplies ${V}_{P}$ . It is guaranteed that ${V}_{P} \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ since $P$ is guarded and due to Lemma 31. We now show that ${Q}_{2}$ is acyclic ${V}_{P}$ -connex. We know that ${T}_{P}$ is a subtree of $T$ that contains ${V}_{P}$ ,but it may contain additional variables,each appearing in only atom,so we need to modify the tree. For every vertex ${A}_{i}$ in ${T}_{P}$ ,add another vertex with ${A}_{i}^{\prime } = \operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{i}\right)  \cap  {V}_{P}$ and an edge $\left( {{A}_{i},{A}_{i}^{\prime }}\right)$ . Then,for every edge $\left( {{A}_{i},{A}_{j}}\right)$ in ${T}_{P}$ ,replace it with the edge $\left( {{A}_{i}^{\prime },{A}_{j}^{\prime }}\right)$ . The new graph is a tree since it is connected and the number of added vertices is equal to the number of added edges (hence, after the modification, the number of edges remains equal to the number of vertices minus one). We next show that the running intersection property is maintained. For every edge $\left( {{A}_{i},{A}_{j}}\right)$ removed, $\operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{i}\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{j}\right)  \subseteq  {V}_{P}$ ,and so by definition of the new vertices, $\operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{i}\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{j}\right)  =$ $\operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{i}^{\prime }\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{j}^{\prime }\right)$ . Given two nodes $B$ and $C$ of the join-tree,the path between them in the modified join-tree is similar to the path in the original join-tree,except it may contain the node $\left( {{A}_{i}^{\prime },{A}_{j}^{\prime }}\right)$ if it contained the node $\left( {{A}_{i},{A}_{j}}\right)$ before. Since the running intersection property holds in $T$ ,every node on the path between $B$ and $C$ contains $\operatorname{var}\left( B\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( C\right)$ . Since $\operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{i}\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{j}\right)  = \operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{i}^{\prime }\right)  \cap$ $\operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{j}^{\prime }\right)$ ,the running intersection property also holds in the modified tree. As the variables of the subtree that consists of the new vertices are exactly ${V}_{P}$ ,this concludes the proof that ${Q}_{2}$ supplies ${V}_{P}$ .

首先，我们断言${Q}_{2}$提供${V}_{P}$。根据引理31及$P$受保护的性质，可确保${V}_{P} \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$成立。现证明${Q}_{2}$是${V}_{P}$无环连通的。已知${T}_{P}$是包含${V}_{P}$的$T$子树，但其可能包含仅出现在单个原子中的额外变量，故需调整该树结构。对于${T}_{P}$中的每个顶点${A}_{i}$，添加带${A}_{i}^{\prime } = \operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{i}\right)  \cap  {V}_{P}$的新顶点及边$\left( {{A}_{i},{A}_{i}^{\prime }}\right)$；对于每条边$\left( {{A}_{i},{A}_{j}}\right)$，将其替换为边$\left( {{A}_{i}^{\prime },{A}_{j}^{\prime }}\right)$。由于修改后的图保持连通且新增顶点数与新增边数相等（边数始终等于顶点数减一），故仍为树结构。接下来证明运行相交性得以保持：对于被移除的每条边$\left( {{A}_{i},{A}_{j}}\right)$，$\operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{i}\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{j}\right)  \subseteq  {V}_{P}$，根据新顶点定义可知$\operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{i}\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{j}\right)  =$$\operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{i}^{\prime }\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{j}^{\prime }\right)$。在连接树中给定节点$B$与$C$，修改后路径与原路径相似，仅当原路径含$\left( {{A}_{i},{A}_{j}}\right)$节点时可能替换为$\left( {{A}_{i}^{\prime },{A}_{j}^{\prime }}\right)$节点。由于$T$满足运行相交性，$B$与$C$间路径所有节点均包含$\operatorname{var}\left( B\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( C\right)$。鉴于$\operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{i}\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{j}\right)  = \operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{i}^{\prime }\right)  \cap$$\operatorname{var}\left( {A}_{j}^{\prime }\right)$，修改后的树仍保持该性质。由于新顶点构成的子树变量集恰好为${V}_{P}$，由此证得${Q}_{2}$确实提供${V}_{P}$。

After the extension step we described,there are no free-paths that start in ${z}_{0}$ and end in ${z}_{k + 1}$ since these variables are now neighbors. If both of the CQs are now free-connex, then we are done. Otherwise, we use the extension recursively. We can apply the extension again as we show next that the UCQ ${Q}_{1}^{ + } \cup  {Q}_{2}^{ + }$ conforms to the conditions of this lemma. Note that,after a free-path from ${z}_{0}$ to ${z}_{k + 1}$ is treated,and even after future extension,there will be no free-path from ${z}_{0}$ to ${z}_{k + 1}$ since they are now neighbors. Since there is a finite number of variable pairs, after a finite number of such steps, all pairs that have a free-path between them are resolved. At this point, we have an acyclic extension with no free-paths, so it is free-connex. It is left to prove that the conditions of this lemma are maintained after the extension steps as long as at least one of the extended CQs is not free-connex. We show that in the following three claims.

经过前述的扩展步骤后，由于变量${z}_{0}$与${z}_{k + 1}$已成为相邻节点，二者之间不再存在自由路径。若两个CQ此时均为自由连接形态，则过程终止；否则递归执行扩展。如后续所示，因UCQ${Q}_{1}^{ + } \cup  {Q}_{2}^{ + }$满足本引理条件，可再次实施扩展。需注意，当处理完${z}_{0}$至${z}_{k + 1}$的自由路径后，即便后续继续扩展，二者因相邻关系也不会再产生自由路径。鉴于变量对数量有限，经过有限次扩展后，所有存在自由路径的变量对都将被消除。此时获得的非循环扩展结构中不含自由路径，故满足自由连接性。尚需证明：只要至少一个扩展后的CQ未达自由连接状态，本引理条件在扩展过程中始终成立。以下三个主张将予以论证。

CLAIM 1. ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ and ${Q}_{2}^{ + }$ are body-isomorphic acyclic.

主张1. ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$与${Q}_{2}^{ + }$为体同构的非循环结构。

Proof of CLAIM 1. First recall that we assume that the CQs are given in the notation defined in the beginning of Section 4.2, so the two CQs have exactly the same body. Since we add the same atom to both CQs,the extensions also have the same body. We show a join-tree ${T}^{ + }$ for the extension. Take the join-tree $T$ according to Lemma 31,and add the vertex $R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$ . Remove all edges in ${T}_{P}$ and add an edge between $R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$ and every atom in ${T}_{P}$ . This construction results in a connected graph with no cycles, and so it is a tree. We claim that the running intersection property is preserved. Let $B$ and $C$ be two nodes in ${T}^{ + }$ . We first handle the case that none of $B$ and $C$ are $R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$ . We need to show that every node on the path between them in ${T}^{ + }$ contains $B \cap  C$ . Since this property holds in $T$ ,every node on the path between them in ${T}^{ + }$ that also appears on the path between them in $T$ preserves this property. By construction,the only new node that may appear on this path is $R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$ . In this case,the two nodes before and after $R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$ on this path contain $B \cap  C$ . By definition, ${V}_{P}$ contains all variables that appear in more that one atom in ${T}_{P}$ ,so $R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$ contains every variable that appears in more than one of its neighbors,and it also contains $B \cap  C$ . It is left to handle the second case. Assume without loss of generality that $C = R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$ . We need to handle the path between $R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$ and $B$ . Let $v \in  {V}_{P} \cap  B$ . Since $v \in  {V}_{P}$ ,there exists some node ${A}_{v}$ in ${T}_{P}$ that contains it. Consider the simple path in $T$ between ${A}_{v}$ and $B$ ,and let ${A}_{v}^{\prime }$ be the last node on this path which is in ${T}_{P}$ . Due to the running intersection property,every node on this path contains ${A}_{v} \cap  B$ ,and so it also contains $v$ . The edge from ${V}_{P}$ to ${A}_{v}^{\prime }$ and the simple path from ${A}_{v}^{\prime }$ to $B$ is therefore a simple path in ${T}^{ + }$ from ${V}_{P}$ to $B$ that contains $v$ .

声明1的证明。首先回顾我们假设CQs采用第4.2节开头定义的表示法，因此两个CQ具有完全相同的本体。由于我们向两个CQ添加了相同的原子，扩展部分也保持相同本体。我们为扩展构建一个连接树${T}^{ + }$：根据引理31取得连接树$T$，添加顶点$R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$，移除${T}_{P}$中所有边，并在$R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$与${T}_{P}$内每个原子间添加边。该构造生成的无环连通图即为树结构。我们声明运行交集性质得以保留：设$B$和$C$为${T}^{ + }$中两个节点。先处理两者均非$R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$的情况，需证明它们路径上所有节点都包含$B \cap  C$。由于该性质在$T$中成立，路径上同时存在于$T$的节点保持该性质。根据构造，路径上唯一可能新增的节点是$R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$，此时该节点前后相邻节点均包含$B \cap  C$。根据定义，${V}_{P}$包含${T}_{P}$中出现于多个原子的所有变量，故$R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$包含其所有邻接原子共有的变量及$B \cap  C$。第二种情况需处理：不妨设$C = R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$，需处理$R\left( {V}_{P}\right)$与$B$间路径。令$v \in  {V}_{P} \cap  B$，因$v \in  {V}_{P}$，存在${T}_{P}$中某节点${A}_{v}$包含之。考虑$T$中${A}_{v}$到$B$的简单路径，设${A}_{v}^{\prime }$为该路径上最后一个属于${T}_{P}$的节点。根据运行交集性质，该路径所有节点均含${A}_{v} \cap  B$，故亦含$v$。因此从${V}_{P}$到${A}_{v}^{\prime }$的边及${A}_{v}^{\prime }$到$B$的简单路径构成${T}^{ + }$中一条包含$v$的简单路径。

CLAIM 2. ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ and ${Q}_{2}^{ + }$ are free-path guarded.

声明2。${Q}_{1}^{ + }$与${Q}_{2}^{ + }$是自由路径防护的。

Proof of CLAIM 2. Since the original CQs are free-path guarded, every free-path in the extension that is also a free-path in the original query is guarded. According to our construction, the only atom that was added in the extension contains exactly ${V}_{P}$ . Thus,a new free-path $\left( {{v}_{0},\ldots ,{v}_{m + 1}}\right)$ contains ${v}_{j},{v}_{j + 1} \in  {V}_{P} \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}^{ + }\right)$ . In particular,since the variables in the center of a free-path must be existential, ${Q}_{2}^{ + }$ does not contain new free-paths. It is left to handle free-paths that appear in ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ but not in ${Q}_{1}$ .

声明2的证明。由于原始CQ具有自由路径防护性，扩展中同时存在于原始查询的自由路径均受防护。根据构造，扩展部分新增原子仅精确包含${V}_{P}$，因此新自由路径$\left( {{v}_{0},\ldots ,{v}_{m + 1}}\right)$包含${v}_{j},{v}_{j + 1} \in  {V}_{P} \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}^{ + }\right)$。特别地，由于自由路径中心变量必须是存在量化的，${Q}_{2}^{ + }$不产生新自由路径。剩余需处理出现在${Q}_{1}^{ + }$但不在${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径。

Let ${P}^{\prime } = \left( {{v}_{0},\ldots ,{v}_{m + 1}}\right)$ be a free-path in ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ but not in ${Q}_{1}$ ,and let ${v}_{j},{v}_{j + 1} \in  {V}_{P}$ . We need to show that ${v}_{i} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}^{ + }\right)$ for all $0 \leq  i \leq  m + 1$ . First note that ${v}_{j},{v}_{j + 1} \in  {V}_{P} \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}^{ + }\right)$ . We next prove the same for ${v}_{0}$ and ${v}_{m + 1}$ .

设${P}^{\prime } = \left( {{v}_{0},\ldots ,{v}_{m + 1}}\right)$为${Q}_{1}^{ + }$中的自由路径而非${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径，且令${v}_{j},{v}_{j + 1} \in  {V}_{P}$。需证明对所有$0 \leq  i \leq  m + 1$均有${v}_{i} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}^{ + }\right)$成立。首先注意${v}_{j},{v}_{j + 1} \in  {V}_{P} \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}^{ + }\right)$。接着对${v}_{0}$与${v}_{m + 1}$进行相同论证。

We first claim that there is a path ${P}_{\text{mid }}$ between ${v}_{j}$ and ${v}_{j + 1}$ that goes only through existential variables in ${Q}_{1}$ . We prove that every variable in ${V}_{P}$ is either of the form ${z}_{i}$ such that $0 < i < k + 1$ (recall that these are the variables in the center of the eliminated free-path) or it has a neighbor of that form. Let $v \in  {V}_{P}$ . By definition of ${V}_{P}$ ,either there exists $0 \leq  i \leq  k + 1$ such that $v = {z}_{i}$ or $v$ appear in two atoms or more in ${T}_{P}$ . In the first case,if $0 < i < k + 1$ ,then it is of the required form. Otherwise,if $i = 0$ ,it has the neighbor ${z}_{1}$ ,and if $i = k + 1$ ,it has the neighbor ${z}_{k}$ . In the second case (if $v$ appear in two atoms or more in ${T}_{P}$ ),according to Lemma 31, $v$ has a neighbor of the required form. Since ${v}_{j},{v}_{j + 1} \in  {V}_{P}$ ,this proves that each of ${v}_{j}$ and ${v}_{j + 1}$ is either of the form ${z}_{i}$ such that $0 < i < k + 1$ or it has a neighbor of that form. Since all ${z}_{i}$ such that $0 < i < k + 1$ are connected through $P$ ,there is a path ${P}_{\text{mid }}$ between ${v}_{j}$ and ${v}_{j + 1}$ that goes only through existential variables in ${Q}_{1}$ .

我们首先断言：存在一条${P}_{\text{mid }}$路径连接${v}_{j}$与${v}_{j + 1}$，且该路径仅经过${Q}_{1}$中的存在性变量。现证明${V}_{P}$中的每个变量要么形如${z}_{i}$且满足$0 < i < k + 1$（此类变量即被消除自由路径的中心变量），要么具有该形式的邻接变量。令$v \in  {V}_{P}$。根据${V}_{P}$的定义，要么存在$0 \leq  i \leq  k + 1$使得$v = {z}_{i}$或$v$在${T}_{P}$中出现于两个及以上原子中。第一种情形下，若$0 < i < k + 1$成立，则该变量已满足要求形式；否则当$i = 0$时，其邻接变量为${z}_{1}$；当$i = k + 1$时，其邻接变量为${z}_{k}$。第二种情形（若$v$在${T}_{P}$中出现于两个及以上原子中），根据引理31，$v$必存在符合要求的邻接变量。由于${v}_{j},{v}_{j + 1} \in  {V}_{P}$，由此证得${v}_{j}$与${v}_{j + 1}$均要么形如${z}_{i}$且满足$0 < i < k + 1$，要么具有该形式的邻接变量。又因所有满足$0 < i < k + 1$的${z}_{i}$变量均通过$P$相互连接，故存在一条${P}_{\text{mid }}$路径连接${v}_{j}$与${v}_{j + 1}$，且该路径仅经过${Q}_{1}$中的存在性变量。

Since ${P}^{\prime }$ is chordless,no variable in ${P}^{\prime }$ other than ${v}_{j}$ and ${v}_{j + 1}$ is in ${V}_{P}$ . One consequence of this is that ${P}_{s} = \left( {{v}_{0},\ldots ,{v}_{j}}\right)$ and ${P}_{t} = \left( {{v}_{j + 1},\ldots ,{v}_{m + 1}}\right)$ are also paths in ${Q}_{1}$ . Another consequence is that ${v}_{0}$ and ${v}_{m + 1}$ are not both in ${V}_{P}$ . Since ${P}^{\prime }$ is chordless and $m \geq  1$ ,we have that ${v}_{0}$ and ${v}_{m + 1}$ are not neighbors in ${Q}^{ + }$ . Since they are not both on the new atom, ${v}_{0}$ and ${v}_{m + 1}$ are not neighbors in $Q$ either. By concatenating ${P}_{s},{P}_{\text{mid,}}$ and ${P}_{t}$ ,we obtain a path in $Q$ from ${v}_{0}$ to ${v}_{m + 1}$ that goes only through existential variables in ${Q}_{1}$ . Take a simple chordless path ${P}_{\ell }$ contained in it. Since ${v}_{0}$ and ${v}_{m + 1}$ are not neighbors,the path ${P}_{\ell }$ is of length two at least,and so it is a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ . Since ${Q}_{1}$ is free-path guarded,we conclude that ${v}_{0},{v}_{m + 1} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ .

由于${P}^{\prime }$是无弦的，${P}^{\prime }$中除${v}_{j}$和${v}_{j + 1}$外的任何变量都不在${V}_{P}$中。由此可得，${P}_{s} = \left( {{v}_{0},\ldots ,{v}_{j}}\right)$和${P}_{t} = \left( {{v}_{j + 1},\ldots ,{v}_{m + 1}}\right)$也是${Q}_{1}$中的路径。另一个推论是${v}_{0}$和${v}_{m + 1}$不会同时存在于${V}_{P}$中。鉴于${P}^{\prime }$无弦且$m \geq  1$，可知${v}_{0}$与${v}_{m + 1}$在${Q}^{ + }$中不相邻。由于它们不在新原子结构上同时出现，${v}_{0}$和${v}_{m + 1}$在$Q$中亦不相邻。通过连接${P}_{s},{P}_{\text{mid,}}$和${P}_{t}$，我们得到$Q$中一条从${v}_{0}$到${v}_{m + 1}$的路径，该路径仅经过${Q}_{1}$中的存在性变量。取其包含的简单无弦路径${P}_{\ell }$。由于${v}_{0}$和${v}_{m + 1}$非邻接，路径${P}_{\ell }$长度至少为二，因此是${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径。既然${Q}_{1}$是自由路径防护的，我们可得出结论${v}_{0},{v}_{m + 1} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$。

So far,we have that ${v}_{0},{v}_{j},{v}_{j + 1},{v}_{m + 1} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ . Assume by contradiction that there exists $0 <$ $i < j$ such that ${v}_{i} \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ ,and consider the subpath of ${P}_{s}$ beginning with the last variable before ${v}_{i}$ on ${P}_{s}$ that is in free $\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ and ending with the first after ${v}_{i}$ on ${P}_{s}$ that is in free $\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ . This is a free-path in ${Q}_{2}$ containing ${v}_{i}$ . We know that ${v}_{i} \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ as it is in the center of the free-path ${P}^{\prime }$ ,but this contradicts the fact that ${Q}_{2}$ is free-path guarded. In the same way,we can show that ${v}_{i} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ for all $j + 1 < i < m + 1$ . Overall,we have seen that ${v}_{i} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}^{ + }\right)$ for all $0 \leq  i \leq  m + 1$ as required.

至此我们已证明${v}_{0},{v}_{j},{v}_{j + 1},{v}_{m + 1} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$。假设存在$0 <$$i < j$使得${v}_{i} \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$，考虑${P}_{s}$的子路径：起始于${P}_{s}$上${v}_{i}$之前最后一个属于自由$\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$的变量，终止于${v}_{i}$之后第一个属于自由$\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$的变量。这是${Q}_{2}$中包含${v}_{i}$的自由路径。已知${v}_{i} \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$因其位于自由路径${P}^{\prime }$中心，但这与${Q}_{2}$具有自由路径防护性矛盾。同理可证对所有$j + 1 < i < m + 1$都有${v}_{i} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$。综上，我们已按要求证明对所有$0 \leq  i \leq  m + 1$都满足${v}_{i} \in  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  = \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}^{ + }\right)$。

CLAIM 3. ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ and ${Q}_{2}^{ + }$ are bypass guarded.

声明3. ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$与${Q}_{2}^{ + }$具有旁路防护性。

Proof of Claim 3. First,let ${P}^{\prime } = \left( {{t}_{0},\ldots ,{t}_{m + 1}}\right)$ be a free-path in ${Q}_{2}^{ + }$ ,and assume by contradiction that there exists some $u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}^{ + }\right)$ that appears in two subsequent ${P}^{\prime }$ -atoms. This means that there exists $i$ such that ${Q}_{2}^{ + }$ has an atom containing $\left\{  {{t}_{i - 1},{t}_{i},u}\right\}$ and an atom containing $\left\{  {{t}_{i},{t}_{i + 1},u}\right\}$ . As explained in the previous claim, ${Q}_{2}^{ + }$ has no new free-paths,so ${P}^{\prime }$ is a free-path in ${Q}_{2}$ as well. Since ${Q}_{2}$ is bypass guarded, $u$ does not appear in two subsequent ${P}^{\prime }$ -atoms in ${Q}_{2}$ ,so one of these atoms is new in ${Q}_{2}^{ + }$ . Assume without loss of generality that it is $\left\{  {{t}_{i},{t}_{i + 1},u}\right\}$ . Then, $\left\{  {{t}_{i},{t}_{i + 1},u}\right\}   \subseteq  {V}_{P} \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ . This contradicts the fact that ${t}_{i} \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ since ${P}^{\prime }$ is a free-path.

命题3的证明。首先，设${P}^{\prime } = \left( {{t}_{0},\ldots ,{t}_{m + 1}}\right)$为${Q}_{2}^{ + }$中的自由路径，并反设存在某个$u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}^{ + }\right)$出现在两个相邻的${P}^{\prime }$-原子中。这意味着存在$i$使得${Q}_{2}^{ + }$包含一个含$\left\{  {{t}_{i - 1},{t}_{i},u}\right\}$的原子和一个含$\left\{  {{t}_{i},{t}_{i + 1},u}\right\}$的原子。如前一命题所述，${Q}_{2}^{ + }$不存在新的自由路径，因此${P}^{\prime }$在${Q}_{2}$中同样是自由路径。由于${Q}_{2}$是旁路防护的，$u$不会在${Q}_{2}$的两个相邻${P}^{\prime }$-原子中出现，故其中一个原子在${Q}_{2}^{ + }$中是新增的。不妨设该原子为$\left\{  {{t}_{i},{t}_{i + 1},u}\right\}$。于是，$\left\{  {{t}_{i},{t}_{i + 1},u}\right\}   \subseteq  {V}_{P} \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$。这与${t}_{i} \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$的事实相矛盾，因为${P}^{\prime }$是自由路径。

Now let ${P}^{\prime } = \left( {{t}_{0},\ldots ,{t}_{m + 1}}\right)$ be a free-path in ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ . Assume by contradiction that there exists some $u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}^{ + }\right)$ ,that appears in two subsequent ${P}^{\prime }$ -atoms. This means that ${Q}^{ + }$ has an atom containing $\left\{  {{t}_{i - 1},{t}_{i},u}\right\}$ and an atom containing $\left\{  {{t}_{i},{t}_{i + 1},u}\right\}$ . Since ${P}^{\prime }$ is chordless, ${t}_{i - 1}$ and ${t}_{i + 1}$ are not neighbors, and so $\left( {{t}_{i - 1},u,{t}_{i + 1}}\right)$ is a chordless path as well. According to Claim 2, $\operatorname{var}\left( {P}^{\prime }\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}^{ + }\right)$ ,and so $\left( {{t}_{i - 1},u,{t}_{i + 1}}\right)$ is a free-path in ${Q}_{2}^{ + }$ . According to Claim 2 again, $\left\{  {{t}_{i - 1},u,{t}_{i + 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}^{ + }\right)$ . Since the only free variables on a free-path are at its ends,this means that ${P}^{\prime }$ is of length two,and $i = m = 1$ . Since $u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ and ${V}_{P} \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ ,we have that the atoms containing $\left\{  {{t}_{0},{t}_{1},u}\right\}$ and $\left\{  {{t}_{1},{t}_{2},u}\right\}$ are not the added atom,and so they appear also in ${Q}_{2}$ and in ${Q}_{1}$ . This means that ${P}^{\prime }$ is a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ ,and $u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ appears in two subsequent atoms of ${P}^{\prime }$ in ${Q}_{1}$ . Thus, ${Q}_{1}$ is not bypass-guarded, in contradiction to the conditions of this lemma.

现设${P}^{\prime } = \left( {{t}_{0},\ldots ,{t}_{m + 1}}\right)$为${Q}_{1}^{ + }$中的自由路径。假设存在某个$u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}^{ + }\right)$出现在两个连续的${P}^{\prime }$原子中，这意味着${Q}^{ + }$包含一个含$\left\{  {{t}_{i - 1},{t}_{i},u}\right\}$的原子和一个含$\left\{  {{t}_{i},{t}_{i + 1},u}\right\}$的原子。由于${P}^{\prime }$是无弦的，${t}_{i - 1}$与${t}_{i + 1}$不相邻，因此$\left( {{t}_{i - 1},u,{t}_{i + 1}}\right)$同样是无弦路径。根据主张2，$\operatorname{var}\left( {P}^{\prime }\right)  \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}^{ + }\right)$，故$\left( {{t}_{i - 1},u,{t}_{i + 1}}\right)$是${Q}_{2}^{ + }$中的自由路径。再次应用主张2可得$\left\{  {{t}_{i - 1},u,{t}_{i + 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}^{ + }\right)$。由于自由路径上唯一的自由变量位于其端点，这表明${P}^{\prime }$长度为二，且$i = m = 1$。鉴于$u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$和${V}_{P} \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$，含有$\left\{  {{t}_{0},{t}_{1},u}\right\}$和$\left\{  {{t}_{1},{t}_{2},u}\right\}$的原子并非新增原子，因此它们也存在于${Q}_{2}$和${Q}_{1}$中。这意味着${P}^{\prime }$是${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径，且$u \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$出现在${Q}_{1}$中${P}^{\prime }$的两个连续原子里。由此，${Q}_{1}$不满足旁路防护条件，与本引理的前提相矛盾。

This proves that the lemma can be applied iteratively.

由此证得该引理可迭代应用。

Since Lemma 27, Lemma 28, and Lemma 32 cover all cases of a union of two self-join-free body-isomorphic acyclic CQs, we have a dichotomy that characterizes the fragment of UCQs we discuss.

由于引理27、引理28和引理32覆盖了两个无自连接且体同构的非循环CQ之并集的所有情形，我们获得了描述所讨论UCQ片段的二分性结论。

THEOREM 33. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ be a UCQ of self-join-free body-isomorphic CQs.

定理33. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$为由无自连接且体同构的CQ构成的UCQ。

- If ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are acyclic free-path guarded and bypass guarded,then $Q$ is free-connex and $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayClin}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

- 若${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$是非循环的自由路径防护且旁路防护的，则$Q$为自由连通且$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayClin}}_{\text{lin }}$。

- Otherwise, $Q$ is not free-connex and $\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ ,under the assumptions HYPER-CLIQUE, MAT-MUL and 4-CLIQUE.

- 否则，在假设超团问题（HYPER-CLIQUE）、矩阵乘法问题（MAT-MUL）和4-团问题（4-CLIQUE）下，$Q$非自由连通且$\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

Proof. Since the CQs are body-isomorphic, they are either both acyclic or both cyclic. First assume that the CQs are acyclic. By Lemma 32,if ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are both free-path guarded and bypass guarded,then $Q$ is free-connex,and $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ by Theorem 14. Otherwise,either one of ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ is not free-path guarded,or they both are but one of them is not bypass guarded. In these cases,by Lemma 27 and Lemma 28, $\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ assuming MAT-MUL and 4-CLIQUE. By Theorem 21,if the CQs are cyclic, $\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle$ is not in ${\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ assuming HYPERCLIQUE. In all cases where $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$ ,we know that $Q$ is not free-connex by Theorem 14.

证明。由于这些CQ（Conjunctive Queries，合取查询）是体同构的，它们要么都是无环的，要么都是环状的。首先假设CQ是无环的。根据引理32，若${Q}_{1}$和${Q}_{2}$均满足自由路径守卫与旁路守卫条件，则$Q$是自由连通的，且根据定理14可得$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。否则，${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$中至少有一个不满足自由路径守卫条件，或二者均满足但存在一个不满足旁路守卫条件。在这些情况下，根据引理27和引理28，在假设MAT-MUL（矩阵乘法）与4-CLIQUE（4-团问题）成立时，$\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$成立。根据定理21，若CQ是环状的，在假设HYPERCLIQUE（超团问题）成立时，$\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle$不属于${\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。在所有$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$的情况下，根据定理14可知$Q$不是自由连通的。

By combining Theorem 33 with Theorem 21, we have the following dichotomy for the case of unions containing exactly two intractable CQs.

通过结合定理33与定理21，我们得到以下关于包含恰好两个难解CQ的并集的二分性结论。

THEOREM 34. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ be a union of difficult ${CQ}$ s.

定理34. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$为困难${CQ}$的并集。

- If $Q$ is free-connex,then $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

- 若$Q$是自由连通的，则$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

- If $Q$ is not free-connex,then $\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$ ,assuming MAT-MUL,HYPERCLIQUE and 4- CLIQUE.

- 若$Q$不是自由连通的，则在假设MAT-MUL（矩阵乘法）、HYPERCLIQUE（超团问题）与4-CLIQUE（4-团问题）成立时，$\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$成立。

Proof. If $Q$ is free-connex,Theorem 4 proves that it is tractable. If ${Q}_{1}$ and ${Q}_{2}$ are acyclic and body-isomorphic but $Q$ is not free-connex,then Theorem 33 proves that $Q$ is intractable. Otherwise, $Q$ is a union of difficult CQs that does not contain body-isomorphic acyclic CQs,and Theorem 21 proves that it is intractable.

证明。若$Q$是自由连通的，定理4证明其是易解的。若${Q}_{1}$与${Q}_{2}$是无环且体同构但$Q$不是自由连通的，则定理33证明$Q$是难解的。否则，$Q$是不包含体同构无环CQ的困难CQ并集，定理21证明其是难解的。

## 5 TOWARDS A DICHOTOMY

## 5 迈向二分性理论

In this section, we examine the next steps that are required to fully characterize which UCQs are in ${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$ . We pinpoint some of the difficulties that must be tackled when formulating such a dichotomy, accompanied by examples. In Section 5.1, we discuss unions containing only acyclic CQs, and, in Section 5.2, we discuss those that contain at least one cyclic CQ.

本节我们将探讨完全刻画哪些UCQ（Union of Conjunctive Queries，合取查询并集）属于${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$所需的后续步骤。通过示例说明，我们指出在构建此类二分性理论时必须解决的若干难点。第5.1节讨论仅含无环CQ的并集，第5.2节讨论包含至少一个环状CQ的情况。

### 5.1 Unions of Acyclic CQs

### 5.1 无环CQ的并集

We inspect two ways of extending the results of Section 4.2. The first such extension is to a union of two CQs that are not body-isomorphic. If there is a difficult CQ ${Q}_{1}$ in the union where for every other CQ ${Q}_{i}$ in the union there is no body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{i}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ ,we can reduce ${Q}_{1}$ to the union as described in Lemma 18. Since ${Q}_{1}$ is difficult,the union is intractable too. In case there is a body-homomorphism to the hard queries, one might think that it is sufficient for intractability to have unguarded difficult structures similarly to the previous section. This is incorrect.

我们考察两种扩展第4.2节结果的路径。第一种扩展针对非体同构的两个CQ并集。若并集中存在一个困难CQ${Q}_{1}$，且对于并集中其他每个CQ${Q}_{i}$都不存在从${Q}_{i}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态映射，则如引理18所述可将${Q}_{1}$归约至该并集。由于${Q}_{1}$是困难的，该并集也是难解的。当存在指向困难查询的体同态时，可能有人认为类似于前文，仅需存在无守卫的困难结构即可导致难解性。这是不正确的。

Example 35 (Unresolved UCQ with CQs that are not body-isomorphic). Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

示例35（未解决的含非体同构CQ的UCQ）。设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$满足：

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right) \text{and}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,{t}_{1}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{t}_{2},y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {w,{t}_{3}}\right) .
$$

The query ${Q}_{1}$ is acyclic non-free-connex,while ${Q}_{2}$ is free-connex. Further, ${Q}_{1}$ is not contained in ${Q}_{2}$ but there is a body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . The variable $z$ is part of the free-path (x,z,y)in ${Q}_{1}$ ,but the variables ${t}_{1}$ and ${t}_{2}$ that map to it via the body-homomorphism are not free in ${Q}_{2}$ . If we extend the notion of guarding to non-body-isomorphic CQs in the natural way,the free-path(x,z,y)is not guarded. Nevertheless,we cannot compute matrix multiplication in $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ time by encoding it to the free-path(x,z,y)like before. Over such a construction,there can be ${n}^{3}$ many results to ${Q}_{2}$ as $w$ and $y$ are not connected in ${Q}_{2}$ so they can have distinct values. This is not an issue when discussing body-isomorphic CQs. We do not know whether this example is in ${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

查询${Q}_{1}$是非自由连接的无环结构，而${Q}_{2}$属于自由连接类型。虽然${Q}_{1}$不包含于${Q}_{2}$，但存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态映射。变量<b2></b0>构成${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径(x,z,y)部分，但通过体同态映射到该路径的变量${t}_{1}$和${t}_{2}$在${Q}_{2}$中并非自由变量。若将守卫概念自然扩展至非体同构的合取查询，则自由路径(x,z,y)不受守卫约束。然而，我们无法通过先前将矩阵乘法编码至自由路径(x,z,y)的方法在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内完成计算。在此构造中，由于$w$和$y$在${Q}_{2}$中未连接，它们可取值不同，因此对${Q}_{2}$可能产生${n}^{3}$种结果。该问题在讨论体同构的合取查询时不会出现。目前尚不确定此例是否属于${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$类问题。

A future characterization of the union of CQs that are not body-isomorphic would need an even more careful approach than the one used in Section 4.2 in order to handle the case that variables mapping to the free-path are not connected via other variables mapping to the free-path.

未来对非体同构合取查询并集的特征描述，需要比4.2节所用方法更为精细的策略，以处理映射到自由路径的变量之间未通过其他自由路径变量相连的情况。

A second way of extending Section 4.2 is to consider more than two body-isomorphic acyclic CQs. This case may be tractable or intractable, and for some queries of this form, we do not have a classification yet. Example 15 shows a tractable union of such form, while the following example shows that free-paths that share edges can be especially problematic within a union.

扩展4.2节的第二种方式是考虑两个以上的体同构无环合取查询。此类问题可能可解或难解，部分此类查询至今尚未明确分类。示例15展示了该形式的可解并集，而后续例子则表明，共享边界的自由路径在并集运算中会引发特殊难题。

Example 36 (Unresolved UCQ with More than Two Body-Isomorphic CQs). Let $k \geq  4$ and consider the UCQ $Q$ containing $k$ body-isomorphic CQs,with an atom ${R}_{i}\left( {{x}_{i},z}\right)$ for every $1 \leq  i \leq  k - 1$ . The heads are all possible combinations of $k - 1$ out of the $k$ variables of the query, $\left\{  {z,{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k - 1}}\right\}$ . In the case of $k = 4$ ,we have the following UCQ:

示例36（含两个以上体同构查询的未决UCQ问题）。设$k \geq  4$，考虑包含$k$个体同构查询的UCQ$Q$，其中每个$1 \leq  i \leq  k - 1$对应一个原子${R}_{i}\left( {{x}_{i},z}\right)$。查询头部分别由该查询$k$个变量中选取$k - 1$个的所有可能组合构成，即$\left\{  {z,{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k - 1}}\right\}$。当$k = 4$时，UCQ形式如下：

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right) ,{Q}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},z}\right) ,{Q}_{3}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{3},z}\right) ,{Q}_{4}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},z}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{1},z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},z}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{3},z}\right) \text{.}
$$

The query ${Q}_{1}$ has free-paths $\left( {{x}_{i},z,{x}_{j}}\right)$ between all possible pairs of $i$ and $j$ . In this case,enumerating the solutions to the UCQ is not in DelayClin assuming 4-CLIQUE: Encode each relation with all edges in the input graph,and concatenate the variable names. That is,for every edge(u,v)in the graph, add $\left( {\left( {u,{x}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {v,z}\right) }\right)$ to ${R}_{1}$ . By concatenating variable names,we can identify which solutions come from which $\mathrm{{CQ}}$ as described in Lemma 16 and ignore the answers to all $\mathrm{{CQs}}$ other than ${Q}_{1}$ . The answers to ${Q}_{1}$ give us 3 vertices that have a common neighbor. We can check in constant time if every pair of the 3 vertices are neighbors,and if so,we found a 4-clique. As there can be $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ solutions to the UCQ,we solve 4-clique in $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ time. This proves that this UCQ when $k = 4$ is intractable assuming 4-CLIQUE.

查询${Q}_{1}$在$i$与$j$所有可能配对间均存在自由路径$\left( {{x}_{i},z,{x}_{j}}\right)$。在4-CLIQUE假设下，枚举该UCQ解集不属于DelayClin类：将输入图中所有边编码至各关系，并串联变量名。具体而言，对图中每条边(u,v)，向${R}_{1}$添加$\left( {\left( {u,{x}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {v,z}\right) }\right)$。通过变量名串联可如引理16所述识别各解对应的$\mathrm{{CQ}}$，并忽略${Q}_{1}$之外所有$\mathrm{{CQs}}$的答案。${Q}_{1}$的解给出三个存在共同邻接顶点的顶点，我们可在常数时间内验证这三个顶点是否两两相邻，若成立则发现4-团。由于UCQ可能产生$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$个解，我们可在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$时间内求解4-团问题，由此证明当$k = 4$时该UCQ在4-CLIQUE假设下属于难解问题。

If we apply the same strategy for a general $k$ ,we get that tractability of this UCQ implies solving $k$ -clique in time $O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$ . However,this does not result in a lower bound for large $k$ values: It is assumed that one can not find a $k$ -clique in time ${n}^{\frac{\omega k}{3} - o\left( 1\right) }$ ,which does not contradict an $O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$ algorithm. In fact,for sufficiently large $k$ ,algorithms for $k$ -clique detection that require time $O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$ do exist [15]. However, this reduction does not seem to fully capture the hardness of this query, as it encodes all relations with the same set of edges. We do not know if queries of the structure given here are hard in general,or if they become easy for larger $k$ values,as any current approach that we know of for constant delay enumeration fails for them.

若将相同策略应用于一般$k$，可推得该UCQ的可追踪性意味着在$O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$时间内求解$k$-团问题。然而这并未对较大$k$值产生下界：学界假定无法在${n}^{\frac{\omega k}{3} - o\left( 1\right) }$时间内找到$k$-团，这与$O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$算法并不矛盾。事实上，对于足够大的$k$，确实存在需要$O\left( {n}^{k - 1}\right)$时间的$k$-团检测算法[15]。但此归约未能完全捕捉该查询的复杂性，因其用相同边集编码所有关系。我们尚不确定此处给出的结构查询是否普遍困难，或是否在较大$k$值时变得简单——目前所知的所有恒定延迟枚举方法对此类查询均告失效。

We now describe some classification we can achieve when we exclude cases like Example 36.

现在描述排除示例36这类情况后所能实现的分类。

5.1.1 Lower Bound for Isolated Free-paths. When generalizing the notion of guarding free-paths to unions of several CQs, a free-path does not have to be guarded by a single CQ. We formalize this in the following definition.

5.1.1 孤立自由路径的下界。将守卫自由路径的概念推广到多个CQ的并集时，自由路径不必由单个CQ守卫。我们通过以下定义形式化这一概念。

Definition 37. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$ be a union of body-isomorphic CQs,and let $P =$ $\left( {{z}_{0},\cdots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$ be a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ . We say that a set $\mathcal{U} \subseteq  {2}^{\operatorname{var}\left( P\right) }$ is a union guard for $P$ if:

定义37. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$为体同构CQ的并集，$P =$$\left( {{z}_{0},\cdots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$是${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径。若满足以下条件，则称集合$\mathcal{U} \subseteq  {2}^{\operatorname{var}\left( P\right) }$为$P$的并集守卫：

- $\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}   \in  \mathcal{U}$ .

- For every $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \subseteq  u \in  \mathcal{U}$ with $a + 1 < c$ ,we have that $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \in  \mathcal{U}$ for some $a < b < c$ .

- 对于每个满足$a + 1 < c$的$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \subseteq  u \in  \mathcal{U}$，存在$a < b < c$使得$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \in  \mathcal{U}$成立

- For every $u \in  \mathcal{U}$ ,we have $u \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right)$ for some $1 \leq  i \leq  n$ .

- 对于每个$u \in  \mathcal{U}$，存在$1 \leq  i \leq  n$使得$u \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{i}\right)$成立

We sometimes refer to the set $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}$ with $a < b < c$ as $\left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)$ .

我们有时将满足$a < b < c$的集合$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}$称为$\left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)$

Note that,when $n = 2$ ,if a UCQ is free-path guarded by Definition 26,every free-path $P$ it contains has the union guard $\mathcal{U} = \{ \operatorname{var}\left( P\right) \}$ . Definition 37 allows several variable sets in the union guard, and different sets are allowed to be free in different CQs. We now show that if a UCQ contains a free-path with no union guard, then the entire union is intractable.

注意当$n = 2$时，若UCQ符合定义26的自由路径守卫条件，则其包含的每条自由路径$P$都具有并集守卫$\mathcal{U} = \{ \operatorname{var}\left( P\right) \}$。定义37允许并集守卫中存在多个变量集，且不同集合可在不同CQ中自由出现。现证明若UCQ包含无并集守卫的自由路径，则整个并集将难以处理。

THEOREM 38. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$ be a UCQ of body-isomorphic acyclic CQs. If there exists a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ that is not union guarded,then $\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$ ,assuming MAT-MUL.

定理38. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$为体同构无环CQ的UCQ。若${Q}_{1}$中存在无并集守卫的自由路径，则在MAT-MUL假设下$\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\operatorname{lin}}$成立。

Proof. Let $P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$ be a free-path of ${Q}_{1}$ that is not union guarded. We know that $\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ . Since $P$ is not union guarded,there exist some $a$ and $c$ such that $a + 1 <$ $c$ ,and $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{r}\right)$ for some ${Q}_{r} \in  Q$ ,but for all ${Q}_{s} \in  Q$ and for all $a < b < c$ ,we have $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{s}\right)$ . Note that ${P}^{\prime } = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{a + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{c}}\right)$ is a free-path of ${Q}_{r}$ . We define ${V}_{a} = \left\{  {z}_{a}\right\}$ , ${V}_{b} = \left\{  {{z}_{a + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{c - 1}}\right\}$ ,and ${V}_{c} = \left\{  {z}_{c}\right\}$ . Since ${P}^{\prime }$ is a free-path,there is no atom in $Q$ that contains both ${z}_{a}$ and ${z}_{c}$ as variables.

证明。设$P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$为${Q}_{1}$的非联合保护自由路径。已知$\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$。由于$P$不受联合保护，存在某些$a$与$c$使得$a + 1 <$$c$，且对某个${Q}_{r} \in  Q$有$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{r}\right)$，但对所有${Q}_{s} \in  Q$及$a < b < c$均有$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{s}\right)$。注意${P}^{\prime } = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{a + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{c}}\right)$是${Q}_{r}$的自由路径。我们定义${V}_{a} = \left\{  {z}_{a}\right\}$、${V}_{b} = \left\{  {{z}_{a + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{c - 1}}\right\}$及${V}_{c} = \left\{  {z}_{c}\right\}$。因${P}^{\prime }$是自由路径，$Q$中不存在同时以${z}_{a}$和${z}_{c}$为变量的原子。

The rest of the proof is similar to Lemma 27. We partition the atoms into nonempty sets ${\mathcal{R}}_{A} =$ $\left\{  {R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)  \mid  {V}_{c} \cap  \overrightarrow{v} = \varnothing }\right\}$ and ${\mathcal{R}}_{B} = \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)  \smallsetminus  {\mathcal{R}}_{A}$ ,and we have that the atoms of ${\mathcal{R}}_{A}$ do not contain the variable of ${V}_{c}$ ,and the atoms of ${\mathcal{R}}_{B}$ do not contain the variable of ${V}_{a}$ . Let $A$ and $B$ be Boolean $n \times  n$ matrices represented as binary relations. We define a database instance $I$ over $Q$ as follows: For every atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ ,if $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  {\mathcal{R}}_{A}$ we set ${R}^{I} = \left\{  {{\tau }_{\left( a,b, \bot  \right) }\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \mid  \left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A}\right\}$ ,and if $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  {\mathcal{R}}_{B}$ we set ${R}^{I} = \left\{  {{\tau }_{\left(  \bot  ,b,c\right) }\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \mid  \left( {b,c}\right)  \in  B}\right\}$ . Here, ${\tau }_{\left( a,b,c\right) }$ is the function defined in Lemma 27.

剩余证明过程与引理27类似。将原子划分为非空集合${\mathcal{R}}_{A} =$$\left\{  {R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)  \mid  {V}_{c} \cap  \overrightarrow{v} = \varnothing }\right\}$和${\mathcal{R}}_{B} = \operatorname{atoms}\left( Q\right)  \smallsetminus  {\mathcal{R}}_{A}$，其中${\mathcal{R}}_{A}$的原子不包含${V}_{c}$的变量，${\mathcal{R}}_{B}$的原子不包含${V}_{a}$的变量。设$A$与$B$是表示为二元关系的布尔$n \times  n$矩阵。我们在$Q$上定义数据库实例$I$如下：对每个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$，若$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  {\mathcal{R}}_{A}$则设${R}^{I} = \left\{  {{\tau }_{\left( a,b, \bot  \right) }\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \mid  \left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A}\right\}$，若$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \in  {\mathcal{R}}_{B}$则设${R}^{I} = \left\{  {{\tau }_{\left(  \bot  ,b,c\right) }\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)  \mid  \left( {b,c}\right)  \in  B}\right\}$。此处${\tau }_{\left( a,b,c\right) }$是引理27中定义的函数。

Consider an answer $\mu  \in  Q\left( I\right)$ . Since the variables ${z}_{i}$ are connected via the path in all CQs,we have that $\mu \left( {z}_{a}\right)  = a,\mu \left( {z}_{a + 1}\right)  = \cdots  = \mu \left( {z}_{c - 1}\right)  = b$ and $\mu \left( {z}_{c}\right)  = c$ for some $\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A$ and $\left( {b,c}\right)  \in  B$ . Since $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{r}\right)$ ,the product ${AB}$ is encoded in ${Q}_{r}\left( I\right)$ . Since for all ${Q}_{s} \in  Q$ and for all $a <$ $b < c$ we have $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{s}\right)$ ,every ${Q}_{s}\left( I\right)$ is of size at most $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ . Thus,there are $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ answers to $Q$ . Assume by contradiction that we can enumerate the solutions of $Q\left( I\right)$ with linear preprocessing and constant delay. To distinguish the answers of ${Q}_{r}$ from those of the other CQs, we can concatenate the variable names to the values, as described in Lemma 16. That way, we can ignore the answers that correspond to the values(a,b)or(b,c),and use the(a,c)pairs as the answers to matrix multiplication. This solves matrix multiplication in $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ time,in contradiction to MAT-MUL.

考虑一个答案$\mu  \in  Q\left( I\right)$。由于变量${z}_{i}$通过所有CQ中的路径相连，因此存在$\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  A$和$\left( {b,c}\right)  \in  B$使得$\mu \left( {z}_{a}\right)  = a,\mu \left( {z}_{a + 1}\right)  = \cdots  = \mu \left( {z}_{c - 1}\right)  = b$且$\mu \left( {z}_{c}\right)  = c$。鉴于$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{r}\right)$，乘积${AB}$被编码在${Q}_{r}\left( I\right)$中。由于对所有${Q}_{s} \in  Q$及所有$a <$$b < c$都有$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \notin  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{s}\right)$，故每个${Q}_{s}\left( I\right)$的规模至多为$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$。因此，$Q$存在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$个答案。假设我们能用线性预处理和常数延迟枚举$Q\left( I\right)$的解，通过反证法可区分${Q}_{r}$与其他CQ的答案——如引理16所述，可将变量名与值拼接，从而忽略对应值(a,b)或(b,c)的答案，并将(a,c)对作为矩阵乘法的解。这能在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内解决矩阵乘法，与MAT-MUL矛盾。

As we do not know of a classification for Example 36, we restrict the UCQs we consider to cases where the free-paths within each CQ do not share variables. Then, we manage to obtain a similar characterization to that of Section 4.2.

鉴于我们尚未掌握示例36的分类标准，故将所研究的UCQ限制为各CQ内自由路径不共享变量的情形。由此，我们成功获得了与第4.2节类似的表征结果。

Definition 39. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$ be a union of body-isomorphic CQs,and let $P$ be a free-path of ${Q}_{1}$ . We say that $P$ is isolated if the following two conditions hold:

定义39. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$为体同构CQ的并集，$P$是${Q}_{1}$的自由路径。当以下两个条件满足时，称$P$为孤立路径：

- $Q$ is var(P)-connex and

- $Q$满足var(P)-连通性

- $\operatorname{var}\left( {P}^{\prime }\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( P\right)  = \varnothing$ for all free-paths ${P}^{\prime } \neq  P$ in ${Q}_{1}$ .

- $\operatorname{var}\left( {P}^{\prime }\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( P\right)  = \varnothing$对${Q}_{1}$中所有自由路径${P}^{\prime } \neq  P$成立

Note that isolated is a stronger property than bypass-guarded. Given a free-path $P$ ,A bypass-guarded query can have a variable in two subsequent $P$ -atoms as long as this variable is free in another CQ. An isolated free-path cannot have such a variable at all. If all free-paths in a union of body-isomorphic acyclic CQs are union guarded and isolated, we can show that the union is tractable. To achieve this result, we first need to show a structural property given in the following lemma:

需注意"孤立性"是比"旁路防护"更强的性质。对于自由路径$P$，旁路防护查询允许两个相邻$P$原子包含同一变量（只要该变量在其它CQ中自由），而孤立自由路径完全不允许此类变量。若某体同构无环CQ并集的所有自由路径均满足并集防护且孤立，则可证明该并集具有易处理性。为达成此结论，需先证明以下引例给出的结构特性：

LEMMA 40. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$ be a ${UCQ},P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$ a free-path of ${Q}_{1}$ and $\mathcal{U}$ a union guard of $P$ . There exists some ${\mathcal{U}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathcal{U}$ such that:

引理40. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$为${Q}_{1}$的自由路径${UCQ},P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$，$\mathcal{U}$为$P$的并集防护。则存在${\mathcal{U}}^{\prime } \subseteq  \mathcal{U}$满足：

- $\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}   \subseteq  v \in  {\mathcal{U}}^{\prime }$ ;

- for all $1 \leq  i \leq  m,\left\{  {{z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i}}\right\}   \subseteq  v \in  {\mathcal{U}}^{\prime }$ ;

- 对所有$1 \leq  i \leq  m,\left\{  {{z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i}}\right\}   \subseteq  v \in  {\mathcal{U}}^{\prime }$成立

- there exists a join-tree ${T}_{P}$ for $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\operatorname{var}\left( P\right) ,{\mathcal{U}}^{\prime }}\right)$ .

- 存在一个连接树 ${T}_{P}$ 用于 $\mathcal{H} = \left( {\operatorname{var}\left( P\right) ,{\mathcal{U}}^{\prime }}\right)$ 。

Proof. We use the inductive definition of a union guard to define ${\mathcal{U}}^{\prime }$ and ${T}_{P}$ . Moreover,for the ease of explanations,we define ${T}_{P}$ with a parent-child relation. By the first two points of Definition ${37},\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}   \in  \mathcal{U}$ and thus we have $\left( {{z}_{0},{z}_{\ell },{z}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  \mathcal{U}$ for some $1 \leq  \ell  \leq  k$ . We set $\left( {{z}_{0},{z}_{\ell },{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$ as a root vertex of ${T}_{P}$ . The second condition of Definition 37 defines the parent-child condition of ${T}_{P}$ with at most two children per vertex: If $v = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)  \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)$ ,then
