证明。我们采用联合守卫的归纳定义来定义 ${\mathcal{U}}^{\prime }$ 和 ${T}_{P}$ 。此外，为便于说明，我们通过父子关系定义 ${T}_{P}$ 。根据定义 ${37},\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}   \in  \mathcal{U}$ 的前两点，因此对于某个 $1 \leq  \ell  \leq  k$ 我们有 $\left( {{z}_{0},{z}_{\ell },{z}_{k + 1}}\right)  \in  \mathcal{U}$ 。我们将 $\left( {{z}_{0},{z}_{\ell },{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$ 设为 ${T}_{P}$ 的根顶点。定义37的第二条件规定了 ${T}_{P}$ 的父子关系，每个顶点最多有两个子节点：若 $v = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)  \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)$ ，则

---

<!-- Footnote -->

- if $b > a + 1$ ,there exists some $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{j},{z}_{b}}\right\}   \in  \mathcal{U}$ with $a < j < b$ . Add one such vertex to the set of children of $v$ .

- 若 $b > a + 1$ ，则存在某个 $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{j},{z}_{b}}\right\}   \in  \mathcal{U}$ 满足 $a < j < b$ 。将这样一个顶点添加到 $v$ 的子节点集合中。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

- if $c > b + 1$ ,there exists some $\left\{  {{z}_{b},{z}_{j},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \in  \mathcal{U}$ with $b < j < c$ . Add one such vertex to the set of children of $v$ .

- 若 $c > b + 1$ ，则存在某个 $\left\{  {{z}_{b},{z}_{j},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \in  \mathcal{U}$ 满足 $b < j < c$ 。将这样一个顶点添加到 $v$ 的子节点集合中。

Note that given $\mathcal{U}$ ,the choice of ${T}_{P}$ might not be unique,and the vertices in ${T}_{P}$ correspond to a subset ${\mathcal{U}}^{\prime }$ of $\mathcal{U}$ .

注意给定 $\mathcal{U}$ 时，${T}_{P}$ 的选择可能不唯一，且 ${T}_{P}$ 中的顶点对应于 $\mathcal{U}$ 的子集 ${\mathcal{U}}^{\prime }$ 。

CLAIM 4. ${T}_{P}$ is a join tree of $\mathcal{H}$ .

声明4. ${T}_{P}$ 是 $\mathcal{H}$ 的连接树。

Proof of the Claim. For every $v \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)$ ,denote by ${T}_{P}\left( v\right)$ the maximal subtree of ${T}_{P}$ rooted in $v$ . Note that for $v = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)$ ,we have $\operatorname{var}\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right)  \subseteq  \left\{  {{z}_{a},\ldots ,{z}_{c}}\right\}$ by construction.

声明证明。对于每个 $v \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)$ ，用 ${T}_{P}\left( v\right)$ 表示以 $v$ 为根的 ${T}_{P}$ 的最大子树。注意对于 $v = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)$ ，根据构造我们有 $\operatorname{var}\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right)  \subseteq  \left\{  {{z}_{a},\ldots ,{z}_{c}}\right\}$ 。

We first prove that for every ${v}_{p},{v}_{c} \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)$ ,if ${v}_{c}$ is a descendent of ${v}_{p}$ and ${z}_{i} \in  {v}_{c} \cap  {v}_{p}$ ,then ${z}_{i}$ is contained in every vertex on the path between ${v}_{p}$ and ${v}_{c}$ . Let ${v}_{c} \in  {T}_{P}\left( {v}_{p}\right)$ ,and by way of contradiction let ${v}_{q}$ be the first vertex the path not containing ${z}_{i}$ . Then,by construction of ${T}_{P}$ ,the parent of ${v}_{q}$ must either be of the form $\left( {{z}_{i},{z}_{a},{z}_{b}}\right)$ ,or $\left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{i}}\right)$ . In both cases, ${v}_{q}$ is of the form $\left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{j},{z}_{b}}\right)$ ,and $\operatorname{var}\left( {{T}_{P}\left( {v}_{q}\right) }\right)  \subseteq  \left\{  {{z}_{a},\ldots ,{z}_{b}}\right\}$ . In the first case $a > i$ ,and in the second case $b < i$ . In either case, ${z}_{i} \notin  {T}_{P}\left( {v}_{q}\right)$ ,which is a contradiction to the fact that ${z}_{i} \in  {v}_{c}$ .

我们首先证明，对于任意${v}_{p},{v}_{c} \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)$，若${v}_{c}$是${v}_{p}$与${z}_{i} \in  {v}_{c} \cap  {v}_{p}$的后继节点，则${z}_{i}$必然包含在${v}_{p}$到${v}_{c}$路径上的每个顶点中。设${v}_{c} \in  {T}_{P}\left( {v}_{p}\right)$，反设${v}_{q}$为该路径上首个不包含${z}_{i}$的顶点。根据${T}_{P}$的构造规则，${v}_{q}$的父节点要么具有$\left( {{z}_{i},{z}_{a},{z}_{b}}\right)$形式，要么为$\left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{i}}\right)$形式。两种情形下，${v}_{q}$都呈现$\left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{j},{z}_{b}}\right)$形态，且$\operatorname{var}\left( {{T}_{P}\left( {v}_{q}\right) }\right)  \subseteq  \left\{  {{z}_{a},\ldots ,{z}_{b}}\right\}$。第一种情形下$a > i$，第二种情形下$b < i$。无论何种情况，都将导致${z}_{i} \notin  {T}_{P}\left( {v}_{q}\right)$，这与${z}_{i} \in  {v}_{c}$的事实相矛盾。

Let ${v}_{1},{v}_{2}$ be two distinct vertices in ${T}_{P}$ and ${z}_{i} \in  {v}_{1} \cap  {v}_{2}$ for some $0 \leq  i \leq  k + 1$ . Let ${v}_{3}$ be a vertex on the unique path from ${v}_{2}$ to ${v}_{1}$ . We make a case distinction by how ${v}_{1}$ and ${v}_{2}$ are connected. In case either ${v}_{1} \in  {T}_{P}\left( {v}_{2}\right)$ or ${v}_{2} \in  {T}_{P}\left( {v}_{1}\right)$ ,we already showed that ${v}_{3}$ contains ${z}_{i}$ . So assume that ${v}_{1} \notin$ ${T}_{P}\left( {v}_{2}\right)$ and ${v}_{2} \notin  {T}_{P}\left( {v}_{1}\right)$ . This means that there is some ${v}_{p} \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)  \smallsetminus  \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$ with ${v}_{1},{v}_{2} \in  {T}_{P}\left( v\right)$ , and distinct children ${u}_{1},{u}_{2}$ of ${v}_{p}$ such that ${v}_{1} \in  {T}_{P}\left( {u}_{1}\right)$ and ${v}_{2} \in  {T}_{P}\left( {u}_{2}\right)$ . For ${v}_{p} = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)$ ,we have that $\operatorname{var}\left( {{T}_{P}\left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {{T}_{P}\left( {u}_{2}\right) }\right)  = \left\{  {z}_{b}\right\}$ by construction,and since ${z}_{i} \in  {v}_{1} \cap  {v}_{2}$ ,it follows that ${z}_{b} = {z}_{i}$ . Thus,either ${v}_{3}$ is on the unique path from ${v}_{p}$ to ${v}_{1}$ or from ${v}_{p}$ to ${v}_{2}$ . Since ${v}_{1}$ and ${v}_{2}$ are descendents of ${v}_{p}$ ,we already showed that ${z}_{b} \in  {v}_{3}$ .

设${v}_{1},{v}_{2}$为${T}_{P}$和${z}_{i} \in  {v}_{1} \cap  {v}_{2}$中两个不同的顶点，其中$0 \leq  i \leq  k + 1$。令${v}_{3}$为从${v}_{2}$到${v}_{1}$唯一路径上的顶点。我们根据${v}_{1}$与${v}_{2}$的连接方式进行情形划分：若${v}_{1} \in  {T}_{P}\left( {v}_{2}\right)$或${v}_{2} \in  {T}_{P}\left( {v}_{1}\right)$成立，则已证${v}_{3}$包含${z}_{i}$；现假设${v}_{1} \notin$${T}_{P}\left( {v}_{2}\right)$且${v}_{2} \notin  {T}_{P}\left( {v}_{1}\right)$，即存在${v}_{p} \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)  \smallsetminus  \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$满足${v}_{1},{v}_{2} \in  {T}_{P}\left( v\right)$，以及${v}_{p}$的不同子节点${u}_{1},{u}_{2}$使得${v}_{1} \in  {T}_{P}\left( {u}_{1}\right)$和${v}_{2} \in  {T}_{P}\left( {u}_{2}\right)$。对于${v}_{p} = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)$，由构造可知$\operatorname{var}\left( {{T}_{P}\left( {u}_{1}\right) }\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {{T}_{P}\left( {u}_{2}\right) }\right)  = \left\{  {z}_{b}\right\}$，又因${z}_{i} \in  {v}_{1} \cap  {v}_{2}$，故${z}_{b} = {z}_{i}$。因此，${v}_{3}$必位于从${v}_{p}$到${v}_{1}$或从${v}_{p}$到${v}_{2}$的唯一路径上。由于${v}_{1}$和${v}_{2}$均为${v}_{p}$的后代节点，已证${z}_{b} \in  {v}_{3}$。

CLAIM 5. For every $1 \leq  i \leq  k + 1$ ,the set $\left\{  {{z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i}}\right\}$ is contained in a vertex of ${T}_{P}$ .

断言5. 对任意$1 \leq  i \leq  k + 1$，集合$\left\{  {{z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i}}\right\}$必包含于${T}_{P}$的某个顶点中。

Proof of the Claim. We describe a path from the root to a vertex containing $\left\{  {{z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i}}\right\}$ . We start with the root,and at each step we consider a vertex $v = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)$ such that $a \leq  i - 1,i \leq  c$ . We have that either $i \leq  b$ or $i - 1 \geq  b$ . Assume that $i \leq  b$ . If $b = a + 1$ ,we have that $a = i - 1$ and $b = i$ ,so we found the vertex we need. Otherwise, $v$ has a child $\left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{t},{z}_{b}}\right)$ with $a \leq  i - 1,i \leq  b$ . We consider this child next. The case that $i - 1 \geq  b$ is symmetrical. Since the tree is finite,the process will end and we will find such vertex.

断言证明. 我们构造一条从根节点到包含$\left\{  {{z}_{i - 1},{z}_{i}}\right\}$顶点的路径：从根出发，每步选择满足$a \leq  i - 1,i \leq  c$的顶点$v = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)$。此时要么$i \leq  b$要么$i - 1 \geq  b$。假设$i \leq  b$成立，若$b = a + 1$则$a = i - 1$与$b = i$同时成立，即得目标顶点；否则$v$存在满足$a \leq  i - 1,i \leq  b$的子节点$\left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{t},{z}_{b}}\right)$，继续考察该子节点。$i - 1 \geq  b$情形对称处理。由于树有限，该过程必终止于目标顶点。

This concludes the proof of the Lemma.

至此完成了引理的证明。

With this structural property at hand, we can show a union-extension for CQs in which every free-path is union guarded and isolated.

基于此结构特性，我们可以展示CQs（合取查询）的联合扩展，其中每个自由路径都是联合守卫且孤立的。

THEOREM 41. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$ be a union of body-isomorphic acyclic ${CQ}$ s. If every free-path in $Q$ is union guarded and isolated,then $\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

定理41. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$为体同构无环${CQ}$的联合。若$Q$中每个自由路径都是联合守卫且孤立的，则$\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

Proof. Let ${Q}_{1} \in  Q$ . We use the fact that every free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ is union guarded and isolated to show that ${Q}_{1}$ is union-free-connex with respect to $Q$ . Since this is true for every $\mathrm{{CQ}}$ in the union, $Q$ is free-connex,and according to Theorem 14, $\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

证明. 设${Q}_{1} \in  Q$。利用${Q}_{1}$中每个自由路径都是联合守卫且孤立的特性，可证${Q}_{1}$相对于$Q$是联合无连通的。由于这对联合中每个$\mathrm{{CQ}}$都成立，故$Q$是自由连通的，根据定理14可得$\operatorname{EnuM}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

We show how to eliminate a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ by adding virtual atoms. This process can be applied repeatedly to treat all free-paths in ${Q}_{1}$ . Let $P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$ be a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ ,and consider a join-tree ${T}_{P}$ given by Lemma 40. We extend every $\mathrm{{CQ}}{Q}_{j}$ in $Q$ to a union extension ${Q}_{j}^{ + }$ as follows: For every $v \in  {T}_{P}$ ,add the atom ${R}_{v}\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ to atoms(Q),where ${R}_{v}$ is a fresh relational symbol. In Claim 7, we show that these variables sets are supplied, so this is indeed a valid union extension. In Claim 8, we show that extension eliminates $P$ without introducing new free-paths. As the proof of Claim 7 uses a bottom-up induction on ${T}_{P}$ ,we will need to use Claim 6 regarding the subtrees of ${T}_{P}$ . We use the same notation as in the proof of Lemma 40: For every vertex $v \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)$ ,we denote by ${T}_{P}\left( v\right)$ the subtree of ${T}_{P}$ rooted in $v$ . Note that $V\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right)$ is a subset of ${\mathcal{U}}^{\prime }$ .

我们演示如何通过添加虚拟原子来消除${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径。此过程可重复应用以处理${Q}_{1}$中所有自由路径。设$P = \left( {{z}_{0},\ldots ,{z}_{k + 1}}\right)$为${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径，考虑引理40给出的连接树${T}_{P}$。我们将$Q$中每个$\mathrm{{CQ}}{Q}_{j}$扩展为联合扩展${Q}_{j}^{ + }$如下：对每个$v \in  {T}_{P}$，向atoms(Q)添加原子${R}_{v}\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$，其中${R}_{v}$是新的关系符号。声明7证明这些变量集已被提供，故此为有效联合扩展。声明8表明该扩展在未引入新自由路径的前提下消除了$P$。由于声明7的证明对${T}_{P}$采用自底向上归纳法，需使用关于${T}_{P}$子树的声明6。沿用引理40证明中的符号：对每个顶点$v \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)$，用${T}_{P}\left( v\right)$表示以$v$为根的${T}_{P}$子树。注意$V\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right)$是${\mathcal{U}}^{\prime }$的子集。

CLAIM 6. Let $v \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)$ . There exists an ext-var $\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right)$ -connex tree ${T}^{\prime }$ for the hypergraph ${\mathcal{H}}^{\prime } =$ $\left( {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) ,E\left( {\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right) }\right)  \cup  V\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right) }\right)$ .

声明6. 设$v \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)$。存在超图${\mathcal{H}}^{\prime } =$$\left( {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) ,E\left( {\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right) }\right)  \cup  V\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right) }\right)$的扩展变量$\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right)$连通树${T}^{\prime }$。

Proof of the Claim. Let $v = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)$ . By construction of ${T}_{P}$ ,we have that $\operatorname{var}\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right)  =$ $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{a + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{c}}\right\}$ . Denote by $R$ the subpath $\left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{a + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{c}}\right)$ of $P$ . It is possible to show that since $P$ is a chordless path,for every subpath of $P$ ,we have that $Q$ is $\operatorname{var}\left( R\right)$ -connex. Let $T$ be an ext- $\operatorname{var}\left( R\right)$ -connex tree for $\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$ ,and let ${T}_{R} \subseteq  T$ be a connected subtree of $T$ with $\operatorname{var}\left( {T}_{R}\right)  = \operatorname{var}\left( R\right)$ . We construct a new tree ${T}^{\prime }$ by first removing the edges among vertices of ${T}_{R}$ ,adding the tree ${T}_{P}\left( v\right)$ and then reconnecting the vertices of ${T}_{R}$ to ${T}_{P}\left( v\right)$ as follows: Let $u \in  V\left( {T}_{R}\right)$ . Since $R$ is a chordless path,we have that $u = \left\{  {z}_{s}\right\}$ for some $a \leq  s \leq  c$ or $u = \left\{  {{z}_{s},{z}_{s + 1}}\right\}$ for some $a \leq  s \leq  c - 1$ . Thus,there exists some vertex $w \in  {T}_{P}\left( v\right)$ with $u \subseteq  w$ . Chose some arbitrary $w \in  {T}_{P}\left( v\right)$ with this property and add an edge(u,w).

命题证明。设$v = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)$。根据${T}_{P}$的构造，我们有$\operatorname{var}\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right)  =$$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{a + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{c}}\right\}$。记$R$为$P$的子路径$\left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{a + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{c}}\right)$。由于$P$是无弦路径，可以证明对于$P$的任意子路径，$Q$都具有$\operatorname{var}\left( R\right)$-连通性。设$T$是$\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$的扩展$\operatorname{var}\left( R\right)$-连通树，${T}_{R} \subseteq  T$是$T$的连通子树且满足$\operatorname{var}\left( {T}_{R}\right)  = \operatorname{var}\left( R\right)$。我们通过以下步骤构建新树${T}^{\prime }$：先移除${T}_{R}$顶点间的边，添加树${T}_{P}\left( v\right)$，然后将${T}_{R}$的顶点重新连接到${T}_{P}\left( v\right)$：设$u \in  V\left( {T}_{R}\right)$。由于$R$是无弦路径，存在某个$a \leq  s \leq  c$使得$u = \left\{  {z}_{s}\right\}$，或某个$a \leq  s \leq  c - 1$使得$u = \left\{  {{z}_{s},{z}_{s + 1}}\right\}$。因此存在顶点$w \in  {T}_{P}\left( v\right)$满足$u \subseteq  w$。任选满足该性质的$w \in  {T}_{P}\left( v\right)$并添加边(u,w)。

Since $T$ is a tree,removing the edges of ${T}_{R}$ results in a forest. For every $u \in  V\left( {T}_{R}\right)$ ,the connected component of this forest that contains $u$ does not contain any other vertices in $V\left( {T}_{R}\right)$ . Thus,in every step of adding an edge,we attach a new tree to ${T}_{P}\left( v\right)$ ,and no such tree is attached to ${T}_{P}\left( v\right)$ more then once. Thus, ${T}^{\prime }$ is again a tree and acyclic.

由于$T$是树，移除${T}_{R}$的边会形成森林。对于每个$u \in  V\left( {T}_{R}\right)$，该森林中包含$u$的连通分量不会包含$V\left( {T}_{R}\right)$中其他顶点。因此在每次添边步骤中，我们都将新树附加到${T}_{P}\left( v\right)$，且每棵树至多被附加一次。故${T}^{\prime }$仍是树且无环。

CLAIM 7. ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ is a union-extension.

命题7。${Q}_{1}^{ + }$是联合扩展。

Proof. We prove via a bottom-up induction on ${T}_{P}$ that every vertex $v \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)$ is supplied by some union-extension of a $\mathrm{{CQ}}$ in $Q$ .

证明。我们对${T}_{P}$自底向上归纳证明：每个顶点$v \in  V\left( {T}_{P}\right)$都由$Q$中某个$\mathrm{{CQ}}$的联合扩展提供。

For the base case,note that the leaves of ${T}_{P}$ are triples of the form $\left( {{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},{z}_{i + 2}}\right)$ for $0 \leq  i \leq  k - 1$ . Since $P$ is a chordless path,for every subpath of $P$ we have that $Q$ is $\operatorname{var}\left( R\right)$ -connex. Therefore, $Q$ is $\left\{  {{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},{z}_{i + 2}}\right\}$ -connex. Since also $\left( {{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},{z}_{i + 2}}\right)$ is contained in some free $\left( {Q}_{j}\right)$ ,all leaves are supplied.

对于基本情况，注意到${T}_{P}$的叶子是形如$\left( {{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},{z}_{i + 2}}\right)$的三元组，其中$0 \leq  i \leq  k - 1$。由于$P$是无弦路径，对于$P$的每个子路径，我们有$Q$是$\operatorname{var}\left( R\right)$-连通的。因此，$Q$也是$\left\{  {{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},{z}_{i + 2}}\right\}$-连通的。由于$\left( {{z}_{i},{z}_{i + 1},{z}_{i + 2}}\right)$还包含在某个自由$\left( {Q}_{j}\right)$中，所有叶子均得到满足。

Consider some vertex $v = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)$ of ${T}_{P}$ that is not a leaf,and assume that for every child ${v}^{\prime }$ of $v$ ,we have that every vertex in $T\left( {v}^{\prime }\right)$ is supplied. We need to show that $v$ is supplied. Consider the ext-var $\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right)$ -connex tree ${T}^{\prime }$ of the hypergraph $\left( {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) ,E\left( {\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right) }\right)  \cup  V\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right) }\right)$ ,that was constructed in the proof for Claim 6 . In this tree,we replace the node $v$ by the two nodes ${v}_{1} = \left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b}}\right\}$ and ${v}_{2} = \left\{  {{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}$ ,and then add the edge $\left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right)$ . For every edge $e = \left( {u,v}\right)$ that was lost when deleting $v$ ,we do the following: If $u$ contains the variable ${z}_{c}$ ,add an edge $\left( {u,{v}_{2}}\right)$ ; otherwise,add the edge $\left( {u,{v}_{1}}\right)$ . Since no vertex in ${T}^{\prime }$ besides $v$ contains both ${z}_{a}$ and ${z}_{c}$ ,this is again a valid join tree. Moreover,both $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b}}\right\}$ and $\left\{  {{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}$ are contained in vertices of ${T}^{\prime } \smallsetminus  \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$ : If $v$ has two children, then the children contain $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b}}\right\}$ and $\left\{  {{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}$ . Otherwise,if $v$ has only one child node,we have that either $b = a + 1$ or $c = a + 1$ . In both such cases, $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b}}\right\}$ or $\left\{  {{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}$ is an edge of the path $P$ and thus contained in a vertex in ${T}^{\prime }$ . Thus,we have that ${T}^{\prime }$ is ext- $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}$ -connex acyclic. Let ${Q}_{j} \in  Q$ such that $\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{j}\right)$ . Since every vertex in ${T}^{\prime }$ is supplied,there exists a union-extension ${Q}_{j}^{ + }$ with ${Q}_{j}^{ + }\left( {\operatorname{free}\left( Q\right) }\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{{v}_{1}}\left( \overrightarrow{{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{{v}_{N}}\left( \overrightarrow{{v}_{N}}\right)$ and $\left\{  {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{N}}\right\}   = V\left( {T}^{\prime }\right)$ . Therefore, ${Q}_{j}^{ + }$ supplies $v$ .

考虑超图${T}_{P}$中某个非叶顶点$v = \left( {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right)$，假设对于$v$的每个子节点${v}^{\prime }$，$T\left( {v}^{\prime }\right)$中的所有顶点均被供应。需证明$v$也被供应。考察在声明6的证明中构建的超图$\left( {\operatorname{var}\left( Q\right) ,E\left( {\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right) }\right)  \cup  V\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right) }\right)$的ext-var$\left( {{T}_{P}\left( v\right) }\right)$-连通树${T}^{\prime }$。在此树中，我们将节点$v$替换为${v}_{1} = \left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b}}\right\}$和${v}_{2} = \left\{  {{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}$两个节点，并添加边$\left( {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right)$。对于删除$v$时丢失的每条边$e = \left( {u,v}\right)$：若$u$包含变量${z}_{c}$，则添加边$\left( {u,{v}_{2}}\right)$；否则添加边$\left( {u,{v}_{1}}\right)$。由于${T}^{\prime }$中除$v$外没有顶点同时包含${z}_{a}$和${z}_{c}$，这仍构成有效的连接树。此外，$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b}}\right\}$和$\left\{  {{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}$均包含于${T}^{\prime } \smallsetminus  \left\{  {{v}_{1},{v}_{2}}\right\}$的顶点中：若$v$有两个子节点，则子节点分别包含$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b}}\right\}$和$\left\{  {{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}$；若$v$仅有一个子节点，则必有$b = a + 1$或$c = a + 1$。这两种情况下，$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b}}\right\}$或$\left\{  {{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}$都是路径$P$的边，因而被${T}^{\prime }$中某顶点包含。由此可得${T}^{\prime }$具有ext-$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}$-连通无环性。设${Q}_{j} \in  Q$满足$\left\{  {{z}_{a},{z}_{b},{z}_{c}}\right\}   \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{j}\right)$。因${T}^{\prime }$中所有顶点均被供应，存在满足${Q}_{j}^{ + }\left( {\operatorname{free}\left( Q\right) }\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{{v}_{1}}\left( \overrightarrow{{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{{v}_{N}}\left( \overrightarrow{{v}_{N}}\right)$和$\left\{  {{v}_{1},\ldots ,{v}_{N}}\right\}   = V\left( {T}^{\prime }\right)$的并集扩展${Q}_{j}^{ + }$，故${Q}_{j}^{ + }$可供应$v$。

CLAIM 8. The set of free-paths in ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ equals the set of free-paths in ${Q}_{1}$ minus $P$ .

权利要求8. ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$中的自由路径集合等于${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径集合减去$P$。

Proof of the CLAIM. Since $\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}$ is contained in a vertex of $T$ by Lemma 40,it is also contained in the variables of some added atom ${R}_{v}\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ ,thus $P$ is not a free-path of ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ . For the sake of a contradiction,assume that there is some new free-path ${P}^{\prime } = \left( {{z}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{z}_{m + 1}^{\prime }}\right)$ in ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ . The only new edges in $\mathcal{H}\left( {Q}_{i}^{ + }\right)$ are between variables in $\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)$ ,thus $\left| {\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {P}^{\prime }\right) }\right|  \geq  2$ . Let ${z}_{s}$ and ${z}_{t}$ be the first and last elements in ${P}^{\prime }$ that are also in $P$ . Note that ${z}_{s}$ and ${z}_{t}$ are not neighbors in $P$ . Replacing the path between ${z}_{s}$ and ${z}_{t}$ in ${P}^{\prime }$ with the path between ${z}_{s}$ and ${z}_{t}$ in $P$ ,we get the path ${P}^{\prime \prime } = \left( {{z}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{z}_{s},{z}_{s + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{t - 1},{z}_{t},\ldots ,{z}_{m + 1}^{\prime }}\right)$ ,which is a path in $\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$ . This path contains a chord-less sub-path ${P}^{\prime \prime \prime }$ between ${z}_{0}^{\prime }$ and ${z}_{m + 1}^{\prime }$ containing at least one element in $\left\{  {{z}_{s},{z}_{s + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{t - 1},{z}_{t}}\right\}$ . Thus, ${P}^{\prime \prime }$ is a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ with a non-empty intersection with $P$ ,which is a contradiction to the assumption that every free-path is isolated.

权利要求的证明。根据引理40，由于$\left\{  {{z}_{0},{z}_{k + 1}}\right\}$包含在$T$的某个顶点中，它必然也存在于某个新增原子${R}_{v}\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$的变量集中，因此$P$不是${Q}_{1}^{ + }$的自由路径。假设存在矛盾，即${Q}_{1}^{ + }$中存在新的自由路径${P}^{\prime } = \left( {{z}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{z}_{m + 1}^{\prime }}\right)$。$\mathcal{H}\left( {Q}_{i}^{ + }\right)$中唯一的新边位于$\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)$的变量之间，故$\left| {\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {P}^{\prime }\right) }\right|  \geq  2$。设${z}_{s}$和${z}_{t}$为${P}^{\prime }$中同时存在于$P$的首尾元素。注意${z}_{s}$与${z}_{t}$在$P$中非相邻。将${P}^{\prime }$中${z}_{s}$到${z}_{t}$的路径替换为$P$中对应路径，得到路径${P}^{\prime \prime } = \left( {{z}_{0}^{\prime },\ldots ,{z}_{s},{z}_{s + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{t - 1},{z}_{t},\ldots ,{z}_{m + 1}^{\prime }}\right)$，该路径存在于$\mathcal{H}\left( Q\right)$中。此路径包含${z}_{0}^{\prime }$与${z}_{m + 1}^{\prime }$之间无弦的子路径${P}^{\prime \prime \prime }$，且该子路径至少包含$\left\{  {{z}_{s},{z}_{s + 1},\ldots ,{z}_{t - 1},{z}_{t}}\right\}$中的一个元素。因此，${P}^{\prime \prime }$是${Q}_{1}$中与$P$存在非空交集的自由路径，这与"所有自由路径皆孤立"的假设矛盾。

If ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ is free connex,then we are done. Otherwise,by Claim 8,every free-path ${P}^{\prime }$ in ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ is a free-path in ${Q}_{1}$ and thus union guarded and isolated in ${Q}_{1}$ . Since the added atoms only contain variables of $\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)$ and $\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {P}^{\prime }\right)  = \varnothing$ ,we have that ${P}^{\prime }$ is also union guarded and isolated in ${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ . The tree-structure from Claim 6 over the root is a join-tree for the union extension. Since the extension consists of body-isomorphic acyclic queries, we can iteratively apply this process until ${Q}_{1}$ becomes free-connex.

若${Q}_{1}^{ + }$是自由连通的，则证明完成。否则根据权利要求8，${Q}_{1}^{ + }$中的每个自由路径${P}^{\prime }$都是${Q}_{1}$中的自由路径，因此在${Q}_{1}$中既是联合守卫的又是孤立的。由于新增原子仅包含$\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)$和$\operatorname{var}\left( P\right)  \cap  \operatorname{var}\left( {P}^{\prime }\right)  = \varnothing$的变量，${P}^{\prime }$在${Q}_{1}^{ + }$中同样保持联合守卫与孤立性。基于权利要求6构建的根节点树结构成为该联合扩展的连接树。由于该扩展由体同构的非循环查询组成，我们可以迭代应用此过程，直至${Q}_{1}$变为自由连通。

Theorem 38 and Theorem 41 form a dichotomy for unions of body-isomorphic acyclic CQs where all free-paths are isolated: if all free-paths are are guarded,the UCQ is in Delay ${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$ ; otherwise,it is not in DelayClin assuming MAT-MUL. It is still left to handle cases like Example 36, of unions containing body-isomorphic acyclic CQs where some free-path is union guarded but not isolated. After solving this case, and regarding unions of difficult CQs, we should also explore unions containing body-isomorphic acyclic CQs but also other difficult CQs.

定理38与定理41构成了关于体同构无环CQ并集的一个二分法，其中所有自由路径都是孤立的：若所有自由路径均被防护，则该UCQ属于Delay${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$类；反之，在假设MAT-MUL成立时，它不属于DelayClin类。仍需处理如例36所示的情况，即包含体同构无环CQ但存在某些自由路径被并集防护而非孤立的并集。解决此类情况后，针对困难CQ的并集，我们还应探究同时包含体同构无环CQ与其他困难CQ的并集。

### 5.2 Unions Containing Cyclic CQs

### 5.2 含循环CQ的并集

We now discuss UCQs containing at least one cyclic query. We first mention that many of the observations we have regarding acyclic CQs also apply here. In the following examples, ${Q}_{1}$ is cyclic while ${Q}_{2}$ is free-connex. Example 42 shows that unions containing cyclic CQs may be tractable and covered by Theorem 14. Example 43 demonstrates that it is not enough to resolve the cyclic structures in CQs, but that we should also handle free-paths. Finally, Example 44 shows that, much like Example 35 in the acyclic case, even if all difficult structures are unguarded, the original reductions showing the intractability of single CQs may not work.

本节讨论至少包含一个循环查询的UCQ。首先指出，针对无环CQ的许多观察结论在此同样适用。以下示例中，${Q}_{1}$为循环查询而${Q}_{2}$为自由连通查询。例42表明包含循环CQ的并集可能具有易处理性且被定理14覆盖。例43说明仅解决CQ中的循环结构不足，还需处理自由路径。最后，例44展示与无环情形下的例35类似，即使所有困难结构均未受防护，证明单CQ难解性的原始归约方法可能失效。

Example 42 (A Tractable UCQ Containing a Cyclic CQ). Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

例42（含循环CQ的可易处理UCQ）。设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$满足

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,z,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {y,z,w,x}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {t,y,w}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {t,z,w}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {t,y,z}\right) ,
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,z,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z,w,v}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,x,w}\right) .
$$

The CQ ${Q}_{2}$ is free-connex,and $\{ y,x,z,w\}  \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ . Thus, ${Q}_{2}$ supplies ${V}_{1}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ . The body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ maps ${V}_{2} = \{ y,x,z,w\}$ to ${V}_{1} = \{ t,y,z,w\}$ . Adding the virtual atom ${R}^{\prime }\left( {t,y,z,w}\right)$ to ${Q}_{1}$ results in a free-connex union-extension,so ${\operatorname{Enum}}_{1}\left\langle  {{Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}}\right\rangle   \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ according to Theorem 14.

CQ${Q}_{2}$是自由连通的，且$\{ y,x,z,w\}  \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$。因此${Q}_{2}$向${Q}_{1}$提供${V}_{1}$。从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态映射将${V}_{2} = \{ y,x,z,w\}$映至${V}_{1} = \{ t,y,z,w\}$。添加虚拟原子${R}^{\prime }\left( {t,y,z,w}\right)$到${Q}_{1}$后形成自由连通并集扩展，故根据定理14可得${\operatorname{Enum}}_{1}\left\langle  {{Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}}\right\rangle   \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

Example 43 (An Intractable UCQ with Guarded Cycles). Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

例43（含防护循环的难解UCQ）。设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$满足

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,v}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {v,z,x}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,v}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {z,y}\right) \text{and}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,v}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {y,v,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {x,y}\right) .
$$

The union ${Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ is intractable despite the fact that ${Q}_{2}$ guards the cycle variables $\{ v,y,z\}$ . This is due to the unguarded free-path(x,z,y)in ${Q}_{1}$ . Similarly to Example 23,we can encode matrix multiplication to $x,z,y$ and conclude that $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ assuming MAT-MUL.

尽管${Q}_{2}$防护了循环变量$\{ v,y,z\}$，该并集${Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$仍具难解性，原因在于${Q}_{1}$中存在未防护的自由路径(x,z,y)。类比例23，可将矩阵乘法编码至$x,z,y$，从而在假设MAT-MUL条件下推出$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$。

Example 44 (An Unresolved UCQ with Unguarded Difficult Structures). Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

例44（含未防护困难结构的待定UCQ）。设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$满足

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,z,y,v}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z,v}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y,v}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,x,v}\right) \text{and}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,z,y,v}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z,v}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,{t}_{1},v}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {{t}_{2},x,v}\right) \text{.}
$$

We do not know the complexity of this example. As ${Q}_{2}$ does not have a free variable that maps to $y$ via a homomorphism, ${Q}_{1}$ is not union-free-connex,so we cannot conclude using Theorem 14 that ${Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ is tractable. The only difficult structure in ${Q}_{1}$ is the cycle $x,y,z$ ,but encoding the triangle-finding problem to this cycle in ${Q}_{1}$ ,like we did in Example 22,could result in ${n}^{3}$ answers to ${Q}_{2}$ . This means that,if the input graph has triangles,we are not guaranteed to find one in $O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$ time by evaluating the union efficiently.

本例复杂度尚未明确。由于${Q}_{2}$不存在通过同态映射到$y$的自由变量，${Q}_{1}$非并集自由连通，故无法依据定理14判定${Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$可易处理。${Q}_{1}$中唯一困难结构是循环$x,y,z$，但如例22所示，若将三角寻找问题编码至该循环，可能导致${Q}_{2}$产生${n}^{3}$个解。这意味着当输入图存在三角形时，无法保证通过高效评估并集在$O\left( {n}^{2}\right)$时间内找到解。

In addition to these issues, in the cyclic case, even if the original difficult structures are resolved, the extension may introduce new ones. Resolving the following example in general is left for future work.

除上述问题外，在循环情形中，即使原始困难结构被解决，扩展过程仍可能引入新的困难结构。以下示例的通用解法将留待未来研究。

Example 45 (An Unresolved UCQ with Guarded Difficult Structures). We start with a specific UCQ that we later generalize into a family of UCQs. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

示例45（含保护型困难结构的未解决UCQ）。我们从一个特定UCQ出发，随后将其推广为UCQ族。设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$满足

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{4}}\right) \text{,}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{1}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{4},{x}_{3},v}\right) .
$$

There is a body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ ,but ${Q}_{1}$ is not contained in ${Q}_{2}.{Q}_{1}$ is cyclic,as it has the cycle $\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)$ . This is the only difficult structure in ${Q}_{1}$ ,i.e., $\mathcal{H}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ does not contain a hyperclique or a free-path. The query ${Q}_{2}$ is both free-connex and $\left\{  {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$ -connex,and so it supplies $\left\{  {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$ . These variables are mapped to $\left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right\}$ via the body-homomorphism to ${Q}_{1}$ . Nevertheless,extending ${Q}_{1}$ with a virtual atom $R\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)$ does not result in a free-connex CQ. Even though the extension "removes" all difficult structures from ${Q}_{1}$ ,it is intractable as it introduces a new difficult structure,namely the hyperclique $\left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$ .

存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态，但${Q}_{1}$不包含于${Q}_{2}.{Q}_{1}$。由于存在循环$\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)$，该查询具有循环性。这是${Q}_{1}$中唯一的困难结构，即$\mathcal{H}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$既不包含超团也不含自由路径。查询${Q}_{2}$既是自由连接的又是$\left\{  {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$连接的，因此它提供了$\left\{  {{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$。这些变量通过体同态映射到${Q}_{1}$中的$\left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right\}$。然而，用虚拟原子$R\left( {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3}}\right)$扩展${Q}_{1}$并不会产生自由连接CQ。尽管该扩展"移除"了${Q}_{1}$中所有困难结构，但由于引入了新的困难结构——即超团$\left\{  {{x}_{1},{x}_{2},{x}_{3},{x}_{4}}\right\}$，该查询仍难以处理。

In this case,we have $\operatorname{ENUM}\left\langle  {{Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}}\right\rangle   \notin  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ assuming 4-CLIQUE,with a reduction similar to that of Example 25: Given an input graph, compute all triangles and encode them to the three relations. For every triangle $\{ a,b,c\}$ ,add the tuple $\left( {\left( {a,{x}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {b,{x}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)$ to ${R}_{1},\left( {\left( {a,{x}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {b,{x}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)$ to ${R}_{2}$ and $\left( {\left( {a,{x}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {b,{x}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)$ to ${R}_{3}$ . By concatenating variable names,we can identify which solutions correspond to which CQ as described in Lemma 16, and thus are able to ignore the answers to ${Q}_{2}$ . The answers to ${Q}_{1}$ represent three vertices that appear in a 4-clique: For every answer $\left( {\left( {b,{x}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {d,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)$ to ${Q}_{1}$ ,we know that $\left( {\left( {b,{x}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {d,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}_{1}$ ,and there exists some $a$ with $\left( {\left( {a,{x}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {d,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}_{2}$ and $\left( {\left( {a,{x}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {b,{x}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {d,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}_{3}$ . This means that $\{ a,b,c,d\}$ is a 4-clique. In the opposite direction,for every 4-clique $\{ a,b,c,d\}$ ,we have that $\{ b,c,d\} ,\{ a,c,d\}$ ,and $\{ a,b,d\}$ are triangles. By construction,the tuple $\left( {\left( {b,{x}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {d,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)$ is an answer to ${Q}_{1}$ . As there are $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ triangles and there can be at most $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ solutions to ${Q}_{2}$ ,we solve 4-clique in $O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$ time. This proves that the UCQ is intractable assuming 4-CLIQUE.

在此案例中，我们基于4-CLIQUE假设，采用类似于示例25的归约方法：给定输入图，计算所有三角形并将其编码至三个关系中。对于每个三角形$\{ a,b,c\}$，将元组$\left( {\left( {a,{x}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {b,{x}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)$添加至${R}_{1},\left( {\left( {a,{x}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {b,{x}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)$、${R}_{2}$以及$\left( {\left( {a,{x}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {b,{x}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)$添加至${R}_{3}$。通过连接变量名，可识别哪些解对应于引理16中所述的CQ，从而忽略${Q}_{2}$的答案。对${Q}_{1}$的答案代表出现在4-团中的三个顶点：对于${Q}_{1}$的每个答案$\left( {\left( {b,{x}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {d,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)$，我们知悉$\left( {\left( {b,{x}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {d,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}_{1}$，且存在满足$\left( {\left( {a,{x}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {d,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}_{2}$和$\left( {\left( {a,{x}_{1}}\right) ,\left( {b,{x}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {d,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}_{3}$的$a$。这意味着$\{ a,b,c,d\}$构成一个4-团。反之，对于每个4-团$\{ a,b,c,d\}$，$\{ b,c,d\} ,\{ a,c,d\}$与$\{ a,b,d\}$均为三角形。根据构造，元组$\left( {\left( {b,{x}_{2}}\right) ,\left( {c,{x}_{3}}\right) ,\left( {d,{x}_{4}}\right) }\right)$是${Q}_{1}$的答案。由于存在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$个三角形且${Q}_{2}$的解最多为$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$个，我们可在$O\left( {n}^{3}\right)$时间内求解4-团问题。由此证明，在4-CLIQUE假设下该UCQ具有难解性。

This example can be generalized to higher orders. There, we do not have a similar lower bound. Consider the union of the following:

此示例可推广至更高阶情形。但该情况下不存在类似的低界。考虑以下查询的并集：

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {{x}_{2},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)  \leftarrow  \left\{  {{R}_{i}\left( {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{i - 1},{x}_{i + 1},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)  \mid  1 \leq  i \leq  k - 1}\right\}  
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {{x}_{2},\ldots ,{x}_{k}}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {{x}_{2},\ldots ,{x}_{k - 1},{x}_{1}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {{x}_{k},{x}_{3},\ldots ,{x}_{k - 1},v}\right) .
$$

Again,the query ${Q}_{1}$ is cyclic and ${Q}_{2}$ is free-connex. Even though ${Q}_{2}$ supplies variables that map to $\left\{  {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k - 1}}\right\}$ via a body-homomorphism,adding a virtual atom with these variables does not result in a free-connex extension, as this extension is again cyclic. As in Example 36, we can encode $k$ -clique to this example in general,but this does not imply a lower bound for large $k$ values.

再次说明，查询${Q}_{1}$是循环的，而${Q}_{2}$属于自由连接型。尽管${Q}_{2}$通过体同态映射提供了对应$\left\{  {{x}_{1},\ldots ,{x}_{k - 1}}\right\}$的变量，添加含这些变量的虚拟原子并不会形成自由连接扩展，因为该扩展本身仍是循环的。如示例36所示，我们通常可将$k$-团问题编码至此例，但这并不意味对大$k$值存在低界。

## 6 MODIFIED SETTINGS

## 6 修改后的设置

We devote this section to discussing the implications of this work when the settings are slightly different than the ones assumed so far: in Section 6.1, we discuss the common case that there are cardinality dependencies in the database schema; in Section 6.2, we discuss unions of conjunctive queries with disequalities; and in Section 6.3, we discuss the case where we want to restrict the space requirements of our algorithm. Reaching complete results regarding the tractability of UCQs in these settings is beyond the scope of this work, but we do discuss some insights we can conclude regarding these settings following our work.

本节专门探讨当设定条件与先前假设略有不同时本研究的意义：在6.1节中，我们讨论数据库模式中存在基数依赖的常见情况；在6.2节中，讨论带不等式的联合合取查询；在6.3节中，探讨需要限制算法空间复杂度的情况。虽然在这些设定下完全解决UCQs可处理性问题超出了本文研究范围，但我们仍将基于研究成果分享相关见解。

### 6.1 Cardinality Dependencies

### 6.1 基数依赖

In this work, we inspect the complexity of answering UCQs when we cannot assume anything on the database instance given as input. Nevertheless, usually we do know what the database we have at hand represents, and this implies restrictions on the combinations of values that the relations may hold. One way of formalizing such restrictions is through Cardinality Dependencies (CDs), also known as degree bounds [1], given as part of the schema. Cardinality dependencies are of the form $\left( {R : A \rightarrow  B,c}\right)$ where $A,B \subseteq  \{ 1,\ldots ,\operatorname{arity}\left( R\right) \}$ and $c$ is a positive natural number. This dependency means that,for every database instance of this schema,the number of tuples in $R$ with the same values in the $A$ indices but distinct values in the $B$ indices is at most $c$ . Functional

本研究在无法对输入数据库实例做任何假设的前提下，考察了回答UCQs的复杂度问题。然而实际应用中，我们通常知晓当前数据库的语义背景，这意味着关系中值的组合存在约束。基数依赖（Cardinality Dependencies，简称CDs）或称度数边界[1]正是形式化此类约束的方式之一，其作为模式组成部分给出。基数依赖表现为$\left( {R : A \rightarrow  B,c}\right)$形式，其中$A,B \subseteq  \{ 1,\ldots ,\operatorname{arity}\left( R\right) \}$且$c$为正自然数。该依赖意味着：对于该模式的任何数据库实例，在$A$索引处值相同但在$B$索引处值不同的$R$元组数量至多为$c$。函数

Dependencies (FDs) are a special case of cardinality dependencies where $c = 1$ ,and keys are a special case of FDs where $A \cup  B = \{ 1,\ldots ,\operatorname{arity}\left( R\right) \}$ .

依赖（FDs）是基数依赖的特例（当$c = 1$时），而键又是FDs的特例（当$A \cup  B = \{ 1,\ldots ,\operatorname{arity}\left( R\right) \}$时）。

As we know, the tractable CQs over general instances are the ones with a free-connex structure. In the presence of cardinality dependencies, additional CQs are tractable. The tractable CQs can be identified by treating the dependencies as between variables and "chasing" the dependencies: the variables of the right-hand side of a dependency are added to any atom that contains the variables of its left-hand side; this extension is applied to the head of the CQ in addition to the atoms. Applying any such number of extension steps generates a ${CD}$ -extended ${CQ}$ ,and if this $\mathrm{{CQ}}$ is free-connex, the original CQ is tractable [11]. This resembles the technique used in this article to show the tractability of a UCQ where we extend the CQs of the union with additional atoms to reach a tractable (free-connex) structure. Following is the formal definition of CD-Extended CQs.

众所周知，通用实例上可处理的CQs需具有自由连接结构。引入基数依赖后，更多CQs变得可处理。识别方法是将依赖视为变量间约束并进行"追踪"：将依赖右侧变量加入所有包含其左侧变量的原子中；这种扩展不仅应用于CQ的原子部分，也应用于其头部。经过任意次数的扩展步骤后，若生成的$\mathrm{{CQ}}$具有自由连接结构，则原CQ可处理[11]。这与本文采用的技术类似——通过为UCQ中的CQs添加原子来构建可处理的（自由连接）结构。以下是CD扩展CQs的形式化定义。

Definition 46 (CD-Extended ${CQ}\left\lbrack  {11}\right\rbrack$ ). Let $Q\left( \overrightarrow{p}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( \overrightarrow{{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m}\left( \overrightarrow{{v}_{m}}\right)$ be a $\mathrm{{CQ}}$ over a schema with the dependencies $\Delta$ . There are two possible types of extension steps:

定义46（CD扩展${CQ}\left\lbrack  {11}\right\rbrack$）。设$Q\left( \overrightarrow{p}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( \overrightarrow{{v}_{1}}\right) ,\ldots ,{R}_{m}\left( \overrightarrow{{v}_{m}}\right)$是带有依赖$\Delta$的模式上的$\mathrm{{CQ}}$，存在两种扩展步骤：

- The extension of an atom ${R}_{i}\left( \overrightarrow{{v}_{i}}\right)$ according to an CD ${R}_{j} : A \rightarrow  b$ .

- 根据CD${R}_{j} : A \rightarrow  b$对原子${R}_{i}\left( \overrightarrow{{v}_{i}}\right)$进行扩展

Prerequisites: $\overrightarrow{{v}_{j}}\left\lbrack  A\right\rbrack   \subseteq  \overrightarrow{{v}_{i}}$ and $\overrightarrow{{v}_{j}}\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack   \notin  \overrightarrow{{v}_{i}}$ .

前提条件：$\overrightarrow{{v}_{j}}\left\lbrack  A\right\rbrack   \subseteq  \overrightarrow{{v}_{i}}$且$\overrightarrow{{v}_{j}}\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack   \notin  \overrightarrow{{v}_{i}}$

Effect: The arity of ${R}_{i}$ increases by one, ${R}_{i}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}\right)$ is replaced by ${R}_{i}\left( {{\overrightarrow{v}}_{i},{\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack  }\right)$ . In addition,every ${R}_{k}\left( \overrightarrow{{v}_{k}}\right)$ such that ${R}_{k} = {R}_{i}$ and $k \neq  i$ is replaced with ${R}_{k}\left( {\overrightarrow{{v}_{k}},{t}_{k}}\right)$ ,where ${t}_{k}$ is a fresh variable in every such step.

效果：${R}_{i}$的元数增加一，${R}_{i}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{i}\right)$被替换为${R}_{i}\left( {{\overrightarrow{v}}_{i},{\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack  }\right)$。此外，每个满足${R}_{k} = {R}_{i}$和$k \neq  i$的${R}_{k}\left( \overrightarrow{{v}_{k}}\right)$都会被替换为${R}_{k}\left( {\overrightarrow{{v}_{k}},{t}_{k}}\right)$，其中每个替换步骤中的${t}_{k}$都是新生成的变量。

- The extension of the head $Q\left( \overrightarrow{p}\right)$ according to an CD ${R}_{j} : A \rightarrow  b$ .

- 根据CD ${R}_{j} : A \rightarrow  b$对头部$Q\left( \overrightarrow{p}\right)$进行扩展。

Prerequisites: $\overrightarrow{{v}_{j}}\left\lbrack  A\right\rbrack   \subseteq  \overrightarrow{p}$ and $\overrightarrow{{v}_{j}}\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack   \notin  \overrightarrow{p}$ .

前提条件：$\overrightarrow{{v}_{j}}\left\lbrack  A\right\rbrack   \subseteq  \overrightarrow{p}$和$\overrightarrow{{v}_{j}}\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack   \notin  \overrightarrow{p}$。

Effect: The head is replaced by $Q\left( {\overrightarrow{p},{\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack  }\right)$ .

效果：头部被替换为$Q\left( {\overrightarrow{p},{\overrightarrow{v}}_{j}\left\lbrack  b\right\rbrack  }\right)$。

The CD-extension of $Q$ is the CQ obtained by performing all possible extension steps on $Q$ according to CDs of $\Delta$ until a fixpoint is reached.

$Q$的CD扩展是通过根据$\Delta$的CD对所有可能的扩展步骤执行，直到达到不动点而获得的CQ。

We devote this section to discussing whether there is a need to combine these two techniques and how this can be done. First, we discuss the need to combine the two techniques.

本节专门讨论是否需要结合这两种技术以及如何实现。首先，我们讨论结合这两种技术的必要性。

Example 47. Consider the UCQ $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with:

示例47。考虑包含以下内容的UCQ $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$：

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,w,u}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,z}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {z,w}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( u\right) 
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,w,u}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,w}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {t,u}\right) 
$$

over a schema with the FD ${R}_{3} : 2 \rightarrow  1$ .

在具有FD ${R}_{3} : 2 \rightarrow  1$的模式上。

The CQ ${Q}_{1}$ in itself is not free-connex,as it contains the free-path(x,y,z,w). Even if we take the FD into account, ${Q}_{1}$ is not tractable. The FD-extension of ${Q}_{1}$ is obtained by "chasing" the FD $w \rightarrow  z$ ,and adding $z$ to the query head

CQ ${Q}_{1}$本身不是自由连接的，因为它包含自由路径(x,y,z,w)。即使考虑FD，${Q}_{1}$也是难处理的。${Q}_{1}$的FD扩展是通过"追逐"FD $w \rightarrow  z$并将$z$添加到查询头部获得的。

$$
{Q}_{1}^{ + }\left( {x,w,u,z}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,z}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {z,w}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( u\right) .
$$

${Q}_{1}^{ + }$ is not free-connex as it contains the free-path(x,y,z). This means that,by using the FD techniques alone, $Q$ does not become tractable. If we take the union into account but ignore the FD, ${Q}_{2}$ can supply $\{ x,w,u\}$ ,which map to $\{ x,y,z\}$ in ${Q}_{1}$ via a body-homomorphism,but adding atoms with any subset of these variables cannot make ${Q}_{1}$ tractable,because it cannot prevent the appearance of a free-path between $x$ and $w$ . It can only shorten it from(x,y,z,w)to(x,z,w). This means that by using the union techniques alone, $Q$ does not become tractable. Nonetheless,if we combine the extensions,we both add $z$ to the head and add an atom with $\{ x,y,z\}$ ,we get the following tractable extension:

${Q}_{1}^{ + }$不是自由连接的，因为它包含自由路径(x,y,z)。这意味着仅使用FD技术时，$Q$不会变得易处理。如果考虑并集但忽略FD，${Q}_{2}$可以提供映射到${Q}_{1}$中$\{ x,y,z\}$的$\{ x,w,u\}$（通过体同态），但添加这些变量任何子集的原子不能使${Q}_{1}$易处理，因为它无法阻止$x$和$w$之间出现自由路径。只能将其从(x,y,z,w)缩短为(x,z,w)。这意味着仅使用并集技术时，$Q$不会变得易处理。然而，如果结合扩展，既将$z$添加到头部又添加包含$\{ x,y,z\}$的原子，我们得到以下易处理的扩展：

$$
{Q}_{1}^{+ + }\left( {x,w,u,z}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,z}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {z,w}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( u\right) ,{R}^{\prime }\left( {x,y,z}\right) .
$$

Practically,this means that $Q$ can be solved efficiently by first solving ${Q}_{2}$ ,then using its results as another atom ${R}^{\prime }$ ,then solving ${Q}_{1}^{+ + }$ with the help of ${R}^{\prime }$ ,and translating every answer to ${Q}_{1}^{+ + }$ to an answer to ${Q}_{1}$ by projecting out $z$ . The FD guarantees that the translation phase does not result in duplicates. If we had a cardinality dependency instead of an FD, we would have a constant number of duplicates per answer to ${Q}_{1}$ ,and the duplicates could be ignored efficiently using the Cheater’s Lemma (Lemma 7).

实际上，这意味着可以通过先解决${Q}_{2}$，然后将其结果作为另一个原子${R}^{\prime }$，再借助${R}^{\prime }$解决${Q}_{1}^{+ + }$，并通过投影去除$z$将每个${Q}_{1}^{+ + }$的答案转换为${Q}_{1}$的答案，从而高效解决$Q$。FD保证转换阶段不会产生重复项。如果有基数依赖而非FD，则每个${Q}_{1}$的答案会有固定数量的重复项，这些重复项可利用作弊者引理（引理7）高效忽略。

Example 47 shows that if we have UCQs over a schema with CDs, we can identify additional tractable cases by combining the two techniques. The two types of extensions can easily be combined in any order. To show this, we just need to modify the definitions of a free-connex union to account for CDs, and use the same proof as before.

示例47表明，若在具有基数依赖(CDs)的模式上使用联合查询(UCQs)，通过结合两种技术可识别更多易处理案例。这两种扩展类型可按任意顺序轻松组合。为证明这一点，只需调整自由连接并集的定义以纳入基数依赖，并沿用先前相同的证明方法。

Definition 48. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$ be a UCQ over a schema with cardinality dependencies.

定义48. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}$为基于带基数依赖模式的联合查询。

- A ${CD}$ -extension for $Q$ is of the form $Q = {Q}_{1}^{\prime } \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}^{\prime }$ such that for all $i = 1,\ldots ,n$ either ${Q}_{i}^{\prime } = {Q}_{i}$ or ${Q}_{i}^{\prime }$ is the CD-extension of ${Q}_{i}$ .

- $Q$的${CD}$扩展形式为$Q = {Q}_{1}^{\prime } \cup  \ldots  \cup  {Q}_{n}^{\prime }$，其中对所有$i = 1,\ldots ,n$，${Q}_{i}^{\prime } = {Q}_{i}$或${Q}_{i}^{\prime }$须为${Q}_{i}$的基数依赖扩展。

- A CD-union extension sequence for $Q$ is a sequence ${Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{N}$ where $Q = {Q}^{1}$ and for all $1 < j \leq  N$ ,it holds that ${Q}^{j}$ is either a union extension or a CD-extension of ${Q}^{j - 1}$ .

- $Q$的基数依赖并集扩展序列是指序列${Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{N}$，其中$Q = {Q}^{1}$且对所有$1 < j \leq  N$，${Q}^{j}$须为${Q}^{j - 1}$的并集扩展或基数依赖扩展。

Note that a CD-extension of a CQ may result in an increase in the arity of the head. Therefore, a CD-extension of a UCQ may not be a valid UCQ in terms of the standard definitions, as these require all CQ heads to have the same arity. However, as the algorithms we use in this article for the evaluation of UCQs do not rely on the size of the heads, we can evaluate such a union as usual.

需注意，对查询(CQ)进行基数依赖扩展可能导致头部元数增加。因此按标准定义，扩展后的联合查询可能无效——因其要求所有子查询头部元数相同。但本文采用的联合查询评估算法不依赖头部大小，故可照常评估此类并集。

THEOREM 49. Let $Q$ be a UCQ over a schema with cardinality dependencies. If $Q$ has a CD-union extension sequence ending in a UCQ containing only free-connex ${CQ}$ ,then $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

定理49. 设$Q$为基于带基数依赖模式的联合查询。若$Q$存在终止于仅含自由连接${CQ}$之联合查询的基数依赖并集扩展序列，则$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle  \in  {\operatorname{DelayC}}_{\text{lin }}$成立。

Proof. Consider an extension sequence ${Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{N}$ . First,we inductively build an instance for ${Q}^{N}$ . The instance for ${Q}^{1}$ is given as input. If ${Q}^{i + 1}$ is a union-extension of ${Q}^{i}$ ,use the construction given in this article for CQs without CDs (i.e., follow the provision phase described in Theorem 14) to obtain the instance for ${Q}^{i + 1}$ . Otherwise, ${Q}^{i + 1}$ is a CD-extension of ${Q}^{i}$ ,and so use the construction for CD-extensions [11,Theorem 2 (Claim 1)] instead. Then,evaluate every CQ in ${Q}^{N}$ using the CDY algorithm. For every answer produced during the construction or in the final evaluation, translate it back to an answer to ${Q}^{1}$ by projecting out any extra variables (these were possibly introduced due to CD-extensions). The correctness follows from the correctness of the individual extensions. The desired time complexity is achieved using the Cheater’s Lemma: since $N$ (the length of the extension sequence) is constant with respect to the input database, the number of times every answer is produced is bound by a constant.

证明. 考察扩展序列${Q}^{1},\ldots ,{Q}^{N}$。首先为${Q}^{N}$递归构建实例：${Q}^{1}$实例由输入给出。若${Q}^{i + 1}$是${Q}^{i}$的并集扩展，则采用本文针对无基数依赖查询的构建方法（即遵循定理14的预备阶段）获取${Q}^{i + 1}$实例；否则当${Q}^{i + 1}$为${Q}^{i}$的基数依赖扩展时，改用文献[11]定理2（主张1）的扩展构建方法。随后使用CDY算法评估${Q}^{N}$中各查询。对构建过程或最终评估生成的每个答案，通过投影剔除额外变量（可能由基数依赖扩展引入）将其转换回${Q}^{1}$的答案。正确性由各扩展步骤的正确性保证。借助Cheater引理可实现预期时间复杂度：因扩展序列长度$N$相对于输入数据库为常数，故每个答案的生成次数受常数限制。

Note that, in Example 47, the order of applying the extension does not matter. That is, first adding ${R}^{\prime }$ and then adding $z$ wherever $w$ appears results in the same query as first adding $z$ and then adding ${R}^{\prime }$ . This is not always the case. Consider the same UCQ as before but with the FD ${R}_{3} : 1 \rightarrow  2$ instead of the reverse FD. When we treat the FD as between variables in ${Q}_{1}$ ,we get $z \rightarrow  w$ ,and we can add $w$ wherever $z$ appears in ${Q}_{1}$ as an FD-extension. The individual extensions are still not enough: adding $w$ to ${R}_{2}$ results in the free-path(x,y,w),and adding an atom with $\{ x,y,z\}$ or a subset of these variables does not help as before. The combination of the two extensions is enough if we first use the union and only then use the FD. We get the following free-connex CQ:

请注意，在示例47中，扩展应用的顺序并不影响结果。即先添加${R}^{\prime }$再在$w$出现处添加$z$，与先添加$z$再添加${R}^{\prime }$得到的查询相同。但这种情况并非总是成立。考虑相同的UCQ（联合查询），但将FD（函数依赖）${R}_{3} : 1 \rightarrow  2$替换为反向FD。当我们将FD视为${Q}_{1}$中变量间关系时，得到$z \rightarrow  w$，并可在${Q}_{1}$中$z$出现处添加$w$作为FD扩展。单独扩展仍不足：向${R}_{2}$添加$w$会生成自由路径(x,y,w)，而添加含$\{ x,y,z\}$或其变量子集的原子也如前所述无济于事。若先进行联合操作再应用FD，两种扩展的组合即足够。最终得到如下自由连接CQ（连接查询）：

$$
{Q}_{1}^{\prime }\left( {x,w,u}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,z,w}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {z,w}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( u\right) ,{R}^{\prime }\left( {x,y,z,w}\right) .
$$

If we apply the extensions in the opposite order,we do not have $w$ in ${R}^{\prime }$ ,and we have the free-path (x,z,w),so the extension is not tractable.

若以相反顺序应用扩展，${R}^{\prime }$中将不包含$w$，且会生成自由路径(x,z,w)，因此该扩展不具备易处理性。

Overall, we saw that the two techniques can and should be combined to find more tractable cases. However, one should be aware that it is not enough to first take the CD-extensions of all CQs in the union and then perform union-extensions. The order in which the different extensions are combined matters.

总体而言，我们证实这两种技术能够且应当结合使用以发现更多易处理案例。但需注意，仅对所有CQ先进行CD扩展再执行联合扩展并不足够。不同扩展的组合顺序至关重要。

### 6.2 Disequalities

### 6.2 不等性条件

In this section, we discuss the enumeration complexity of unions of CQs with disequalities. In the case of individual CQs with disequalities, the disequalities have no effect on the enumeration complexity [4]. That is, one can simply ignore the disequalities, and the remaining CQ is free-connex if and only if the query is tractable (under the same assumptions as in Theorem 3). A natural question is whether this happens also with UCQs. We next show that it does not, and some tractable UCQs become intractable with the addition of disequalitites.

本节探讨含不等性条件的CQ联合查询的枚举复杂性。对于单个含不等性条件的CQ，这些条件对枚举复杂度无影响[4]。即只需忽略不等性条件，剩余CQ为自由连接当且仅当该查询可高效处理（在定理3相同假设下）。自然产生的疑问是：UCQ是否同样如此？下文将证明事实并非如此，部分原本可高效处理的UCQ会因加入不等性条件而变得棘手。

Example 50. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

示例50. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$满足：

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,z,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,z}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {z,z}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{5}\left( w\right) 
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,z,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {w,z}\right) ,z \neq  w.
$$

We can show this union is hard by encoding triangle detection to the cycle in ${Q}_{1}$ as usual. Due to the disequality, ${Q}_{2}$ has no answers over this construction,so finding an answer to the UCQ in linear time detects a triangle in linear time. If there was no disequality, ${Q}_{2}$ would supply variables that map to the cycle in ${Q}_{1}$ via a body-homomorphism,and the UCQ would become tractable.

通过将三角形检测编码至${Q}_{1}$中的循环（按常规方法），可证明该联合查询的困难性。由于不等性条件，${Q}_{2}$在此构造下无解，故线性时间内求解UCQ即等价于线性时间检测三角形。若无不等性条件，${Q}_{2}$将通过体同态为${Q}_{1}$中的循环提供变量映射，使得UCQ可高效处理。

This example shows that (unlike with CQs) the positive results presented in this article for UCQs do not hold when we ignore disequalities. Nevertheless, a simple adjustment of the proofs of Section 3 reveals cases where a UCQ with disequalities containing hard CQs is easy.

此例表明（与CQ不同），本文所述UCQ的积极结论在忽略不等性条件时不成立。然而，通过简单调整第3节证明，可发现某些含困难CQ的不等性UCQ仍存在易处理情形。

Definition 51. Let ${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2}$ be CQs with disequalities.

定义51. 设${Q}_{1},{Q}_{2}$为含不等性条件的CQ。

- A body-homomorphism from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ is a mapping $h : \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ such that: -for every atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ of ${Q}_{2},R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {Q}_{1}$ . -for every disequality $v \neq  u$ of ${Q}_{2},h\left( v\right)  \neq  h\left( u\right)$ is in ${Q}_{1}$ .

- 从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态指映射$h : \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)  \rightarrow  \operatorname{var}\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$满足：-对于${Q}_{2},R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {Q}_{1}$的每个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$；-对于${Q}_{2},h\left( v\right)  \neq  h\left( u\right)$的每个不等条件$v \neq  u$，$v \neq  u$须存在于${Q}_{1}$中。

- We say that ${Q}_{2}$ supplies a set $V$ of variables if ${Q}_{2}$ is free-connex and $V \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$ .

- 当${Q}_{2}$为自由连接且满足$V \subseteq  \operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right)$时，称${Q}_{2}$提供变量集$V$。

Note that the definition of a body-homomorphism now takes the disequalities into account. Note also that the definition of supplying variables here is simpler than before as we require the supplying CQ to be free-connex. ${}^{3}$ We set the definition of a union extension as in Section 3 (but using the new definition of supplied variables and body-homomorphisms). Using these definitions, we can get a result similar to that of Theorem 14. We first prove the equivalent of Lemma 10 in our settings.

需注意，体同态（body-homomorphism）的定义现已将不等式因素纳入考量。同时需指出，此处变量供给（supplying variables）的定义较之前更为简化，因为我们要求供给的合取查询（CQ）满足自由连接（free-connex）性质。${}^{3}$ 我们沿袭第3节的方式定义并集扩展（union extension）（但采用新的变量供给定义和体同态定义）。基于这些定义，我们可以得到与定理14相似的结论。首先需证明本框架下与引理10等价的命题。

LEMMA 52. Let ${Q}_{2}$ be a ${CQ}$ with disequalities that supplies the variables ${\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}$ . Given an instance $I$ ,one can compute with linear time preprocessing and constant delay $M = {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ ,and $M$ can be translated in time $O\left( \left| M\right| \right)$ to a relation ${R}^{M}$ such that: for every ${CQ}{Q}_{1}$ with a body-homomorphism $h$ from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ and for every answer ${\mu }_{1} \in  \operatorname{full}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) \left( I\right)$ ,there is the tuple ${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{M}$ .

引理52. 设${Q}_{2}$为带有不等式的${CQ}$，其供给变量${\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}$。给定实例$I$，可通过线性时间预处理和常数延迟计算$M = {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$，且$M$可在$O\left( \left| M\right| \right)$时间内转换为关系${R}^{M}$，使得：对于每个存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态$h$的${CQ}{Q}_{1}$，及每个答案${\mu }_{1} \in  \operatorname{full}\left( {Q}_{1}\right) \left( I\right)$，都存在元组${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{M}$。

Proof. Let ${Q}_{2}$ be a CQ with disequalities that supplies the variables ${\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}$ . Since ${Q}_{2}$ is free-connex, it can be answered with linear preprocessing and constant delay [4]. We define ${R}^{M} = \left\{  {{\mu }_{2}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)  \mid  }\right.$ ${\mu }_{2} \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right) \}$ . Let ${Q}_{1}$ be a CQ such that there is a body-homomorphism $h$ from ${Q}_{2}$ to ${Q}_{1}$ ,and let ${\mu }_{1} \in$ full $\left( {Q}_{1}\right) \left( I\right)$ . Since $h$ is a body-homomorphism,for every atom $R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$ in ${Q}_{2},R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)$ is an atom in ${Q}_{1}$ , and for every disequality $v \neq  u$ of ${Q}_{2},h\left( v\right)  \neq  h\left( u\right)$ is in ${Q}_{1}$ . Since ${\mu }_{1}$ is an answer to full $\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$ ,for such atoms and disequalities ${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{I}$ and ${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( v\right) }\right)  \neq  {\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( u\right) }\right)$ . This means that ${\mu }_{1} \circ  h{ \mid  }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) }$ is an answer to ${Q}_{2}$ ,so there exists ${\mu }_{2} \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ such that ${\mu }_{1} \circ  h{ \mid  }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) } = {\mu }_{2}$ . By construction, ${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right) }\right)  =$ ${\mu }_{2}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)  \in  {R}^{M}$ .

证明. 设${Q}_{2}$为带有不等式且供给变量${\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}$的合取查询（CQ）。由于${Q}_{2}$满足自由连接性质，可通过线性预处理和常数延迟应答[4]。我们定义${R}^{M} = \left\{  {{\mu }_{2}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)  \mid  }\right.$${\mu }_{2} \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right) \}$。设${Q}_{1}$为存在从${Q}_{2}$到${Q}_{1}$的体同态$h$的CQ，令${\mu }_{1} \in$完全$\left( {Q}_{1}\right) \left( I\right)$。由于$h$是体同态，对于${Q}_{2},R\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)$中的每个原子$R\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right)$，其在${Q}_{1}$中对应原子，且${Q}_{2},h\left( v\right)  \neq  h\left( u\right)$的每个不等式$v \neq  u$在${Q}_{1}$中存在。因${\mu }_{1}$是完全$\left( {Q}_{1}\right)$的答案，故对这些原子和不等式${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( \overrightarrow{v}\right) }\right)  \in  {R}^{I}$与${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( v\right) }\right)  \neq  {\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( u\right) }\right)$成立。这意味着${\mu }_{1} \circ  h{ \mid  }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) }$是${Q}_{2}$的答案，故存在${\mu }_{2} \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$使得${\mu }_{1} \circ  h{ \mid  }_{\operatorname{free}\left( {Q}_{2}\right) } = {\mu }_{2}$。根据构造，${\mu }_{1}\left( {h\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right) }\right)  =$${\mu }_{2}\left( {\overrightarrow{v}}_{2}\right)  \in  {R}^{M}$。

With Lemma 52 at hand, we can apply the proof of Theorem 14 as is. We only need to use Lemma 52 instead of Lemma 10. This proves the following result:

根据引理52，我们可以直接套用定理14的证明过程，只需将引理10替换为引理52。由此可证得以下结论：

THEOREM 53. Let $Q$ be a union of ${CQ}$ with disequalities. If $Q$ is free-connex,then $\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle$ is in ${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$ .

定理53. 设$Q$为${CQ}$与不等式的并集。若$Q$是自由连接的，则$\operatorname{Enum}\langle Q\rangle$属于${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }}$。

Example 54. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

示例54. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$满足

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right) ,x \neq  z\text{and}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,w}\right) ,x \neq  y.
$$

This is the same as Example 2 except for the added disequalities. Without the disequalities, this union is easy as ${Q}_{2}$ supplies $\{ x,y,w\}$ ,and adding a virtual atom with a union extension to ${Q}_{1}$ results in a free-connex form. Since ${Q}_{2}$ is free-connex and the disequality of ${Q}_{2}$ maps to that of ${Q}_{1}$ using the body-homomorphism, this union is easy even with the disequalities according to Theorem 53.

除新增的不等式外，此例与示例2完全相同。若无不等式约束，该并集运算很简单——因为${Q}_{2}$提供了$\{ x,y,w\}$，通过向${Q}_{1}$添加带并集扩展的虚拟原子即可得到自由连接形式。由于${Q}_{2}$是自由连接的，且${Q}_{2}$的不等式通过体同态映射到${Q}_{1}$的不等式，根据定理53，即使存在不等式约束该并集运算仍属简单情形。

Theorem 53 can be used also to show the tractability of UCQs that are not naturally of that form. For example,in the case of the last example,the union is easy even without the disequality in ${Q}_{1}$ .

定理53还可用于证明非典型形式的UCQ（联合查询）的可处理性。例如上例中，即使${Q}_{1}$不存在不等式约束，该并集运算仍属简单情形。

Example 55. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

示例55. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$满足

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right) \text{and}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,w}\right) ,x \neq  y.
$$

We can partition the answers to ${Q}_{1}$ to those that assign the same values to $x$ and $z$ and those that do not. $Q$ is equivalent to ${Q}^{\prime } = {Q}_{1}^{ \neq  } \cup  {Q}_{1}^{ = } \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

我们可以将${Q}_{1}$的答案划分为两类：对$x$和$z$赋予相同值的解，以及赋予不同值的解。$Q$等价于满足下列条件的${Q}^{\prime } = {Q}_{1}^{ \neq  } \cup  {Q}_{1}^{ = } \cup  {Q}_{2}$：

$$
{Q}_{1}^{ \neq  }\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,z}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {z,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right) ,x \neq  z\text{and}
$$

$$
{Q}_{1}^{ = }\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,x}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {y,w}\right) \text{and}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,w}\right) ,x \neq  y.
$$

Here, ${Q}_{2}$ and ${Q}_{1}^{ = }$ are free-connex,and ${Q}_{1}^{ \neq  }$ has a free-connex union-extension,as explained in Example 54.

如示例54所述，此处${Q}_{2}$和${Q}_{1}^{ = }$是自由连接的，且${Q}_{1}^{ \neq  }$具有自由连接的并集扩展。

---

<!-- Footnote -->

${}^{3}$ This is a restriction we impose for simplicity,and it is possible that a more refined condition may lead to additional tractable cases. However, this seems to require going into the details of the algorithm for CQs with disequalities, and it is left for future work.

${}^{3}$ 这是为简化分析而设的限制条件，更精确的条件可能推导出更多可处理情形。但这需要深入研究带不等式的CQ算法细节，故留待后续工作解决。

<!-- Footnote -->

---

Unlike in the case of individual CQs, the complexity of answering unions of CQs changes with the addition of disequalities. We proved the tractability of some unions with disequalities that can be easily concluded from the results of this article, but identifying all of the tractable cases is left for future work.

与单条CQ不同，添加不等式会改变CQ并集的求解复杂度。我们证明了部分带不等式的并集具有可处理性（这可直接从本文结论推导），但所有可处理情形的完整判定尚待未来研究。

### 6.3 Restricted Space

### 6.3 受限空间

In this article, we only considered time bounds. The class CDoLin describes the problems that can be solved with the same time bounds, but with the additional restriction that the available space for writing during the enumeration phase is constant. Evaluating free-connex CQs is in CD $\circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$ ,and Kazana offers a comparison between Delay ${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$ and $\mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$ [20,Section 8.1.2]. All lower bounds presented in this article naturally hold for $\mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$ as we know that,by definition of the classes, $\mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}} \subseteq  {\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\mathrm{{lin}}}$ . The tractability of unions containing only free-connex CQs,as explained in Theorem 4, also holds for this class. However, in our techniques for the tractable UCQs that do not contain only tractable CQs, the memory to which we write during the enumeration phase may increase in size by a constant with every new answer. An interesting question is whether we can achieve the same time bounds when restricting the memory according to $\mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$ .

本文仅讨论时间复杂度。CDoLin类描述的是在枚举阶段可用书写空间恒定的前提下，仍能保持相同时间效率的问题。自由连接CQ的求值属于CD$\circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$类，Kazana就Delay${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$与$\mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$进行了对比研究[20,第8.1.2节]。本文所有下界结论自然适用于$\mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$，因为根据类定义可知$\mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}} \subseteq  {\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\mathrm{{lin}}}$。定理4所述仅含自由连接CQ的并集可处理性，对此类同样成立。然而，在处理非全可处理UCQ的技术中，枚举阶段的存储空间可能随每个新答案的产生而恒定增长。值得探讨的问题是：在$\mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$的内存限制下，能否实现相同的时间效率？

The first reason we use polynomial space is to regularize the delay and avoid duplicates. This is achieved in the Cheater's Lemma (Lemma 7) by storing all results we produce. We should mention in that context that regularizing the delay is arguably not of practical importance. In practice, once we have an answer, we will probably want to use it immediately. For this reason, one might be satisfied with simply using a relaxed complexity measure which captures the fact that an algorithm performs "just as good" as linear preprocessing and constant delay when given enough space: linear partial time (see Definition 5). The Cheater's Lemma shows that, whenever we have an algorithm $\mathcal{A}$ that runs in linear partial time,there is a linear preprocessing and constant delay algorithm ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ that uses additional space and computes the same results,where every result in $\mathcal{A}$ is returned no later than the same result in ${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$ . In fact,if we denote by ${\operatorname{Lin}}_{\text{Partial }}$ the class of enumeration problems that can be solved by a linear partial time algorithm, ${\mathrm{{Lin}}}_{\text{Partial }} = {\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\mathrm{{lin}}}$ . Please note that this does not imply ${\operatorname{Lin}}_{\text{Partial }} = \mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$ ,as we currently do not know whether ${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }} = \mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$ .

我们采用多项式空间的首要原因是为了规范化延迟并避免重复输出。这一目标在《作弊者引理》（Lemma 7）中通过存储所有生成结果得以实现。需要说明的是，规范化延迟在实践中可能并不具有实际重要性。实际操作中，当获得答案时我们往往会立即使用。因此，研究者可能满足于采用宽松的复杂度衡量标准——线性部分时间（见Definition 5），该标准表明算法在获得足够空间时能达到"与线性预处理和恒定延迟相当"的性能。《作弊者引理》证明：对于任何线性部分时间算法$\mathcal{A}$，总存在一个使用额外空间的线性预处理恒定延迟算法${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$，其计算结果与$\mathcal{A}$完全相同且每个结果的输出时刻均不迟于${\mathcal{A}}^{\prime }$。若用${\operatorname{Lin}}_{\text{Partial }}$表示可由线性部分时间算法解决的枚举问题类，则${\mathrm{{Lin}}}_{\text{Partial }} = {\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\mathrm{{lin}}}$。需注意这并不意味${\operatorname{Lin}}_{\text{Partial }} = \mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$，因为我们尚未确认${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }} = \mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$是否成立。

The other reason that we use large amounts of space is that we need to store the generated relations that correspond to the virtual atoms in order to evaluate the union extension as specified in Theorem 14. The discussion above implies that it makes sense to focus on eliminating this fundamental reason. As the next example demonstrates, this seems to require a different approach than the one presented in this article.

使用大量空间的另一个原因，是需要存储与虚拟原子相对应的生成关系以便实现定理14中所述的并集扩展。上述讨论表明，集中精力消除这一根本原因具有现实意义。如下例所示，这需要采用不同于本文所述方法的新思路。

Example 56. Let $Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with

示例56. 设$Q = {Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$满足

$$
{Q}_{1}\left( {x,y,z,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,y}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,z}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {z,x}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {x,w}\right) \text{and}
$$

$$
{Q}_{2}\left( {x,y,z,w}\right)  \leftarrow  {R}_{1}\left( {x,{t}_{1}}\right) ,{R}_{2}\left( {y,{t}_{2}}\right) ,{R}_{3}\left( {z,{t}_{3}}\right) ,{R}_{4}\left( {w,{t}_{4}}\right) \text{.}
$$

${Q}_{1}$ is cyclic,so it is not possible to find an answer to it in linear time. However,since ${Q}_{2}$ is free-connex and supplies variables that map to the cycle $\{ x,y,z\}$ in ${Q}_{1}$ via a body-homomorphism,and since ${Q}_{1}$ becomes free connex when adding an atom with these variables,the union is solvable in linear preprocessing time and constant delay by saving the results of ${Q}_{2}$ as a relation as suggested in Section 3. In this case, there is also a way of solving it with no additional space: use CDY to solve ${Q}_{2}$ efficiently; for every answer(a,b,c,d)to ${Q}_{2}$ ,check if $a = d,\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  {R}_{1},\left( {b,c}\right)  \in  {R}_{2}$ and $\left( {c,a}\right)  \in  {R}_{3}$ ; if all these conditions are met,go over all tuples $\left( {a,e}\right)  \in  {R}_{4}$ and output(a,b,c,e). These checks can be done in constant time per answer in the RAM model by constructing lookup tables for each relation during preprocessing. This finds all answers to ${Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$ with constant time per answer. However, if an answer belongs to both CQs, we print it twice. The duplicates can easily be avoided by replacing ${R}_{4}$ in ${Q}_{1}$ with a modified version,lacking the tuples that cause duplicates. During preprocessing,compute ${R}_{4}^{\prime } = {R}_{4} \smallsetminus  \left\{  {\left( {a,e}\right)  \in  {R}_{4}\mid \exists t\text{ s.t. }\left( {e,t}\right)  \in  {R}_{4}}\right.$ . During enumeration,for every qualifying answer(a,b,c,d)to ${Q}_{2}$ ,go over all tuples $\left( {a,e}\right)  \in  {R}_{4}^{\prime }$ and output(a,b,c,e).

${Q}_{1}$是循环的，因此无法在线性时间内找到解答。但由于${Q}_{2}$是自由连接查询并通过体同态提供映射到${Q}_{1}$中循环$\{ x,y,z\}$的变量，且当添加包含这些变量的原子时${Q}_{1}$会变为自由连接，根据第3节建议将${Q}_{2}$结果保存为关系，该并集可在线性预处理时间和常数延迟内求解。此案例还存在无需额外空间的解法：使用CDY高效求解${Q}_{2}$；对${Q}_{2}$的每个答案(a,b,c,d)，检查$a = d,\left( {a,b}\right)  \in  {R}_{1},\left( {b,c}\right)  \in  {R}_{2}$和$\left( {c,a}\right)  \in  {R}_{3}$；若条件均满足，则遍历所有元组$\left( {a,e}\right)  \in  {R}_{4}$并输出(a,b,c,e)。在RAM模型中，通过在预处理阶段为每个关系构建查找表，这些检查可在常数时间内完成。该方法能以常数时间找出${Q}_{1} \cup  {Q}_{2}$的所有答案，但若答案同时属于两个CQ则会重复输出。通过将${Q}_{1}$中的${R}_{4}$替换为剔除重复元组的修改版可避免重复：预处理阶段计算${R}_{4}^{\prime } = {R}_{4} \smallsetminus  \left\{  {\left( {a,e}\right)  \in  {R}_{4}\mid \exists t\text{ s.t. }\left( {e,t}\right)  \in  {R}_{4}}\right.$；枚举阶段对${Q}_{2}$的每个合格答案(a,b,c,d)，遍历$\left( {a,e}\right)  \in  {R}_{4}^{\prime }$并输出(a,b,c,e)。

Example 56 shows that reducing the space requirements is sometimes possible. We do not know, however, whether this is the case for all free-connex UCQs. Consider Example 2. The same approach would mean that,for every answer(a,b,c)to ${Q}_{2}$ ,we go over all tuples $\left( {c,d}\right)  \in  {R}_{3}$ and print(a,c,d). The problem here is that we will print many duplicates: every answer(a,c,d)will be produced as many times as there are different $b$ values with $\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$ .

示例56表明降低空间需求有时可行。但我们尚不确定是否所有自由连接UCQ都适用此方法。以示例2为例，相同方法意味着对${Q}_{2}$的每个答案(a,b,c)，需遍历$\left( {c,d}\right)  \in  {R}_{3}$并打印(a,c,d)。问题在于会产生大量重复：每个答案(a,c,d)会被输出多次，次数等于满足$\left( {a,b,c}\right)  \in  {Q}_{2}\left( I\right)$的不同$b$值的数量。

In conclusion, UCQs that contain intractable CQs can sometimes be made easy by using virtual atoms. In this article, we proposed instantiating a relation that corresponds to each virtual atom during enumeration. This instantiation can sometimes be avoided, but it requires a different approach, and it is left for future work to find when this can be done. The discussion above also gives rise to a possible relaxation that can be considered a first step: Can we evaluate such UCQs efficiently with only constant writing memory available during enumeration, when we allow for linear partial time and a constant number of duplicates per answer? Note that, in Example 56, we do not need this relaxation, so another interesting question is whether this relaxation is useful for our task or can all UCQs that can be answered with this relaxed measure also be answered with linear preprocessing and constant delay without extra space.

综上，包含难解CQ的UCQ有时可通过虚拟原子简化处理。本文提出在枚举阶段实例化对应每个虚拟原子的关系。虽然这种实例化有时可避免，但需采用不同方法，何时能实现这一点留待未来研究。上述讨论还引出一个可考虑的首步松弛方案：若允许线性部分时间和每个答案产生常数级重复，能否在枚举期间仅用常数写入内存高效求解此类UCQ？需注意的是，示例56无需此松弛方案，故另一有趣问题是：该松弛对我们的任务是否有用，或所有适用此松弛方案的UCQ是否都能通过线性预处理和常数延迟无额外空间求解？

## 7 CONCLUSIONS

## 7 结论

In this article,we study the enumeration complexity of UCQs with respect to DelayClin. We formalized how CQs within a union can make each other easier by supplying variables, and we introduced union extensions. Then, we defined free-connex UCQs, and showed that these are tractable. In particular, we demonstrated that UCQs containing only intractable CQs may be tractable.

本文研究了UCQ在DelayClin下的枚举复杂度。我们形式化了并集中CQ如何通过变量供给相互简化，并引入并集扩展概念。随后定义了自由连接UCQ，证明其易处理性，特别展示了仅含难解CQ的UCQ也可能易处理。

We showed that in case of a union of two difficult CQs or two acyclic body-isomorphic CQs, free-connexity fully captures the tractable cases. Nevertheless, achieving a full classification of UCQs remains an open problem. Section 5 discusses the next steps that are required to fully characterize which UCQs are in Delay ${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$ and provides examples with unknown complexity. Resolving Examples 35, 36, 44, and 45 are necessary steps on the way to a future dichotomy.

我们证明，在涉及两个困难CQ（Conjunctive Queries）或两个无环且体同构的CQ的并集情况下，自由连接性完全刻画了可处理案例。然而，对UCQs（Union of Conjunctive Queries）实现完整分类仍是一个开放性问题。第5节讨论了全面表征哪些UCQs属于Delay${\mathrm{C}}_{\text{lin }}$类所需的后续步骤，并列举了复杂度未知的实例。解决示例35、36、44和45是实现未来二分法的必经之路。

In addition to the general settings, we also showed that in three other variations: cardinality dependencies in the schema, UCQs with disequalities and restricted space, we can identify tractable UCQs that contain intractable CQs. Section 6 offers a preliminary idea on how the results of the article can be applied in these variations, and leaves substantial room for future exploration of this topic. For example, Theorem 53 in Section 6.2 shows the tractability of free-connex UCQs with disequalities, but as Example 55 demonstrates, additional such queries are tractable. Can we find a characterization that captures all such tractable UCQs with disequalities?

除常规设定外，我们还证明了在三种变体中——模式中的基数依赖、带不等式的UCQs以及受限空间条件下——能够识别包含难解CQ的可处理UCQs。第6节初步探讨了如何将本文成果应用于这些变体，并为该主题的后续探索留下广阔空间。例如，第6.2节的定理53展示了带不等式的自由连接UCQs的可处理性，但如示例55所示，还存在其他可处理的此类查询。我们能否找到刻画所有带不等式可处理UCQs的特征？

Most of this article ignores space consumption in order to concentrate on time. An interesting question is how much space is required to achieve the time bounds described here. As Example 56 shows, sometimes we can solve UCQs with only constant memory available for writing during the enumeration phase. It is left open whether all free-connex UCQs can be solved under this restriction,and in general whether ${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }} = \mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$ (see the discussion in Section 6.3).

本文大部分内容为聚焦时间效率而暂不考虑空间消耗。一个有趣的问题是：实现所述时间界限需要多少空间？如示例56所示，有时我们能在枚举阶段仅用恒定内存解决UCQs。所有自由连接UCQs是否都能在此限制下求解仍悬而未决，总体而言${\mathrm{{DelayC}}}_{\text{lin }} = \mathrm{{CD}} \circ  \mathrm{{Lin}}$（参见第6.3节讨论）。
